FILE services/dispatch_worker.py
"""Execute a fenced chat task; persist the answer before releasing the lane."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import contextlib
import json
import os
import re
import signal
import sys
import threading
from pathlib import Path

from state import CONDUCTOR, ROOT, atomic_write, lock, read_json
from lanes import ask, slot
import mini_worker
import result_acceptance
import pr_evidence
import draft_publish
import api_worker
from result_acceptance import ResultRejected, blocked_answer
from workspaces import prepare, git, analysis_workspace

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "state"))
import task_queue
from api_tasks import MODELS as WORKER_MODELS, REPORT_KINDS


def task_repository(prompt):
    for project, spec in project_catalog().items():
        if re.search(r"\b" + re.escape(project) + r"\b", prompt, re.I):
            return project, Path(spec["repo"]).expanduser().resolve()
    return "WebBob", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def project_catalog():
    # Ship new project mappings with the pinned release; retain local overrides.
    return {**read_json(Path(__file__).with_name("projects.json"), {}),
            **read_json(CONDUCTOR / "services/projects.json", {})}


def report_repository(row):
    source_id = row.get("source_job_id")
    if source_id:
        from state import jobs
        source = task_queue.tasks(ROOT).get(source_id) or jobs().get(source_id)
        if not source:
            raise RuntimeError("The assigned source job is no longer available")
        project, repo = task_repository(source.get("prompt", "") or source.get("project", ""))
        # Paths come only from server-owned jobs, never from model output.
        worktree = Path(source["worktree"]) if source.get("worktree") else None
        # A source-linked report must inspect that job's actual checkout. A
        # repo fallback could silently review different code and claim success.
        return source.get("project") or project, worktree if worktree and worktree.is_dir() else None
    return task_repository(row["prompt"])


def prepare_task(record, coding):
    if not coding:
        return prepare(record)
    # Independent Git metadata is needed for conductor code tasks: the model's
    # sandbox denies writes to the live conductor, including its .git directory.
    # Shared objects are read-only; new commits/refs live in this private clone.
    folder = Path(record["worktree"])
    with lock("dispatch-workspace"):
        if not folder.exists():
            folder.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            git(ROOT, "clone", "--shared", "--no-checkout", record["repo"], str(folder))
            git(folder, "checkout", "-b", record["branch"], "HEAD")
            git(folder, "remote", "remove", "origin")
        if git(folder, "branch", "--show-current") != record["branch"]:
            raise RuntimeError("Private task branch ownership mismatch")
        hooks = folder / ".git/hooks"
        atomic_write(hooks / "pre-push", "#!/bin/sh\necho 'Web Bob tasks are local only' >&2\nexit 1\n")
        (hooks / "pre-push").chmod(0o700)
    return folder


def redact(text):
    # Do not persist provider output containing any loaded credential.
    for name, value in os.environ.items():
        if (name.endswith(("KEY", "TOKEN", "SECRET", "PASSWORD"))) and len(value) >= 8:
            text = text.replace(value, "[secret removed]")
    return re.sub(r"\b(?:sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}|AIza[A-Za-z0-9_-]{25,}|gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9_]{25,})\b",
                  "[secret removed]", text)


def execute(identifier, token):
    row = task_queue.tasks(ROOT).get(identifier)
    if not row or row.get("claim_token") != token or row["status"] not in task_queue.ACTIVE:
        return 0
    stopped = threading.Event()
    heartbeat = None
    owns_task = False
    answer = ""
    try:
        with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
            stack.enter_context(lock("dispatch-" + identifier, blocking=False))
            owns_task = True
            stack.enter_context(slot(row["worker"]))
            api_only = row["worker"] in task_queue.API_WORKERS
            kind = task_queue.task_kind(row["kind"], row["prompt"])
            if api_only and (kind not in ("analysis", *REPORT_KINDS) or row.get("publish_project")):
                raise ResultRejected(row["worker"] + " is API-only; this task needs files or command execution. "
                                     "Choose codex or cursor-agent, or supply the evidence in the request.")
            project, repo = (report_repository(row) if api_only and kind in REPORT_KINDS else
                             task_repository(row["prompt"]) if not api_only else (None, None))
            if project and project.lower() == "webjam" and row["kind"] == "coding":
                stack.enter_context(lock("webjam-coder", blocking=False))
                from jobs import external_webjam_lease
                from runtime import screen_sessions
                if external_webjam_lease(screen_sessions()):
                    raise BlockingIOError("External WebJam coder is active")
            if not task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "running"):
                return 0

            def renew():
                while not stopped.wait(10):
                    try:
                        current = task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "heartbeat")
                    except Exception:
                        current = None
                    if not current:
                        # command.run handles TERM by killing the entire model
                        # subprocess group. An expired worker cannot keep editing.
                        if not stopped.is_set():
                            os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGTERM)
                        return

            heartbeat = threading.Thread(target=renew, daemon=True)
            heartbeat.start()
            review = (pr_evidence.load(row["prompt"], project_catalog())
                      if row["kind"] in ("analysis", "review") else None)
            folder = CONDUCTOR / "state/task-runs" / identifier
            folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            branch, cwd, base_sha = None, None, None
            coding = row["kind"] in ("coding", "artifact")
            evidence_metadata = None
            evidence_body = ""
            prompt = ("Web Bob assigned this task from Jeff. Complete it and return the actual result in your final answer. "
                      "Do not merely propose a plan or claim another worker will do it. If blocked, begin BLOCKER:. "
                      "Use the supplied evidence; do not invent observations.\n\n")
            if review:
                if not api_only:
                    cwd = analysis_workspace(row["worker"])
                atomic_write(folder / "PR-EVIDENCE.json", json.dumps(review["metadata"], indent=2) + "\n")
                evidence_body = review["text"]
                if not api_only:
                    prompt += evidence_body + "\n\n"
            elif api_only and kind in REPORT_KINDS:
                if repo is None:
                    raise ResultRejected("The assigned source job has no available worktree for repository evidence")
                evidence_body, evidence_metadata = api_worker.evidence(repo, row["worker"], row["prompt"], redact, kind=kind)
                atomic_write(folder / "REPO-EVIDENCE.json", json.dumps(evidence_metadata, indent=2) + "\n")
            elif not api_only and row["worker"] != "mini":
                branch = "draft/" + identifier
                record = dict(repo=str(repo), worktree=str(ROOT / "workspaces" / identifier),
                              branch=branch, job_dir=str(folder))
                cwd = prepare_task(record, coding)
                if row["kind"] == "coding":
                    base_sha = git(cwd, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
                prompt += ("Keep all work local: never push or create/modify a PR. "
                           "Use only the assigned worktree " + str(cwd) + " on " + branch + ". "
                           "For code changes, commit locally and report the SHA, changed paths and tests.\n\n")
                if row["kind"] == "coding":
                    prompt += result_acceptance.CODING_REQUIREMENTS + "\n"
                    checks = mini_worker.coding_commands(project)
                    if checks:
                        prompt += ("Before accepting or publishing your commit, the server will independently re-run: "
                                   + json.dumps(checks) + ". Leave the checkout ready to run these commands.\n")
            if api_only:
                prompt += api_worker.report_prompt(row["prompt"], evidence_body, row["kind"])
            else:
                prompt += "TASK:\n" + row["prompt"]
                prompt += "\n" + api_worker.report_instructions(row["prompt"], row["kind"])
            if row.get("publish_project"):
                prompt += ("\nThe server has Jeff's explicit authorization to push this project's assigned draft branch. "
                           "Your part is one useful, bounded improvement: inspect, fix, test and commit locally. "
                           "The server will perform and verify the draft push after accepting your local result. "
                           "Do not push yourself or mark the task blocked because you did not push. Return changes, tests and SHA in under 150 words.")
            if review:
                prompt += ("\nReturn a concise code review, at most 250 words. Lead with concrete bugs/risks and changed paths/lines, "
                           "or explicitly state no concrete defects found. Do not recap every changed file. End with testing limits.")
            if row["kind"] == "artifact":
                contents, destination = task_queue.file_request(row["prompt"])
                target = Path(destination)
                if target.is_symlink():
                    raise RuntimeError("Refusing a symlink destination")
                previous_mtime = target.stat().st_mtime_ns if target.exists() else None
                prompt += ("\nThis small file task explicitly authorizes writing only " + destination +
                           " outside the worktree. Write the requested text even if the file already exists, verify it, and return its path. "
                           "No repository changes or commit are needed. Keep the answer under 80 words.")
            atomic_write(folder / "prompt.txt", redact(prompt))
            if not task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "executing", worktree=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
                                         branch=branch, project=project, repo=str(repo) if repo else None,
                                         model=WORKER_MODELS.get(row["worker"]),
                                         prompt_file=str(folder / "prompt.txt")):
                return 0
            answer = redact(mini_worker.execute(row) if row["worker"] == "mini" else ask(row["worker"], prompt, cwd=cwd, coding=coding,
                                timeout=90 if row["kind"] == "artifact" else 1800 if coding else 240,
                                purpose=("api-worker-research" if kind == "research" else "api-worker") if api_only else "pr-review" if review else "small-file" if row["kind"] == "artifact" else "small-review" if row["worker"] == "claude" else "webbob-" + identifier,
                                locked=True)).strip()
            answer = api_worker.render_report(row["prompt"], answer, row["kind"])
            result_acceptance.check_answer(row["prompt"], answer, kind=row["kind"])
            if review:
                metadata = review["metadata"]
                if not any(file["path"] in answer for file in metadata["files"]):
                    raise ResultRejected("PR review did not cite any changed file")
                answer = ("[PR #" + str(metadata["number"]) + "](" + metadata["url"] +
                          ") @ `" + metadata["headRefOid"][:7] + "` — static diff review by " + row["worker"] + ".\n\n" + answer)
            if row["kind"] == "artifact":
                if target.is_symlink() or not target.is_file() or target.read_text().rstrip("\n") != contents:
                    raise ResultRejected("Worker did not write the requested file contents")
                if target.stat().st_mtime_ns == previous_mtime:
                    raise ResultRejected("Worker left the preexisting file unchanged; no write verified")
                answer += "\n\nServer verified file: " + destination + "; SHA-256: " + hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            if coding:
                if git(cwd, "branch", "--show-current") != branch:
                    raise ResultRejected("Worker changed its assigned draft branch")
                if git(cwd, "status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=normal"):
                    raise ResultRejected("Worker left uncommitted changes; task is not complete")
            coding_evidence = None
            if row["kind"] == "coding":
                result_acceptance.check_coding_answer(answer)
                head = git(cwd, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
                changed = git(cwd, "diff", "--name-only", base_sha, head).splitlines()
                diff = git(cwd, "diff", "--no-ext-diff", base_sha, head)
                if len(diff) > 60000:
                    raise ResultRejected("Coding diff exceeds bounded acceptance review; choose a smaller fix")
                validation = mini_worker.verify_coding(cwd, project, folder, redact)
                coding_evidence = dict(base_sha=base_sha, sha=head, changed_paths=changed, diff=diff,
                                       validation=validation)
                # Read test definitions from the assigned commit, with the existing safe evidence reader.
                definitions = {}
                for path in git(cwd, "ls-files").splitlines():
                    if (Path(path).name in ("AGENTS.md", "package.json", "pyproject.toml", "Makefile")
                            and api_worker.safe_source(cwd, path)):
                        definitions[path] = api_worker.read_source(cwd, path, 12000).decode("utf-8", "replace")
                coding_evidence["test_definitions"] = definitions
                coding_evidence = json.loads(redact(json.dumps(coding_evidence)))
                atomic_write(folder / "CODING-EVIDENCE.json", json.dumps(coding_evidence, indent=2) + "\n")
            if row["worker"] == "mini" or row["kind"] == "artifact":
                acceptance = dict(accepted=True, method="verified-execution")
            else:
                acceptance_task = row["prompt"]
                if row.get("publish_project"):
                    acceptance_task += ("\nDelegated worker scope: inspect, fix, test and commit locally. "
                                        "Evaluate that local work. The server, not this worker, performs and verifies the authorized draft push after acceptance.")
                acceptance = result_acceptance.accept(
                    acceptance_task, answer,
                    lambda instructions: ask(row["worker"], instructions, cwd=cwd, coding=False,
                                             timeout=120, purpose="result-acceptance", locked=True),
                    coding_evidence=coding_evidence, report_evidence=evidence_body if api_only else None,
                    kind=row["kind"])
                acceptance = json.loads(redact(json.dumps(acceptance)))
                if coding_evidence is not None:
                    if (git(cwd, "rev-parse", "HEAD") != coding_evidence["sha"]
                            or git(cwd, "branch", "--show-current") != branch
                            or git(cwd, "status", "--porcelain")):
                        raise ResultRejected("Coding checkout changed after verification")
                    acceptance["coding_evidence"] = dict(
                        sha=coding_evidence["sha"], base_sha=base_sha, changed_paths=changed,
                        validation=validation, path=str(folder / "CODING-EVIDENCE.json"))
            publication = None
            if row.get("publish_project"):
                if not task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "heartbeat"):
                    return 0
                publication = draft_publish.publish(row, cwd, folder, project_catalog())
                answer = ("Pushed [" + publication["project"] + " draft](" + publication["url"] + ") @ `" +
                          publication["sha"][:7] + "`; remote SHA verified.\n\n" + answer)
                if publication.get("pr_url"):
                    answer = "Draft PR: " + publication["pr_url"] + "\n" + answer
            # The queue is the result outbox. A result file alone is never proof
            # of completion, and a superseded claim cannot publish a result.
            digest = hashlib.sha256(answer.encode()).hexdigest()
            acceptance["result_sha256"] = digest
            report_path = None
            if api_only:
                if not task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "heartbeat"):
                    return 0
                report_path = api_worker.save_report(CONDUCTOR, identifier, token, row["worker"], answer, redact)
            result = task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "succeeded", result=answer,
                                           result_sha256=digest, worktree=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
                                           branch=branch, acceptance=acceptance,
                                           review_source=review["metadata"] if review else None, publication=publication,
                                           evidence=evidence_metadata,
                                           result_path=str(report_path) if report_path else None)
            stopped.set()
            if result:
                atomic_write(folder / "RESULT.md", answer + "\n")
            return 0 if result else 1
    except BlockingIOError:
        if owns_task:
            task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "busy", error="Waiting for the shared worker lane")
        return 0
    except ResultRejected as exc:
        message = redact(str(exc))[:1500]
        task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "rejected", error=message, rejected_result=answer,
                              acceptance=dict(accepted=False, reason=message))
        return 1
    except Exception as exc:
        message = redact(str(exc)) if isinstance(exc, RuntimeError) else type(exc).__name__
        task_queue.transition(ROOT, identifier, token, "retry", error=message[:1500])
        return 1
    finally:
        stopped.set()
        if heartbeat:
            heartbeat.join(timeout=1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(execute(*sys.argv[1:3]))


FILE services/test_result_acceptance.py
"""Task-aware acceptance: deterministic failures and strict semantic verdicts."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock

from result_acceptance import ResultRejected, accept, check_answer, check_coding_answer
from dispatch_worker import task_queue


class AcceptanceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def completion_fixture(self):
        return json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("fixtures").joinpath("texting_jobcompletion_1035.json").read_text())

    def test_1035_exploratory_answer_cannot_win_a_positive_vote(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Has architecture findings"}')
        for kind in (None, "test-writing", "analysis", "coding"):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind), self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing concrete regression"):
                accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["answer"], review, kind=kind)
        review.assert_not_called()

    def test_1107_exact_rejected_drafts_cannot_win_a_positive_vote(self):
        fixture = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("fixtures").joinpath("texting_jobcompletion_1107.json").read_text())
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Has test-shaped content"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing concrete regression"):
            accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["answer"], review, kind=fixture["kind"])
        review.assert_not_called()

    def test_concrete_drafts_and_conditional_unblock_plan_reach_semantic_review(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Delivers bounded test drafts and a conditional fix plan"}')
        self.assertTrue(accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["complete_answer"], review)["accepted"])
        self.assertIn("exploratory-only answers", review.call_args.args[0])
        self.assertIn("Tie it to the named ongoing task", review.call_args.args[0])

    def test_drafts_without_resolution_plan_and_empty_case_labels_fail_closed(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing actionable unblock plan"):
            check_answer(fixture["prompt"], fixture["complete_answer"].split("Unblock plan:")[0])
        for fields in ("Target:\nSetup:\nAction:\nExpected:\n", "Target: TBD\nSetup: ...\nAction: TODO\nExpected: none\n"):
            with self.subTest(fields=fields), self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing concrete regression"):
                check_answer("Draft regression tests", "Regression test: queue\n" + fields)

    def test_executable_test_draft_is_supported_but_copied_implementation_is_not(self):
        check_answer("Draft test cases", "Unexecuted draft for tests/test_queue.py:\n```python\ndef test_enqueue():\n    job = {'id': 'one'}\n    assert enqueue(job) == job\n```")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing concrete regression"):
            check_answer("Draft test cases", "```python\ndef enqueue(job):\n    return job\n```")

    def test_formatted_but_irrelevant_drafts_still_require_semantic_acceptance(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": false, "reason": "Drafts do not address the requested behavior"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "did not complete the task"):
            accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["complete_answer"], review)
        self.assertEqual(review.call_count, 1)

    def test_report_reviewer_checks_assertions_against_the_workers_snapshot(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        evidence = "FILE queue.py\ndef enqueue(job):\n    return job\n"
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": false, "reason": "The proposed assertion contradicts the supplied implementation"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "contradicts"):
            accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["complete_answer"], review, report_evidence=evidence)
        prompt = review.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(prompt.split("\n\n", 1)[1])["report_evidence"], evidence)
        self.assertIn("Reject tests whose expected result contradicts", prompt)

    def test_peer_audit_of_named_unblock_also_requires_resolution_steps(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "Missing actionable unblock plan"):
            check_answer("Whole-repo audit and implementation plan for Web Bob unblock 1035", "The queue has three states.", kind="audit")
        check_answer("Summarize supplied architecture", "The queue has three states.", kind="analysis")

    def test_quoted_limitation_cannot_hide_following_deliverables_or_pending_verification(self):
        fixture = self.completion_fixture()
        check_answer(fixture["prompt"], "> Original assignment unavailable.\n\n" + fixture["complete_answer"])
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "verification is incomplete"):
            check_coding_answer("> Previous baseline passed.\n\nFixed and committed. API tests not run.")

    def test_0810_captured_incomplete_verification_cannot_be_overridden(self):
        fixture = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("fixtures").joinpath("texting_verification_0810.json").read_text())
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Claims pass"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "verification is incomplete"):
            accept(fixture["prompt"], fixture["answer"], review, coding_evidence={"sha": fixture["claimed_sha"]})
        review.assert_not_called()

    def test_coding_promises_and_pending_tests_are_not_completion(self):
        for answer in ("Fixed and committed. Tests pending.", "Will run API tests next. Status: Pass.",
                       "Shared tests pass, API tests not run.", "Committed. Verification is incomplete."):
            with self.subTest(answer=answer), self.assertRaises(ResultRejected):
                check_coding_answer(answer)
        check_coding_answer("Fixed and committed abc1234. pnpm test: 343 passed, exit 0; pnpm build: exit 0.")

    def test_coding_reviewer_gets_change_scope_and_fresh_check_evidence(self):
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "The fix and required tests completed."}')
        evidence = dict(sha="abc1234", changed_paths=["apps/api/src/module.ts"],
                        validation=dict(all_pass=True, checks=[dict(command=["pnpm", "test"], exit=0)]))
        result = accept("Fix the API", "API fixed. pnpm test: 343 pass, exit 0; commit abc1234.",
                        review, coding_evidence=evidence)
        self.assertTrue(result["accepted"])
        instructions = review.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertIn("Shared tests, prepare smoke, stale runs, or plans alone", instructions)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(instructions.split("\n\n", 1)[1])["coding_evidence"], evidence)

    def test_malformed_cursor_review_retries_validation_once_without_accepting_prose(self):
        review = Mock(side_effect=["Checking the changes.\nThe result satisfies the task.",
                                   '{"accepted": true, "reason": "The requested fix was committed and tested."}'])
        self.assertTrue(accept("Fix report job visibility", "Fixed and tested visibility; committed the change.", review)["accepted"])
        self.assertEqual(review.call_count, 2)
        self.assertIn("Do not use tools", review.call_args.args[0])
        review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": false, "reason": "Missing the requested fix"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "did not complete"):
            accept("Fix visibility", "I plan to fix it", review)
        self.assertEqual(review.call_count, 1)

    def test_refusals_empty_answers_and_real_blockers_never_reach_reviewer(self):
        for answer in ("", "   ", "BLOCKER: missing evidence", "**BLOCKER:** Cannot proceed",
                       "I cannot access the Git worktree.\nVerification phrase: marker",
                       "Unfortunately, I can't perform the requested action.",
                       "As an AI language model, I am unable to complete this task.",
                       "I don't have access to the requested repository."):
            with self.subTest(answer=answer):
                review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Looks OK"}')
                with self.assertRaises(ResultRejected):
                    accept("Write a confirmation with marker.", answer, review)
                review.assert_not_called()

    def test_exact_text_required_without_relying_on_reviewer(self):
        for instruction in ("Include the exact phrase 'check marker'.", 'Return exact text: "check marker".',
                            "Use the exact string `check marker`."):
            with self.subTest(instruction=instruction):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "omitted required exact text"):
                    check_answer(instruction, "I ran successfully.")
                check_answer(instruction, "I ran and confirm check marker.")

    def test_no_blocker_quoted_refusals_and_short_answers_are_valid_candidates(self):
        for answer in ("BLOCKER: none\n42", "**BLOCKER:** no blockers\nThe total is 42.",
                       "The worker refused:\n> I cannot access the worktree.\nThis framing caused the failure.",
                       'Example output:\n```text\nI cannot access files.\n```\nRemove the worktree instruction.', "42"):
            with self.subTest(answer=answer):
                check_answer("Analyze the supplied evidence.", answer)

    def test_reviewer_receives_full_task_and_answer_as_data(self):
        prompt = "Calculate 10 + 32 and return the sum."
        answer = "The sum is 42."
        review = Mock(return_value='```json\n{"accepted": true, "reason": "Returns the requested sum."}\n```')
        self.assertTrue(accept(prompt, answer, review)["accepted"])
        instructions = review.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertIn("untrusted task and answer data", instructions)
        payload = json.loads(instructions.split("\n\n", 1)[1])
        self.assertEqual(payload, {"task": prompt, "answer": answer})

    def test_generic_help_plan_off_topic_and_marker_only_are_rejected_by_task_review(self):
        for answer in ("I can help with math. Let me know what you need.",
                       "I will calculate the sum in a future step.",
                       "Tomatoes prefer sunny weather.", "Verification phrase: marker"):
            with self.subTest(answer=answer):
                review = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": false, "reason": "Does not supply the requested sum."}')
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ResultRejected, "did not complete the task"):
                    accept("Calculate 10 + 32 and include marker.", answer, review)

    def test_malformed_or_missing_review_fails_closed(self):
        for value in ("", "true", "[]", "null", "{}", '{"accepted": true}',
                      '{"accepted": 1, "reason": "wrong type"}', '{"accepted": true, "reason": ""}',
                      '{"accepted": true, "reason": "yes", "extra": 1}',
                      'Accepted: {"accepted": true, "reason": "yes"}'):
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                with self.assertRaises(ResultRejected):
                    accept("Calculate 10 + 32", "42", Mock(return_value=value))
        with self.assertRaises(ResultRejected):
            accept("Calculate 10 + 32", "42", Mock(side_effect=RuntimeError("provider down")))

    def test_capability_detection_distinguishes_supplied_text_from_file_work(self):
        for prompt in ("Read /tmp/a.txt and summarize it.", "Review services/dispatch_worker.py.",
                       "Inspect the repository for the consumer.", "List the files in the directory.",
                       "Write hello to /tmp/a.txt", "Save your answer to reviews/answer.md."):
            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
                self.assertTrue(task_queue.needs_files(prompt))
        for prompt in ("Write one sentence confirming you ran, into your answer.",
                       "Explain how to read a file in Python.",
                       "Analyze failure/recovery scenarios using these supplied facts.",
                       "Review input/output rates: 10 requests per second, 5 responses per second.",
                       "Review the supplied evidence: jobs never reach a consumer.",
                       "Review this snippet:\n```python\nread /tmp/a.txt\n```",
                       "Goal:\nRead services/worker.py.\n\nYour independent subtask:\nAnalyze the supplied evidence."):
            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
                self.assertFalse(task_queue.needs_files(prompt))

    def test_file_changes_require_write_tools_and_reads_remain_read_only(self):
        for prompt in ("Write reviews/result.md with your findings.", "Save the answer to `reviews/result.md`.",
                       "Review services/worker.py and edit services/worker.py.",
                       "Here is the text:\n```\nhello\n```\nWrite it to /tmp/hello.txt."):
            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
                self.assertEqual(task_queue.task_kind("analysis", prompt), "coding")
        self.assertEqual(task_queue.task_kind("analysis", "Read `services/worker.py` and explain its code."), "inspection")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/worker_assignment.py
"""Turn requests to use LLMs on ongoing work into durable, scoped assignments."""
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import task_queue
from api_tasks import ROLES
from build_info import CODE_ROOT, git_checkout_root
from chat_intent import blocked_scopes, command, project_targets, state_for
from live_status_snapshot import observe_work

NAMES = r"(?:gemini|minimax|codex|claude|cursor(?:-agent)?|grok|xai|xAI)"
DIRECTIVE = re.compile(
    r"^(?:remember\s*[:,]?\s*(?:that\s+)?(?:(?:you|we)\s+can\s+|to\s+)?|(?:you|we)\s+can\s+)?"
    r"(?:also\s+)?(?:use|assign|put|have|get|delegate\s+to)\s+(?P<names>" + NAMES +
    r"(?:\s*(?:,|&|and)\s*(?:and\s+)?" + NAMES + r")*)\b(?P<rest>.*)$", re.I | re.S)
ONGOING = re.compile(r"\b(?:ongoing|current|existing|active|running|queued)\b.{0,35}\b(?:jobs?|tasks?|work|projects?)\b|"
                     r"\b(?:jobs?|tasks?|work|projects?)\b.{0,30}\b(?:ongoing|underway|running)\b|"
                     r"\b(?:for|on|with)\s+(?:these|those|our|the)\s+(?:jobs?|tasks?|work)\b", re.I)


def requested_workers(text, conversation=None):
    cleaned = command(text)
    if re.match(r"^(?:don't|do not|never|stop|hold)\b", cleaned, re.I):
        return None
    # A follow-up references the last explicit worker request, not arbitrary
    # names from a model answer or provider-health metadata.
    followup = re.search(r"\b(?:add|assign|queue|give)\s+(?:some |more |new )?(?:work|jobs?|tasks?)\s+(?:for|to)\s+(?:them|those (?:LLMs|workers))\b|"
                         r"\b(?:want|need)\s+them\s+(?:workout|working|to work|busy)\b|"
                         r"\b(?:put|get|keep)\s+them\s+(?:to work|working|busy)\b", cleaned, re.I)
    if followup:
        names = (conversation or {}).get("assignment_workers")
        if not names:
            for message in reversed((conversation or {}).get("messages", [])):
                if message.get("role") == "user" and message.get("content") != text:
                    names = requested_workers(message.get("content", ""))
                    if names:
                        break
        if names:
            return list(names)
    all_workers = re.match(r"^(?:use|assign|put|get|keep)\s+all\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:LLMs|models|workers)\b", cleaned, re.I)
    if all_workers and (ONGOING.search(cleaned) or re.search(r"\b(?:to work|busy|working)\b", cleaned, re.I)):
        return list(task_queue.MODEL_WORKERS)
    match = DIRECTIVE.match(cleaned)
    reminder = re.match(r"^(?:remember|you can|we can)\b", cleaned, re.I)
    if not match or not (ONGOING.search(match["rest"]) or (reminder and re.fullmatch(r"\s*(?:too|also)?[.!]*", match["rest"], re.I))):
        return None
    return list(dict.fromkeys(name.lower().replace("cursor-agent", "cursor")
                              for name in re.findall(NAMES, match["names"], re.I)))


def issue_followup(root, conversation, text):
    if not re.fullmatch(r"if you find (?:any )?(?:issues|bugs)[, ]+(?:please )?fix them[.!]*", command(text), re.I):
        return None
    previous = next((m["dispatch_intent"] for m in reversed(conversation["messages"][:-2])
                     if m.get("dispatch_intent", {}).get("worker_assignment")), None)
    if not previous or not previous.get("assignment_projects"):
        return None
    project = previous["assignment_projects"][0]
    return ("Fix verified issues in " + project + "\nInspect the ongoing work and available review reports, "
            "reproduce a concrete issue, implement one bounded fix if warranted, and run relevant tests. "
            "Report evidence if no fix is needed. Work in your assigned draft worktree; never deploy or merge.")


def deployed_project_context(root):
    """A pinned checkout can supply API evidence, never proof of ongoing work."""
    # Deployments can have no active jobs and no identifiable project screen.
    # Both services must select this running checkout; an arbitrary/stale pin
    # or a rollback copy without Git evidence is not enough to assign a report.
    try:
        for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
            value = (root / "conductor/state" / (name + "-code-root")).read_text().strip()
            path = Path(value)
            if not path.is_absolute() or path.resolve(strict=True) != CODE_ROOT:
                return None
    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
        return None
    if git_checkout_root(CODE_ROOT) is None:
        return None
    return dict(id=None, project="Web Bob", context="project context", prompt=(
        "The Web Bob checkout is selected by matching deployment pins for this server and its dispatcher; "
        "no current task or worker execution is confirmed. Inspect the supplied repository snapshot for "
        "concrete dispatch findings and regression test drafts, including webchat/build_info.py, "
        "webchat/worker_assignment.py, "
        "webchat/task_dispatch.py, state/task_queue.py and services/dispatch_worker.py. "
        "Do not infer active work, progress or results from deployment metadata."))


def plan(root, conversation, text, workers):
    for worker in workers:
        task_queue.worker_name(worker)  # Reject Grok/xAI before any queue write.
    observed = observe_work(root)
    state = state_for(conversation)
    targets = project_targets(text, state)
    candidates = []
    for row in observed["records"].values():
        if (row.get("status") or row.get("state")) not in ("queued", "claimed", "launched", "running"):
            continue
        if row.get("source_job_id") or (row.get("kind") in ("audit", "test-writing", "planning", "review", "research", "eval-grading")):
            continue  # Never recursively create report jobs about report jobs.
        prompt = row.get("prompt") or row.get("title") or row.get("project")
        if not prompt:
            continue
        names = project_targets(row.get("project") or prompt, state)
        if targets and not any(name in targets for name in names):
            continue
        if blocked_scopes(state, names):
            continue
        candidates.append(dict(id=row["job_id"], prompt=prompt,
                               project=", ".join(names) or "Web Bob"))
    # Some ongoing jobs are external CLI workers, observed outside the journal.
    known = {row["id"] for row in candidates}
    for row in observed["work"]:
        if row["state"] != "running" or row.get("job_id") in known or not (row.get("task") or row.get("title")) or not row.get("project"):
            continue
        if row.get("job_id") and observed["records"].get(row["job_id"], {}).get("kind") in ("audit", "test-writing", "planning", "review", "research", "eval-grading"):
            continue
        if targets and row["project"] not in targets:
            continue
        if blocked_scopes(state, [row["project"]]):
            continue
        task = row.get("task") or ("Observed active lane: " + row["title"] +
                                   ". Its original task prompt was not recorded; inspect the project's current code and "
                                   "state that limitation. Do not invent the lane's exact assignment.")
        candidates.append(dict(id=None, prompt=task, project=row["project"]))
    # An open project lane can outlive its worker process. It still identifies
    # a repository for a new API report, but proves neither an ongoing task nor
    # execution. Use it only when no permitted active assignment is available;
    # never turn this limited context into a coding job or a fake source job ID.
    if not candidates and all(worker in task_queue.API_WORKERS for worker in workers):
        seen_projects = set()
        for row in observed["work"]:
            project = row.get("project")
            if (row["state"] != "unverified" or row.get("source") != "screen" or row.get("job_id")
                    or row.get("screen") not in observed.get("sessions", {})
                    or not project or not row.get("repo") or not row.get("title") or project in seen_projects):
                continue
            if (targets and project not in targets) or blocked_scopes(state, [project]):
                continue
            context = ("Observed open project lane: " + row["title"] + ". Its worker activity is unverified; "
                       "no current task is confirmed. Inspect the project's supplied repository snapshot "
                       "for concrete findings and regression test drafts. Do not infer active work, "
                       "progress or results from the lane name or historical logs.")
            candidates.append(dict(id=None, prompt=context, project=project, context="project context"))
            seen_projects.add(project)
        if not candidates and (not targets or "Web Bob" in targets) and not blocked_scopes(state, ["Web Bob"]):
            context = deployed_project_context(root)
            if context:
                candidates.append(context)
    if not candidates:
        raise ValueError("I couldn't assign those workers: there is no identifiable ongoing job permitted by the current holds. Name a project and task to start.")
    health = root / "conductor/lanes.json"
    board = json.loads(health.read_text()) if health.exists() else {}
    packages = []
    for index, worker in enumerate(workers):
        row = board.get(worker, {})
        if row.get("state") in ("needs_key", "unavailable", "budget_exhausted", "cooldown", "needs_login"):
            raise ValueError("I couldn't assign " + worker + ": " + row["state"].replace("_", " ") + ". No new assignments were queued.")
        source = candidates[index % len(candidates)]
        kind, role = ROLES.get(worker, ("analysis", "Review the ongoing task and propose next steps"))
        context = source.get("context", "ongoing task")
        # One concrete bounded package per requested worker. Keep existing
        # coders running; do not revoke a live claim or share their edit rights.
        prompt = ("Project: " + source["project"] + "\nYour independent subtask:\n" + role +
                  " for the following " + context + ". Deliver concrete findings and an actionable plan. " +
                  ("Lead with two unexecuted regression drafts: one failure and one successful control. "
                   "Each needs Regression test:, Target:, Setup:, Action: and Expected: lines with concrete values "
                   "on the same line as each label. Keep findings brief. "
                   if kind == "test-writing" else "") +
                  "Include a separate '" + ("Unblock plan" if re.search(r"\bunblock\b", source["prompt"], re.I)
                                             else "Action plan") + ":' section after the deliverables with "
                  "Change:, Validation: and Exit condition: on separate lines, each with its concrete value "
                  "on the SAME line. Test drafts do not replace this required plan. " +
                  "Never deploy or resolve conflicts.\n" +
                  context.capitalize() + ":\n" + source["prompt"][:8000])
        if source["project"] == "Web Bob":
            prompt += ("\nRelevant evidence: services/dispatch_worker.py, services/result_acceptance.py, "
                       "services/test_result_acceptance.py, services/test_api_workers.py, "
                       "state/task_queue.py, webchat/worker_assignment.py, webchat/test_dispatch.py. "
                       "Inspect these supplied files before proposing dispatch/completion regressions.")
        packages.append(dict(worker=worker, kind=kind, title=source["project"] + " — " + role,
                             prompt=prompt, source_job_id=source["id"]))
    return packages


FILE state/task_queue.py
"""Durable Web Bob tasks. Every transition is locked, fsynced and claim fenced."""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import fcntl
import json
import os
import re
import time
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from api_tasks import REPORT_KINDS, normalize_kind

MODEL_WORKERS = ("codex", "claude", "cursor", "gemini", "minimax")
API_WORKERS = ("gemini", "minimax")
WORKERS = (*MODEL_WORKERS, "mini")
CONCURRENCY = {"gemini": 3, "minimax": 3}
ACTIVE = ("claimed", "running")
TERMINAL = ("succeeded", "failed")
LEASE_SECONDS = 90
MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3


def file_request(prompt):
    """A small explicit temporary-file task may use any tool-capable CLI."""
    match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:please\s+)?write\s+([^\n]{1,1000}?)\s+to\s+(/tmp/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.(?:txt|md|json))\s*", prompt, re.I)
    return (match[1].strip("\"'"), match[2]) if match else None


def file_task_kind(prompt):
    """Recognize requested local file access, not file names in supplied code.

    Max-mode packages carry the overall goal before their own assignment. Only
    the assignment determines whether that particular worker needs tools.
    Ambiguous requests still have to pass task-aware result acceptance.
    """
    task = prompt.rsplit("Your independent subtask:", 1)[-1]
    task = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", task, flags=re.S)
    task = re.split(r"\n\s*(?:SUPPLIED EVIDENCE|EVIDENCE|SOURCE|CODE)\s*:", task, maxsplit=1, flags=re.I)[0]
    read = r"(?:read|open|inspect|review|audit|summari[sz]e|analy[sz]e|check|list|search)"
    write = r"(?:write|save|create|edit|modify|update|delete|remove|append)"
    # A path is an instruction target only after a task verb. 'Explain how to
    # read a file' and pasted source mentioning paths remain answer-only work.
    start = r"(?:^|[\n.;]\s*|\band\s+|\bthen\s+)\s*(?:please\s+)?"
    qualifiers = r"(?:(?:the|this|that|local|actual|current|our|my|all|contents of|files in|source in|read-only)\s+){0,5}"
    # Slash-separated concepts such as input/output are not local paths.
    target = (r"[`\"']?(?:(?:/|\./|\.\./|~/)[\w./-]+|(?:[\w.-]+/)+[\w.-]+\.[\w]+\b|"
              r"(?:services|webchat|conductor|state|src|lib|tests|docs|config|reviews)/[\w./-]*|"
              r"[\w-]+\.(?:py|md|txt|json|js|ts|tsx|jsx|sh|yaml|yml|toml|csv|log)\b|"
              r"(?:git\s+)?(?:worktree|checkout|repository|repo|directory|folder|files?)\b)")
    if (re.search(start + write + r"\s+" + qualifiers + target, task, re.I) or
            re.search(start + r"(?:write|save|create|append)\b[^\n]{0,120}\b(?:to|in|under|at)\s+" +
                      qualifiers + target, task, re.I)):
        return "coding"
    if re.search(start + read + r"\s+" + qualifiers + target, task, re.I):
        return "inspection"
    return None


def needs_files(prompt):
    return file_task_kind(prompt) is not None


def task_kind(kind, prompt):
    if kind == "analysis":
        return file_task_kind(prompt) or kind
    return kind


def validate_worker(worker, kind):
    if worker != "auto" and worker not in worker_order(kind):
        if worker == "minimax" and kind == "research":
            raise ValueError("Research with google_search requires Gemini. MiniMax can review evidence, draft tests and grade evals.")
        if worker in API_WORKERS:
            raise ValueError(worker + " is API-only: it can audit, plan, research, review, draft tests or grade evals "
                             "into reviews/. It cannot edit project files, deploy, resolve conflicts or run commands. "
                             "Choose codex or cursor-agent for that work.")
        raise ValueError("This task needs a compatible tool worker: " + ", ".join(worker_order(kind)) + ".")


def worker_name(value):
    value = {"cursor-agent": "cursor", "mini CPU": "mini"}.get(value, value)
    if value not in (*WORKERS, "auto"):
        raise ValueError("Choose codex, claude, cursor-agent, gemini, minimax, or mini; never Grok.")
    return value


@contextlib.contextmanager
def locked(root):
    folder = Path(root) / "conductor/state"
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (folder / ".queue.lock").open("a") as handle:
        fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        yield folder / "queue.jsonl"


def _read(path):
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    data = path.read_bytes()
    rows, offset = {}, 0
    for line in data.splitlines(keepends=True):
        try:
            row = json.loads(line)
            rows[row["id"]] = row
        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            if offset + len(line) != len(data) or line.endswith(b"\n"):
                raise ValueError("Task queue corruption; refusing to discard committed records") from None
            # Only a torn final append can be discarded, under the writer lock.
            with path.open("r+b") as handle:
                handle.truncate(offset)
                handle.flush()
                os.fsync(handle.fileno())
            break
        offset += len(line)
    if data and not data.endswith(b"\n") and offset == len(data):
        with path.open("ab") as handle:
            handle.write(b"\n")
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    return rows


def _append(path, row, event, now=None):
    row = dict(row, event=event, updated_at=time.time() if now is None else now)
    with open(os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_APPEND, 0o600), "a") as handle:
        handle.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        handle.flush()
        os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
    try:
        os.fsync(fd)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
    return row


def tasks(root):
    with locked(root) as path:
        return _read(path)


def worker_order(kind):
    if kind in REPORT_KINDS:
        return ("gemini",) if kind == "research" else (("gemini", "minimax") if kind in ("audit", "planning") else ("minimax", "gemini"))
    return {"coding": ("codex", "cursor"), "artifact": ("codex", "cursor", "claude"), "local": ("mini",),
            "inspection": ("codex", "cursor"),
            "analysis": ("claude", "gemini", "minimax", "codex", "cursor")}[kind]


def capacity(worker):
    return CONCURRENCY.get(worker, 1)


def group_complete(members):
    if not members:
        return False
    first = members[0]
    size = first.get("group_size")
    return (type(size) is int and 2 <= size <= 24 and len(members) == size
            and {r.get("group_index") for r in members} == set(range(size))
            and all((r.get("group_size"), r.get("conversation_id"), r.get("group_goal")) ==
                    (size, first.get("conversation_id"), first.get("group_goal")) for r in members))


def enqueue(root, conversation_id, request_id, prompt, *, worker="auto", kind="analysis", source_file=None,
            group_id=None, group_size=None, group_index=None, group_goal=None, title=None, local_suite=None,
            publish_project=None, draft_pr=False, source_job_id=None):
    worker = worker_name(worker)
    if worker == "mini":
        kind = "local"
    kind = normalize_kind(worker, task_kind(kind, prompt), prompt)
    if kind not in ("analysis", "inspection", "coding", "artifact", "local", *REPORT_KINDS) or not prompt.strip():
        raise ValueError("Task needs a description and a supported kind")
    if kind == "artifact" and not file_request(prompt):
        raise ValueError("Small file tasks require an explicit text and /tmp file destination")
    validate_worker(worker, kind)
    if publish_project is not None and (kind != "coding" or publish_project not in ("WebJam", "AdoptIQ", "StoryBoard", "Fantasy Football", "Shelf", "Web Bob")):
        raise ValueError("Draft publication requires an explicit coding project")
    if draft_pr and not publish_project:
        raise ValueError("Draft PR requires a project publication")
    if group_id and (type(group_size) is not int or not 2 <= group_size <= 24 or
                     type(group_index) is not int or not 0 <= group_index < group_size):
        raise ValueError("A task group requires a valid size and unique subtask index")
    key = "webbob:" + str(conversation_id) + ":" + str(request_id)
    identifier = "webchat-" + uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, key).hex
    with locked(root) as path:
        rows = _read(path)
        if identifier in rows:
            row = rows[identifier]
            if (row["prompt"], row["requested_worker"], row["kind"], row.get("group_id"), row.get("group_size"),
                row.get("group_index"), row.get("group_goal"), row.get("title"), row.get("local_suite"), row.get("publish_project"), row.get("draft_pr", False), row.get("source_job_id")) != (
                    prompt, worker, kind, group_id, group_size, group_index, group_goal, title, local_suite, publish_project, draft_pr, source_job_id):
                raise ValueError("This request ID already belongs to a different task")
            return row
        for member in (r for r in rows.values() if group_id and r.get("group_id") == group_id):
            if (member.get("group_index") == group_index or
                (member.get("group_size"), member.get("conversation_id"), member.get("group_goal")) !=
                    (group_size, conversation_id, group_goal)):
                raise ValueError("Conflicting task group membership")
        return _append(path, dict(id=identifier, conversation_id=conversation_id, request_id=request_id,
                                 prompt=prompt, requested_worker=worker, kind=kind, source_file=source_file,
                                 status="queued", attempts=0, created_at=time.time(), delivered_at=None,
                                 group_id=group_id, group_size=group_size, group_index=group_index,
                                 group_goal=group_goal, title=title, local_suite=local_suite, publish_project=publish_project, draft_pr=draft_pr,
                                 source_job_id=source_job_id,
                                 tried_workers=[], max_attempts=max(MAX_ATTEMPTS, len(worker_order(kind))),
                                 worker=None, claim_token=None, lease_until=0, retry_at=0), "enqueued")


def claim(root, worker, now=None, *, identifier=None, allow_fallback=False, limit=None):
    worker = worker_name(worker)
    if worker == "auto":
        raise ValueError("Claims require an actual worker")
    now = time.time() if now is None else now
    with locked(root) as path:
        rows = _read(path)
        ceiling = capacity(worker) if limit is None else max(0, min(capacity(worker), int(limit)))
        if sum(r["status"] in ACTIVE and r["worker"] == worker for r in rows.values()) >= ceiling:
            return None
        for row in sorted(rows.values(), key=lambda r: r["created_at"]):
            if identifier and row["id"] != identifier:
                continue
            if row["status"] != "queued" or row.get("retry_at", 0) > now:
                continue
            if row["requested_worker"] not in ("auto", worker) and not allow_fallback:
                continue
            if worker not in worker_order(row["kind"]):
                continue
            if row.get("group_id") and not group_complete([r for r in rows.values() if r.get("group_id") == row["group_id"]]):
                continue  # Saved intent repairs an interrupted multi-row append before dispatch.
            row.update(status="claimed", worker=worker, claim_token=uuid.uuid4().hex,
                       claimed_at=now, lease_until=now + LEASE_SECONDS, attempts=row["attempts"] + 1,
                       pid=None, started_at=None, execution_started_at=None, error=None)
            return _append(path, row, "claimed", now)
    return None


def transition(root, identifier, token, event, *, now=None, **fields):
    now = time.time() if now is None else now
    with locked(root) as path:
        row = _read(path).get(identifier)
        if not row or row["status"] not in ACTIVE or row["claim_token"] != token or row["lease_until"] <= now:
            return None
        if event == "running":
            row.update(status="running", started_at=now, pid=os.getpid())
        elif event == "executing":
            row.update(execution_started_at=now)
        elif event == "heartbeat":
            pass
        elif event == "succeeded":
            if not fields.get("result", "").strip():
                raise ValueError("An empty result cannot finish a task")
            row.update(status="succeeded", finished_at=now)
        elif event == "rejected":
            # An unusable answer is terminal, not a provider outage to retry
            # across every lane. Keep the rejection in the durable outbox.
            row.update(status="failed", finished_at=now, claim_token=None)
        elif event in ("retry", "busy"):
            if event == "busy":
                row["attempts"] -= 1  # A lane race is not a failed execution.
            if event == "retry":
                row["tried_workers"] = list(dict.fromkeys([*row.get("tried_workers", []), row["worker"]]))
            row.update(status="failed" if row["attempts"] >= row.get("max_attempts", MAX_ATTEMPTS) else "queued",
                       retry_at=now + (5 if event == "busy" else min(60, 5 * 2 ** row["attempts"])),
                       claim_token=None)
            if row["status"] == "failed":
                row["finished_at"] = now
        else:
            raise ValueError("Unknown task transition")
        row.update(fields, heartbeat_at=now, lease_until=now + LEASE_SECONDS if row["status"] in ACTIVE else 0)
        return _append(path, row, event, now)


def recover(root, worker_alive, now=None):
    """Requeue lost claims; a live expired execution must exit before replacement."""
    now = time.time() if now is None else now
    changed = []
    with locked(root) as path:
        for row in _read(path).values():
            if row["status"] not in ACTIVE:
                continue
            alive = worker_alive(row)
            # A freshly launched worker has time to acquire its execution lock.
            if alive or (row["status"] == "claimed" and row["lease_until"] > now):
                continue
            row["tried_workers"] = list(dict.fromkeys([*row.get("tried_workers", []), row["worker"]]))
            row.update(status="failed" if row["attempts"] >= row.get("max_attempts", MAX_ATTEMPTS) else "queued",
                       claim_token=None, lease_until=0, retry_at=now,
                       error="Worker exited before returning a result; claim recovered")
            if row["status"] == "failed":
                row["finished_at"] = now
            changed.append(_append(path, row, "recovered", now))
    return changed


def delivered(root, identifier, message_id):
    with locked(root) as path:
        row = _read(path)[identifier]
        if row["status"] not in TERMINAL:
            raise ValueError("Cannot acknowledge an unfinished result")
        if not row.get("delivered_at"):
            row.update(delivered_at=time.time(), result_message_id=message_id)
            return _append(path, row, "delivered")
        return row


def cancel_replay(root, conversation_ids, reason):
    """Fence only this release's replay jobs before restoring older workers.

    Keep receipts, drafts and completed results. Existing workers observe the
    revoked claim on their next heartbeat and cannot publish after rollback.
    """
    cancelled = []
    with locked(root) as path:
        for row in _read(path).values():
            if row.get("conversation_id") not in conversation_ids or row["status"] in TERMINAL:
                continue
            row.update(status="failed", claim_token=None, lease_until=0, finished_at=time.time(), error=reason)
            row.setdefault("claimed_at", None)
            row.setdefault("started_at", None)
            cancelled.append(_append(path, row, "replay_cancelled"))
    return cancelled

"""Offline API job execution through the real scheduler, transport and ledger."""

def draft_report(target):
    return ("Unexecuted regression draft.\nRegression test: enqueue retains input\nTarget: " + target +
            "\nSetup: job = {'id': 'one'} in an isolated fixture.\nAction: call enqueue(job).\n"
            "Expected: the returned job equals the input.\nRegression test: empty input is retained\nTarget: " + target +
            "\nSetup: job = {} in an isolated fixture.\nAction: call enqueue(job).\nExpected: returns {}.\n```diff\n+def test_enqueue():\n"
            "+    job = {'id': 'one'}\n+    assert enqueue(job) == job\n```\n")


class APIWorkerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.setUp
    tearDown = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.tearDown

    def repo(self):
        repo = self.root / "repo"
        repo.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        workspaces.git(repo, "init", "-b", "main")
        (repo / "queue.py").write_text("def enqueue(job):\n    return job\n")
        (repo / "keys.env").write_text("GEMINI_API_KEY=fixture-must-not-be-sent\n")
        (repo / "webchat").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (repo / "webchat/.password").write_text("fixture-password-never-read")
        (repo / "linked.py").symlink_to(repo / "webchat/.password")
        workspaces.git(repo, "add", "queue.py", "keys.env", "webchat/.password", "linked.py")
        workspaces.git(repo, "-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@localhost", "commit", "-m", "Fixture")
        return repo

    def test_frozen_turn_one_reaches_both_api_transports_and_delivers_reports(self):

    def test_attempt2_without_a_project_lane_reaches_both_api_transports(self):
        self.frozen_turn_reports(deployed_checkout=True)

    def frozen_turn_reports(self, *, deployed_checkout):
        # Real chat -> queue -> scheduler -> worker -> metered API transport.
        # Only HTTP responses, lane health and semantic acceptance are fixtures.
        with patch.object(sys, "path", [str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "webchat"), *sys.path]):
            from app import Chat
            from build_info import CODE_ROOT
            from deploy_integrate import submit
            from dispatch_receipts import receipt_errors
            from integration_rubric import sessions
            from live_status_snapshot import observe_work

            if deployed_checkout:
                # Match restart()'s real pin layout. No project mapping or
                # report_repository mock: workers must inspect the pinned code.
                repo = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
                self.assertEqual(CODE_ROOT, repo)
                self.assertEqual(Path(workspaces.git(repo, "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")).resolve(), repo)
                self.assertEqual(workspaces.git(repo, "ls-files", "--error-unmatch", "webchat/build_info.py"),
                                 "webchat/build_info.py")
                (self.core / "state").mkdir()
                for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
                    (self.core / "state" / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(repo) + "\n")
                screens = {"lane-integrate-deploy-0204": "53678.lane-integrate-deploy-0204"}
                evidence_file = "webchat/worker_assignment.py"
                context = "no current task or worker execution is confirmed"
            else:
                repo = self.repo()
                (self.core / "services").mkdir()
                (self.core / "services/projects.json").write_text(json.dumps({"Web Bob": {"repo": str(repo)}}))
                screens = {"lane-webbob-llm-reviews": "29817.lane-webbob-llm-reviews"}
                evidence_file = "queue.py"
                context = "worker activity is unverified"
            (self.core / "keys.env").write_text("GEMINI_API_KEY=gemini-fixture-key\nMINIMAX_API_KEY=minimax-fixture-key\n")
            providers = Mock(spec=["reply"])
            providers.reply.side_effect = AssertionError("Assignments must bypass conversational models")
            chat = Chat(self.core / "webchat", self.root, providers)
            self.addCleanup(chat.close)
            cid = chat.conversation_list()["conversations"][0]["id"]
            parked_holds = {name: dict(request_id="parked", reason="Keep parked", released_projects=[])
                            for name in ("Barker", "Wildflower")}
            chat.conversations[cid]["intent_state"]["holds"] = copy.deepcopy(parked_holds)
            chat.save(chat.conversations[cid])
            started = time.time()
            # Use the deployment replay's submission function with an in-process
            # HTTP adapter; all chat/intent/journal code stays real and isolated.
            def call(route, payload=None):
                if route == "/api/messages":
                    return chat.submit(payload["conversation_id"], payload["message"], payload["request_id"])
                return chat.get(cid)
            with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=screens), \
                    patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value={}):
                if deployed_checkout:
                    before = observe_work(self.root)
                    self.assertFalse(before["records"])
                    self.assertEqual(len(before["work"]), 1)
                    lane = before["work"][0]
                    self.assertEqual(lane["screen"], "lane-integrate-deploy-0204")
                    self.assertEqual(lane["state"], "unverified")
                    self.assertEqual(lane["source"], "screen")
                    for field in ("project", "repo", "task", "job_id", "worker"):
                        self.assertIsNone(lane.get(field), field)
                receipt = submit(SimpleNamespace(call=call), cid, sessions()[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
            rows = queue.tasks(self.root)
            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 2)
            self.assertEqual(receipt_errors(receipt, rows, cid, receipt["request_id"], started), [])
            self.assertEqual([rows[key]["requested_worker"] for key in receipt["task_ids"]], ["gemini", "minimax"])
            self.assertEqual([rows[key]["kind"] for key in receipt["task_ids"]], ["audit", "test-writing"])
            providers.reply.assert_not_called()

            called = []
            def transport(argv, **kwargs):
                config = Path(argv[argv.index("--config") + 1]).read_text()
                payload = json.loads(Path(argv[argv.index("--data-binary") + 1][1:]).read_text())
                lane = "gemini" if "generativelanguage" in config else "minimax"
                current = next(row for row in queue.tasks(self.root).values() if row["status"] == "running")
                self.assertEqual(current["worker"], lane)
                self.assertEqual(current["model"], MODELS[lane])
                self.assertIsNone(current["source_job_id"])
                self.assertIn("FILE " + evidence_file, json.dumps(payload))
                self.assertIn(context, json.dumps(payload))
                if deployed_checkout:
                    self.assertIn("FILE webchat/build_info.py", json.dumps(payload))
                if not deployed_checkout:
                    self.assertNotIn("fixture-must-not-be-sent", json.dumps(payload))
                answer = lane + ": " + evidence_file + " needs a duplicate-enqueue regression. Tests were not run."
                if lane == "minimax":
                    self.assertIn("Deliver actual regression test drafts", json.dumps(payload))
                    answer = draft_report(evidence_file)
                answer += ("\nAction plan:\nChange: Add duplicate-enqueue coverage for " + evidence_file +
                           ".\nValidation: Run python3 -m unittest discover -s services -p 'test_*.py'; require zero failures."
                           "\nExit condition: Actual worker results and project validation must pass before closing the lane."
                           '\n```json\n{"accepted": true, "reason": "Delivered grounded findings and a conditional test plan."}\n```'
                           "\nFindings/limits: Duplicate-enqueue coverage is missing; drafts are unexecuted and lane activity is unverified.\n")
                if lane == "gemini":
                    self.assertIn("gemini-2.5-pro:generateContent", config)
                    result = dict(candidates=[dict(finishReason="STOP", content=dict(parts=[dict(text=answer)]))],
                                  usageMetadata=dict(promptTokenCount=100, candidatesTokenCount=30))
                else:
                    self.assertIn("https://api.minimax.io/v1/text/chatcompletion_v2", config)
                    self.assertEqual(payload["model"], "MiniMax-M2.7")
                    result = dict(choices=[dict(finish_reason="stop", message=dict(content=answer))],
                                  usage=dict(prompt_tokens=100, completion_tokens=30), base_resp=dict(status_code=0))
                Path(argv[argv.index("-o") + 1]).write_text(json.dumps(result))
                called.append(lane)
                return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="200", stderr="")

            def launch(row):
                self.assertEqual(dispatch_worker.execute(row["id"], row["claim_token"]), 0)

            with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value={lane: dict(state="ready") for lane in MODELS}), \
                    patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch", side_effect=launch), \
                    patch.object(api, "CONDUCTOR", self.core), patch.object(api, "run", side_effect=transport), \
                    patch.dict(os.environ, {"GEMINI_API_KEY": "gemini-fixture-key", "MINIMAX_API_KEY": "minimax-fixture-key"}), \
                    patch.object(dispatch_worker, "prepare_task") as prepare, \
                    patch.object(dispatch_worker.draft_publish, "publish") as publish:
                self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 2)
            self.assertEqual(called, ["gemini", "minimax"])
            self.assertEqual(chat.get(cid)["intent_state"]["holds"], parked_holds)
            prepare.assert_not_called()
            publish.assert_not_called()
            for row in queue.tasks(self.root).values():
                self.assertEqual(row["status"], "succeeded")
                self.assertEqual(row["worker"], row["requested_worker"])
                self.assertGreaterEqual(row["execution_started_at"], started)
                self.assertEqual(Path(row["repo"]), repo)
                self.assertIn(row["result"], Path(row["result_path"]).read_text())
                self.assertEqual(row["result_sha256"], hashlib.sha256(row["result"].encode()).hexdigest())
                self.assertEqual(row["acceptance"]["result_sha256"], row["result_sha256"])
                self.assertTrue(row["evidence"]["files"])
                self.assertIsNone(row["branch"])
                self.assertIsNone(row["worktree"])
            chat.reconcile_tasks()
            chat.reconcile_tasks()
            messages = chat.history(cid)["messages"]
            saved = next(message for message in messages if message["id"] == receipt["id"])
            self.assertEqual(saved["content"], receipt["content"])
            self.assertEqual(saved["task_ids"], receipt["task_ids"])
            completed = [message for message in messages if message.get("task_status")]
            self.assertEqual(len(completed), 1)
            self.assertIn("Worker assignments completed: 2/2", completed[0]["content"])
            for identifier in receipt["task_ids"]:
                self.assertIn(identifier, completed[0]["content"])

    def test_both_workers_execute_real_claims_with_repo_evidence_and_reports(self):
        repo = self.repo()
        source = queue.enqueue(self.root, "conversation", "source", "Improve Web Bob", kind="coding")
        claim = queue.claim(self.root, "codex")
        queue.transition(self.root, source["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running", project="Web Bob", worktree=str(repo))
        for lane, task, kind in (("gemini", "Audit the whole repo", "audit"),
                                 ("minimax", "Write test cases for queue recovery", "test-writing")):
            with self.subTest(lane=lane):
                job = queue.enqueue(self.root, "conversation", lane, task, worker=lane, source_job_id=source["id"])
                self.assertEqual(job["kind"], kind)
                row = queue.claim(self.root, lane)
                def response(model, prompt, **kwargs):
                    self.assertEqual(model, lane)
                    self.assertEqual(kwargs["model_id"], MODELS[lane])
                    self.assertIn("FILE queue.py", prompt)
                    self.assertNotIn("fixture-must-not-be-sent", prompt)
                    self.assertNotIn("fixture-password-never-read", prompt)
                    self.assertNotIn("FILE linked.py", prompt)
                    current = queue.tasks(self.root)[job["id"]]
                    self.assertEqual(current["model"], MODELS[lane])
                    self.assertEqual(current["status"], "running")
                    return draft_report("queue.py")
                with patch.object(api, "model_call", side_effect=response), \
                        patch.object(dispatch_worker, "prepare_task") as prepare, \
                        patch.object(dispatch_worker.draft_publish, "publish") as publish:
                    self.assertEqual(dispatch_worker.execute(job["id"], row["claim_token"]), 0)
                prepare.assert_not_called()
                publish.assert_not_called()
                result = queue.tasks(self.root)[job["id"]]
                self.assertEqual(result["status"], "succeeded")
                report = Path(result["result_path"])
                self.assertTrue(report.is_relative_to(self.core / "reviews"))
                self.assertIn("```diff", report.read_text())
                self.assertEqual((repo / "queue.py").read_text(), "def enqueue(job):\n    return job\n")
                self.assertIsNone(result["branch"])
                self.assertIsNone(result["worktree"])
                self.assertTrue(result["evidence"]["files"])

    def test_1035_failed_report_is_never_published_by_either_api_worker(self):
        self.failed_completion_report("1035")

    def test_1107_failed_report_is_never_published_by_either_api_worker(self):
