    def test_runner_dispatches_on_every_cycle_despite_legacy_cadence(self):
        state.write_json(self.core / "dispatch-schedule.json", {"next_legacy_tick": time.time() + 60})
        with patch.object(web_dispatch, "tick") as tick, patch.object(runtime, "run") as run:
            runtime.runner_cycle()
            runtime.runner_cycle()
            self.assertEqual(tick.call_count, 2)
            run.assert_not_called()

    def test_bootstrap_transient_validation_cannot_restore_old_scheduler(self):
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("dispatch_bootstrap", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "bootstrap.py")
        bootstrap = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(bootstrap)
        with patch.object(bootstrap, "HERE", self.core), patch.object(bootstrap, "validate", side_effect=[["services/dispatch-worker.sh"], []]), patch.object(bootstrap, "files", return_value=[]), patch.object(bootstrap, "bounded") as commands:
            bootstrap.gate()
            commands.assert_not_called()

    def test_bootstrap_refuses_to_discard_local_changes_on_persistent_failure(self):
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("dispatch_bootstrap", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "bootstrap.py")
        bootstrap = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(bootstrap)
        with patch.object(bootstrap, "HERE", self.core), patch.object(bootstrap, "validate", return_value=["services/dispatch-worker.sh"]), patch.object(bootstrap, "bounded", return_value=(1, b"", b"")) as commands:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "without rollback"):
                bootstrap.gate()
            self.assertEqual(commands.call_count, 1)
            self.assertIn("diff", commands.call_args.args[0])

    @unittest.skipUnless(sys.platform == "darwin", "macOS sandbox boundary")
    def test_analysis_sandbox_reads_files_but_denies_writes_without_nested_sandbox(self):
        workspace = self.root / "workspace"
        workspace.mkdir()
        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", str(workspace)], check=True)
        evidence = workspace / "evidence.txt"
        evidence.write_text("actual source evidence")
        argv = workspaces.sandbox_command([sys.executable, "-c", "from pathlib import Path; print(Path('evidence.txt').read_text())"], workspace, read_only=True)
        result = command.run(argv, cwd=workspace, capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
        self.assertIn("actual source evidence", result.stdout)
        argv = workspaces.sandbox_command([sys.executable, "-c", "from pathlib import Path; Path('evidence.txt').write_text('overwrite')"], workspace, read_only=True)
        result = command.run(argv, cwd=workspace, capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        self.assertEqual(evidence.read_text(), "actual source evidence")

    def test_codex_uses_one_outer_boundary_for_analysis(self):
        response = '{"item":{"type":"agent_message","text":"Reviewed the actual file."}}'
        with patch.object(lanes, "run", return_value=SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout=response, stderr="")) as run, patch.object(lanes, "sandbox_command", side_effect=lambda argv, cwd, **kw: argv) as boundary, patch.object(lanes.budget, "record_cli"):
            lanes.cli("codex", "Review evidence", self.root, False, 30)
        self.assertTrue(boundary.call_args.kwargs["read_only"])
        self.assertNotIn("-s", run.call_args.args[0])
        self.assertIn("--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox", run.call_args.args[0])

    def test_pinned_webchat_launcher_keeps_existing_data_and_loads_keys(self):
        folder = self.core / "webchat"
        folder.mkdir()
        (self.core / "state").mkdir()
        deployment = self.root / "tested-code"
        (self.core / "state/dispatch-code-root").write_text(str(deployment) + "\n")
        (self.core / "keys.env").write_text("GEMINI_API_KEY=private-test-value\n")
        source = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "webchat/run.sh").read_text()
        probe = self.root / "probe.py"
        probe.write_text("import os,sys,pathlib\nroot=pathlib.Path(os.environ['BOB_ROOT'])\nassert pathlib.Path(os.environ['BOB_WEBCHAT_DIR']) == root/'conductor/webchat'\nassert sys.argv[1] == str(root/'tested-code/webchat/app.py')\nassert os.environ['GEMINI_API_KEY']=='private-test-value'\n(root/'pinned-ok').touch()\n")
        source = source.replace('python3 -u', sys.executable + ' "' + str(probe) + '"')
        runner = folder / "run.sh"
        runner.write_text(source)
        result = subprocess.run(["bash", str(runner)], env=dict(os.environ, BOB_ROOT=str(self.root)), capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "pinned-ok").exists())
        self.assertNotIn("private-test-value", result.stdout + result.stderr)

    def test_worker_launcher_sources_rotated_keys_without_printing_them(self):
        # Use a fake python on the normal inherited PATH; the script's fixed
        # prefixes may contain python3, so replace only the executable in a copy.
        script = Path(__file__).with_name("dispatch-worker.sh").read_text()
        probe = self.root / "probe.py"
        probe.write_text("import os,pathlib\nassert os.environ['GEMINI_API_KEY']=='private-test-value'\npathlib.Path(os.environ['BOB_ROOT'],'credential-loaded').touch()\n")
        script = script.replace('python3 "$BOB_CODE_ROOT/services/dispatch_worker.py"', sys.executable + ' "' + str(probe) + '"')
        runner = self.root / "runner.sh"
        runner.write_text(script)
        (self.core / "keys.env").write_text("GEMINI_API_KEY='private-test-value'\n")
        result = subprocess.run(["bash", str(runner), "task", "claim"], env=dict(os.environ, BOB_ROOT=str(self.root), GEMINI_API_KEY="stale"), capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "credential-loaded").exists())
        self.assertNotIn("private-test-value", result.stdout + result.stderr)

    def test_claim_allows_concurrent_gemini_tasks_but_serializes_codex(self):
        for worker in ("gemini", "minimax", "codex", "cursor", "claude", "mini"):
            for index in range(4):
                self.enqueue(worker + str(index), worker=worker)
            claims = [queue.claim(self.root, worker) for _ in range(4)]
            self.assertEqual(sum(row is not None for row in claims), queue.capacity(worker))

    def test_auto_analysis_task_prefers_claude_when_multiple_lanes_ready(self):
        self.enqueue()
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value={lane: {"state": "ready"} for lane in queue.WORKERS}), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch") as launch:
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_args.args[0]["worker"], "claude")

    def test_every_provider_failure_routes_to_an_untried_compatible_worker(self):
        health = {lane: {"state": "ready"} for lane in queue.WORKERS}
        for worker in queue.MODEL_WORKERS:
            with self.subTest(worker=worker):
                row = self.enqueue(worker, worker=worker)
                claim = queue.claim(self.root, worker, now=100)
                queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "retry", now=101, error="Provider failed")
                with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch"):
                    web_dispatch.tick()
                result = queue.tasks(self.root)[row["id"]]
                self.assertNotEqual(result["worker"], worker)
                self.assertIn(worker, result["tried_workers"])
                queue.transition(self.root, result["id"], result["claim_token"], "succeeded", result="Recovered")

    def test_budget_warning_runs_and_exhausted_lane_shifts_to_headroom(self):
        self.enqueue(worker="gemini")
        health = {"gemini": {"state": "budget_warning"}, "minimax": {"state": "ready"}}
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch") as launch:
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_args.args[0]["worker"], "gemini")
        row = next(iter(queue.tasks(self.root).values()))
        queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], row["claim_token"], "succeeded", result="done")
        self.enqueue("next", worker="gemini")
        health["gemini"]["state"] = "budget_exhausted"
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch") as launch:
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_args.args[0]["worker"], "minimax")

    def test_retry_waits_for_untried_busy_worker_without_spending_attempt(self):
        row = self.enqueue(worker="gemini")
        claim = queue.claim(self.root, "gemini", now=100)
        queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "retry", now=101, error="Provider failed")
        health = {"gemini": {"state": "ready"}, "minimax": {"state": "busy"}}
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch") as launch:
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 0)
            self.assertEqual(queue.tasks(self.root)[row["id"]]["attempts"], 1)
            health["minimax"]["state"] = "ready"
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_args.args[0]["worker"], "minimax")

    def test_group_membership_and_replayed_metadata_are_immutable(self):
        def enqueue(request, index, size=2):
            return queue.enqueue(self.root, "conversation", request, "Independent evidence", worker="gemini",
                                 group_id="group", group_size=size, group_index=index)
        first = enqueue("first", 0)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            enqueue("first", 0, 3)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            enqueue("second", 0)
        self.assertIsNone(queue.claim(self.root, "gemini"))
        enqueue("second", 1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(queue.claim(self.root, "gemini"))
        rows = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        rows[1]["group_index"] = rows[0]["group_index"]
        self.assertFalse(queue.group_complete(rows))

    def test_health_capacity_cannot_raise_the_configured_ceiling(self):
        for index in range(4):
            self.enqueue(str(index), worker="gemini")
        claims = [queue.claim(self.root, "gemini", limit=99) for _ in range(4)]
        self.assertEqual(sum(row is not None for row in claims), 3)

    def test_api_slots_allow_three_real_locks_and_refill_after_release(self):
        import contextlib
        with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
            for _ in range(3):
                stack.enter_context(lanes.slot("gemini"))
            with self.assertRaises(BlockingIOError):
                with lanes.slot("gemini"):
                    pass
        with lanes.slot("gemini"):
            pass

    def test_group_executes_all_five_providers_concurrently_and_refills(self):
        for index, worker in enumerate(queue.MODEL_WORKERS):
            queue.enqueue(self.root, "conversation", worker, "Independent supplied evidence for " + worker,
                          worker=worker, group_id="group", group_size=5, group_index=index)
        health = {lane: {"state": "ready"} for lane in queue.WORKERS}
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch"):
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 5)
        claims = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        barrier = threading.Barrier(5)
        def answer(worker, *_args, **_kwargs):
            barrier.wait(timeout=5)
            return worker + " supplied a completed independent result."
        with patch.object(dispatch_worker, "prepare_task", return_value=self.root), patch.object(dispatch_worker, "ask", side_effect=answer):
            with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=5) as pool:
                result = list(pool.map(lambda row: dispatch_worker.execute(row["id"], row["claim_token"]), claims))
        self.assertEqual(result, [0] * 5)
        rows = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        self.assertLess(max(r["execution_started_at"] for r in rows), min(r["finished_at"] for r in rows))
        self.enqueue("refill", worker="codex")
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch") as launch:
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_args.args[0]["request_id"], "refill")

    def test_coding_failover_never_uses_an_analysis_or_local_worker(self):
        row = self.enqueue(worker="codex", kind="coding")
        health = {lane: {"state": "ready"} for lane in queue.WORKERS}
        health["codex"]["state"] = "budget_exhausted"
        with patch.object(lanes, "refresh", return_value=health), patch.object(web_dispatch, "launch"):
            self.assertEqual(web_dispatch.tick(), 1)
        self.assertEqual(queue.tasks(self.root)[row["id"]]["worker"], "cursor")

    def test_mini_worker_runs_local_test_suite_and_reports_result(self):
        suite = self.root / "services"
        suite.mkdir()
        (suite / "test_cpu.py").write_text("import unittest\nclass CPU(unittest.TestCase):\n def test_sum(self):\n  self.assertEqual(sum(range(100)),4950)\n")
        row = queue.enqueue(self.root, "conversation", "mini", "Run services/test_cpu.py", worker="mini")
        claim = queue.claim(self.root, "mini")
        with patch.object(mini_worker, "CODE_ROOT", self.root), patch.object(dispatch_worker, "ask") as ask:
            self.assertEqual(dispatch_worker.execute(row["id"], claim["claim_token"]), 0)
            ask.assert_not_called()
        result = queue.tasks(self.root)[row["id"]]
        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "succeeded")
        self.assertIn("Ran 1 test", result["result"])
        self.assertIn("OK", result["result"])


FILE services/test_draft_publish.py
"""Exercise the server's exact-ref push against an isolated bare Git remote."""
import subprocess
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import command
import draft_publish


class DraftPublication(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_project_pr_is_created_once_as_draft_and_never_undrafted(self):
        sha, branch = "a" * 40, "draft/webchat-" + "a" * 32
        record = dict(url="https://github.com/owner/repo/pull/1", isDraft=True, headRefOid=sha)
        row = dict(id=branch.removeprefix("draft/"), publish_project="Fantasy Football")
        calls, prs = [], []
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            folder = Path(temporary)
            def fake(argv, **kwargs):
                calls.append(argv)
                if argv[1:3] == ["pr", "create"]:
                    self.assertIn("--draft", argv)
                    body = Path(argv[argv.index("--body-file") + 1]).read_text()
                    self.assertIn("\n\n", body)
                    self.assertIn(row["id"], body)
                    prs.append(record)
                return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, json.dumps(prs), "")
            with patch.object(draft_publish, "run", side_effect=fake):
                for _ in range(2):
                    self.assertEqual(draft_publish.ensure_draft_pr(row, folder, folder, "owner/repo", branch, sha), record["url"])
                record["isDraft"] = False
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "remain a draft"):
                    draft_publish.ensure_draft_pr(row, folder, folder, "owner/repo", branch, sha)
            self.assertEqual(sum(call[1:3] == ["pr", "create"] for call in calls), 1)
            self.assertTrue(all(call[2] in ("list", "create") for call in calls))

    def test_real_push_verifies_remote_sha_and_rejects_wrong_branch(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            repo, remote = root / "task", root / "remote.git"
            row = dict(id="webchat-" + "a" * 32, kind="coding", publish_project="WebJam")
            branch = "draft/" + row["id"]
            def git(*args):
                return subprocess.check_output(["git", *map(str, args)], text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()
            git("init", "-b", branch, repo)
            git("init", "--bare", remote)
            (repo / "fix.txt").write_text("verified draft work\n")
            git("-C", repo, "add", "fix.txt")
            git("-C", repo, "-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@localhost", "commit", "-m", "Fix")
            sha = git("-C", repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
            hook = repo / ".git/hooks/pre-push"
            hook.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nexit 1\n")
            hook.chmod(0o700)
            requests = []
            def local_transport(argv, **kwargs):
                requests.append(argv)
                return command.run([str(remote) if arg == "https://github.com/owner/webjam.git" else arg for arg in argv], **kwargs)
            projects = dict(WebJam=dict(remote="owner/webjam"))
            with patch.object(draft_publish, "run", side_effect=local_transport):
                result = draft_publish.publish(row, repo, root / "job", projects)
            self.assertEqual(result["sha"], sha)
            self.assertEqual(git("--git-dir", remote, "rev-parse", "refs/heads/" + branch), sha)
            self.assertEqual(git("--git-dir", remote, "for-each-ref", "--format=%(refname)"), "refs/heads/" + branch)
            self.assertEqual(hook.read_text(), "#!/bin/sh\nexit 1\n")
            self.assertIn(sha + ":refs/heads/" + branch, requests[0])
            self.assertNotIn("--force", requests[0])
            git("-C", repo, "checkout", "-b", "main")
            with patch.object(draft_publish, "run") as network:
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "assigned, clean branch"):
                    draft_publish.publish(row, repo, root / "job", projects)
                network.assert_not_called()

    def test_no_saved_authorization_never_publishes(self):
        with patch.object(draft_publish, "run") as network:
            self.assertIsNone(draft_publish.publish({}, None, None, {}))
            network.assert_not_called()


FILE services/test_guard_consistency.py
"""Attempt10 controls must agree with the exact guard result before publication."""
import ast
import copy
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import api_worker
import dispatch_worker as dispatch
import result_acceptance as acceptance
import test_dispatch as dispatch_fixture
from test_regression_drafts import DATA as DRAFTS

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DATA = json.loads((REPO / 'services/fixtures/texting_guard_consistency_1803.json').read_text())


def report():
    value = copy.deepcopy(DRAFTS['complete_report'])
    value['regression_tests'] = api_worker.completion_guard_examples()
    return value


def draft(case):
    return '\n'.join(label + ': ' + case[key] for label, key in
                     (('Regression test', 'name'), ('Target', 'target'), ('Setup', 'setup'),
                      ('Action', 'action'), ('Expected', 'expected')))


class GuardConsistencyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_exact_attempt10_capture_rejects_before_semantic_vote(self):
        for key in ('prompt', 'answer', 'rejected_control'):
            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(DATA[key].encode()).hexdigest(), DATA[key + '_sha256'])
        vote = Mock(return_value='{"accepted":true,"reason":"Looks complete"}')
        with self.assertRaises(acceptance.ResultRejected) as raised:
            acceptance.accept(DATA['prompt'], DATA['answer'], vote, kind=DATA['kind'])
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), DATA['error'])
        vote.assert_not_called()

    def test_captured_gemini_nested_report_without_completion_stays_rejected(self):
        fixture = DATA['probe_control']
        self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(fixture['answer'].encode()).hexdigest(), fixture['answer_sha256'])
        with self.assertRaises(acceptance.ResultRejected) as raised:
            api_worker.render_report(fixture['prompt'], fixture['answer'], fixture['kind'])
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), fixture['error'])

    def test_missing_and_ambiguous_json_require_exact_rejection_text(self):
        for answer in ('Findings only.', '```json\n{}\n```\n```json\n{}\n```'):
            for expected in ('returns None', 'ResultRejected: Missing completion JSON',
                             "ResultRejected('Malformed required completion JSON output block')",
                             "ResultRejected('Missing or ambiguous required completion JSON output block!')",
                             'the guard rejects the report'):
                case = api_worker.completion_guard_examples()[0]
                case['setup'] = "prompt='Return JSON with accepted/reason fields.'; answer=" + repr(answer)
                case['expected'] = expected
                with self.subTest(answer=answer, expected=expected), self.assertRaisesRegex(
                        acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(DATA['error'])):
                    acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))

    def test_exact_error_is_valid_draft_but_inner_answer_still_rejects(self):
        for inner in ('Findings only.', '```json\n{}\n```\n```json\n{}\n```'):
            case = api_worker.completion_guard_examples()[0]
            case['setup'] = "prompt='Return JSON with accepted/reason fields.'; answer=" + repr(inner)
            case['expected'] = "raises ResultRejected(" + repr(DATA['literal_guard_error']) + ")"
            acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))
            with self.assertRaises(acceptance.ResultRejected) as raised:
                acceptance.check_completion_json('Return JSON with accepted/reason fields.', inner)
            self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), DATA['literal_guard_error'])
        # The report's own completion JSON and semantic vote are separate gates.
        raw = report()
        raw['regression_tests'][0] = case
        rendered = api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(raw), DRAFTS['kind'])
        vote = Mock(return_value='{"accepted":true,"reason":"Exact unexecuted guard drafts"}')
        self.assertTrue(acceptance.accept(DRAFTS['prompt'], rendered, vote, kind=DRAFTS['kind'])['accepted'])
        vote.assert_called_once()

    def test_pass_path_and_other_guard_error_classes_are_checked(self):
        case = api_worker.completion_guard_examples()[1]
        acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))
        case['expected'] = "ResultRejected('Missing or ambiguous required completion JSON output block')"
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'returned successfully'):
            acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))
        case.update(action='acceptance.check_deliverables(prompt, answer, kind="analysis")',
                    setup="prompt='Return an actionable plan.'; answer='Findings only.'")
        actual = acceptance.guard_draft_expected('check_deliverables', 'Return an actionable plan.',
                                                'Findings only.', kind='analysis')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'Missing actionable action plan'):
            acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))
        case['expected'] = actual
        acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))

    def test_formatted_outer_labels_cannot_bypass_audit(self):
        case = DATA['rejected_control']
        for body in (case, case.replace('Regression test:', '### Regression test:'),
                     '\n'.join('  ' + line for line in case.splitlines()),
                     re.sub(r'(?m)^(Target|Setup|Action|Expected):', r'**\1:**', case)):
            with self.subTest(body=body), self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected,
                                                              'contradicts literal guard outcome'):
                acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(body)

    def test_renderer_rejects_contradiction_without_repair(self):
        raw = report()
        raw['regression_tests'][0]['expected'] = 'returns None'
        before = copy.deepcopy(raw)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(DATA['error'])):
            api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(raw), DRAFTS['kind'])
        self.assertEqual(raw, before)

    def test_typed_expectations_derive_error_only_when_declared_mode_agrees(self):
        raw = report()
        raw['regression_tests'] = api_worker.completion_guard_examples(structured=True)
        rendered = api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(raw), DRAFTS['kind'])
        self.assertIn("Expected: raises ResultRejected(" + repr(DATA['literal_guard_error']) + ")", rendered)
        self.assertIn('Expected: returns None', rendered)
        acceptance.check_answer(DRAFTS['prompt'], rendered, kind=DRAFTS['kind'])
        for index, wrong in ((0, 'pass'), (1, 'reject')):
            changed = copy.deepcopy(raw)
            changed['regression_tests'][index]['expected']['guard_outcome'] = wrong
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'contradicts literal guard outcome'):
                api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(changed), DRAFTS['kind'])
        for expected in ({}, {'guard_outcome': 'unknown'}, {'guard_outcome': 'reject', 'reason': 'invented'}):
            raw['regression_tests'][0]['expected'] = expected
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'Missing concrete regression'):
                api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(raw), DRAFTS['kind'])

    def test_typed_outcome_does_not_execute_expressions_or_fill_missing_inputs(self):
        raw = report()
        raw['regression_tests'][0]['expected'] = {'guard_outcome': 'reject'}
        raw['regression_tests'][0]['setup'] = "prompt=__import__('os').system('must-never-run'); answer='missing'"
        with patch('os.system') as execute, self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'Unverifiable'):
            api_worker.render_report(DRAFTS['prompt'], json.dumps(raw), DRAFTS['kind'])
        execute.assert_not_called()

    def test_examples_follow_the_live_guard_and_keep_successful_control(self):
        cases = api_worker.completion_guard_examples()
        for case in cases:
            values = {s.targets[0].id: ast.literal_eval(s.value) for s in ast.parse(case['setup']).body}
            actual = acceptance.literal_guard_outcome('check_completion_json', **values)
            if actual is None:
                self.assertEqual(case['expected'], 'returns None')
            else:
                self.assertIn(actual, case['expected'])
            acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(draft(case))
        with patch.object(acceptance, 'check_completion_json', side_effect=acceptance.ResultRejected('changed guard text')):
            self.assertEqual(api_worker.completion_guard_examples()[0]['expected'],
                             "raises ResultRejected('changed guard text')")


class GuardPublicationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.setUp
    tearDown = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.tearDown

    def test_contradiction_never_saves_and_exact_drafts_save_verified_hashes(self):
        self.acceptance.stop()
        for engine in ('gemini', 'minimax', 'claude', 'codex', 'cursor'):
            for valid in (False, True):
                raw = report()
                if valid:
                    raw['regression_tests'] = api_worker.completion_guard_examples(structured=True)
                if not valid:
                    raw['regression_tests'][0]['expected'] = "ResultRejected('Missing JSON output')"
                job = dispatch.task_queue.enqueue(self.root, '1803', engine + str(valid), DRAFTS['prompt'],
                        worker=engine, kind=DRAFTS['kind'] if engine in dispatch.task_queue.API_WORKERS else 'analysis')
                claim = dispatch.task_queue.claim(self.root, engine, identifier=job['id'])
                with patch.object(dispatch, 'prepare_task', return_value=self.root), \
                     patch.object(api_worker, 'evidence', return_value=('Captured evidence', {})), \
                     patch.object(dispatch, 'ask', side_effect=[json.dumps(raw),
                         '{"accepted":true,"reason":"Grounded report"}']) as ask:
                    self.assertEqual(dispatch.execute(job['id'], claim['claim_token']), 0 if valid else 1)
                row = dispatch.task_queue.tasks(self.root)[job['id']]
                saved = self.core / 'state/task-runs' / job['id'] / 'RESULT.md'
                if valid:
                    self.assertTrue(row['acceptance']['accepted'])
                    self.assertEqual(saved.read_text().strip(), row['result'])
                    self.assertEqual(row['result_sha256'], hashlib.sha256(row['result'].encode()).hexdigest())
                    self.assertEqual(ask.call_count, 2)
                else:
                    self.assertEqual(row['error'], DATA['error'])
                    self.assertFalse(saved.exists())
                    self.assertNotIn('result_path', row)
                    self.assertEqual(ask.call_count, 1)


FILE services/test_pr_evidence.py
import json
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

import pr_evidence


class PullRequestEvidence(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.projects = {"WebJam": {"remote": "owner/webjam"}, "AdoptIQ": {"remote": "owner/adoptiq"}}
        self.data = dict(number=161, title="Fix", url="https://github.com/owner/webjam/pull/161",
                         headRefOid="a" * 40, baseRefOid="b" * 40, files=[dict(path="src/app.py")])

    def test_bare_review_resolves_repository_and_supplies_the_actual_diff(self):
        diff = "diff --git a/src/app.py b/src/app.py\n-old\n+new\n"
        with patch.object(pr_evidence, "gh", side_effect=[json.dumps(self.data), None, diff, json.dumps(self.data)]):
            evidence = pr_evidence.load("review PR 161", self.projects)
        self.assertIn(diff, evidence["text"])
        self.assertEqual(evidence["metadata"]["headRefOid"], "a" * 40)
        self.assertEqual(evidence["metadata"]["repo"], "owner/webjam")

    def test_changed_head_or_ambiguous_repository_fails_closed(self):
        with patch.object(pr_evidence, "gh", return_value=json.dumps(self.data)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "ambiguous"):
                pr_evidence.load("review PR #161", self.projects)
        after = dict(self.data, headRefOid="c" * 40)
        with patch.object(pr_evidence, "gh", side_effect=[json.dumps(self.data), "diff", json.dumps(after)]):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "changed"):
                pr_evidence.load("review WebJam PR #161", self.projects)

    def test_non_review_never_reads_github(self):
        with patch.object(pr_evidence, "gh") as call:
            self.assertIsNone(pr_evidence.load("Improve WebJam", self.projects))
            call.assert_not_called()


FILE services/test_regression_drafts.py
"""Attempt9 regression drafts must be outer deliverables before publication."""
import ast
import copy
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import api_worker
import dispatch_worker as dispatch
import result_acceptance as acceptance
import state
import worker as legacy
import test_dispatch as dispatch_fixture

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DATA = json.loads((REPO / 'services/fixtures/texting_regression_drafts_1658.json').read_text())
ERROR = 'Missing concrete regression test drafts (setup, action and expected assertion required)'


class RegressionDraftTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_historical_probe_control_cannot_claim_success_for_incomplete_inner_report(self):
        data = json.loads((REPO / 'services/fixtures/texting_regression_control_1658.json').read_text())
        self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(data['answer'].encode()).hexdigest(), data['answer_sha256'])
        setup = [line[7:] for line in data['answer'].splitlines() if line.startswith('Setup: ')][1]
        values = {statement.targets[0].id: ast.literal_eval(statement.value)
                  for statement in ast.parse(setup).body}
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(data['expected_inner_error'])):
            acceptance.check_deliverables(**values)
        # The current concrete example instead calls a narrow guard. Its literal
        # failure and successful control have the actual stated outcomes.
        inputs = [{s.targets[0].id: ast.literal_eval(s.value)
                   for s in ast.parse(case['setup']).body} for case in DATA['complete_report']['regression_tests']]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'Missing or ambiguous required completion JSON'):
            acceptance.check_completion_json(**inputs[0])
        acceptance.check_completion_json(**inputs[1])

    def test_known_false_literal_controls_cannot_win_a_positive_vote(self):
        data = json.loads((REPO / 'services/fixtures/texting_regression_control_1658.json').read_text())
        for fixture in (data, data['envelope_confusion']):
            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(fixture['answer'].encode()).hexdigest(), fixture['answer_sha256'])
            vote = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Looks good"}')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'contradicts literal guard outcome'):
                acceptance.accept(fixture['prompt'], fixture['answer'], vote, kind=fixture['kind'])
            vote.assert_not_called()

    def test_literal_guard_audit_never_runs_model_expressions(self):
        malicious = DATA['complete_answer'].replace("prompt='Return JSON with accepted/reason fields.'",
                    "prompt=__import__('os').system('touch /must-never-run')")
        with patch('os.system') as system, self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, 'Unverifiable literal guard draft'):
            acceptance.check_literal_guard_drafts(malicious)
        system.assert_not_called()

    def test_captured_missing_json_separator_is_not_repaired_into_success(self):
        data = json.loads((REPO / 'services/fixtures/texting_regression_control_1658.json').read_text())['malformed_transport']
        self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(data['answer'].encode()).hexdigest(), data['answer_sha256'])
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, data['error']):
            api_worker.render_report(data['prompt'], data['answer'], data['kind'])

    def test_exact_capture_cannot_win_a_positive_vote(self):
        for key in ('prompt', 'answer'):
            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(DATA[key].encode()).hexdigest(), DATA[key + '_sha256'])
        self.assertEqual(DATA['error'], ERROR)
        vote = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Looks complete"}')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
            acceptance.accept(DATA['prompt'], DATA['answer'], vote, kind=DATA['kind'])
        vote.assert_not_called()

    def test_complete_transport_and_markdown_reach_independent_review(self):
        answer = api_worker.render_report(DATA['prompt'], json.dumps(DATA['complete_report']), DATA['kind'])
        self.assertEqual(answer, DATA['complete_answer'])
        for value in (answer, DATA['complete_answer']):
            vote = Mock(return_value='{"accepted": true, "reason": "Grounded two-case draft and conditional plan"}')
            self.assertTrue(acceptance.accept(DATA['prompt'], value, vote, kind=DATA['kind'])['accepted'])
            vote.assert_called_once()

    def test_every_outer_field_and_two_cases_are_required(self):
        for index in (0, 1):
            for field in ('name', 'target', 'setup', 'action', 'expected'):
                for value in ('', None, {}, []):
                    report = copy.deepcopy(DATA['complete_report'])
                    report['regression_tests'][index][field] = value
                    with self.subTest(index=index, field=field, value=value), self.assertRaisesRegex(
                            acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
                        api_worker.render_report(DATA['prompt'], json.dumps(report), DATA['kind'])
        for cases in ([], DATA['complete_report']['regression_tests'][:1], DATA['complete_report']['regression_tests'] * 2):
            report = dict(DATA['complete_report'], regression_tests=cases)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
                api_worker.render_report(DATA['prompt'], json.dumps(report), DATA['kind'])
        for field in ('Target', 'Setup', 'Action', 'Expected'):
            # One valid case cannot cover for its malformed partner.
            for index in (0, 1):
                parts = DATA['complete_answer'].split('Regression test: ')
                parts[index + 1] = re.sub(r'(?m)^' + field + r': .*$', field + ':\nOnly a following-line value', parts[index + 1])
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
                    acceptance.check_answer(DATA['prompt'], 'Regression test: '.join(parts), kind=DATA['kind'])

    def test_nested_and_historical_labels_never_supply_outer_drafts(self):
        plan = DATA['complete_answer'].split('Unblock plan:')[1]
        history = DATA['complete_answer'].split('Unblock plan:')[0]
        for nested in ('```text\n' + history + '```\n',
                       '\n'.join('> ' + line for line in history.splitlines()) + '\n',
                       '\n'.join('    ' + line for line in history.splitlines()) + '\n',
                       'Setup: answer=' + json.dumps(history) + '\n'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
                acceptance.check_answer(DATA['prompt'], nested + 'Unblock plan:' + plan, kind=DATA['kind'])
        report = copy.deepcopy(DATA['complete_report'])
        report['regression_tests'][0]['setup'] += '\n' + history
        report['regression_tests'][0]['action'] = ''
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(acceptance.ResultRejected, re.escape(ERROR)):
            api_worker.render_report(DATA['prompt'], json.dumps(report), DATA['kind'])

    def test_plain_test_writing_uses_transport_without_inventing_a_plan(self):
        report = dict(DATA['complete_report'], plan=None)
        prompt = 'Draft regression tests for source selection.'
        answer = api_worker.render_report(prompt, json.dumps(report), 'test-writing')
        acceptance.check_answer(prompt, answer, kind='test-writing')
        self.assertNotIn('Unblock plan:', answer)

    def test_worker_gets_one_consistent_output_contract(self):
        prompt = api_worker.report_prompt(DATA['prompt'], 'FILE example.py\npass', DATA['kind'])
        self.assertIn('REPORT SERIALIZATION:', prompt)
        self.assertIn('"regression_tests":', prompt)
        self.assertNotIn('Required behavioral-draft layout', prompt)
        self.assertNotIn('exactly one fenced json block', prompt)


class WorkerRegressionDraftTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.setUp
    tearDown = dispatch_fixture.DispatchTests.tearDown

    def test_all_dispatch_workers_reject_missing_drafts_and_save_complete_reports(self):
        self.acceptance.stop()
        for engine in ('gemini', 'minimax', 'claude', 'codex', 'cursor'):
            for valid in (False, True):
                kind = DATA['kind'] if engine in dispatch.task_queue.API_WORKERS else 'analysis'
                job = dispatch.task_queue.enqueue(self.root, '1658', engine + str(valid), DATA['prompt'], worker=engine, kind=kind)
                claim = dispatch.task_queue.claim(self.root, engine, identifier=job['id'])
                raw = json.dumps(DATA['complete_report']) if valid else DATA['answer']
                with patch.object(dispatch, 'prepare_task', return_value=self.root), \
                     patch.object(api_worker, 'evidence', return_value=('Captured evidence', {})), \
                     patch.object(dispatch, 'ask', side_effect=[raw, '{"accepted": true, "reason": "Grounded report"}']) as ask:
                    self.assertEqual(dispatch.execute(job['id'], claim['claim_token']), 0 if valid else 1)
                self.assertIn('REPORT SERIALIZATION:', ask.call_args_list[0].args[1])
                self.assertEqual(ask.call_count, 2 if valid else 1)
                row = dispatch.task_queue.tasks(self.root)[job['id']]
                saved = self.core / 'state/task-runs' / job['id'] / 'RESULT.md'
                if valid:
                    self.assertTrue(row['acceptance']['accepted'])
                    self.assertEqual(row['result'], DATA['complete_answer'])
                    self.assertEqual(row['result_sha256'], hashlib.sha256(row['result'].encode()).hexdigest())
                    self.assertEqual(saved.read_text().strip(), row['result'])
                    if engine in dispatch.task_queue.API_WORKERS:
                        self.assertEqual(Path(row['result_path']).read_text().strip(), row['result'])
                else:
                    self.assertEqual(row['error'], ERROR)
                    self.assertFalse(saved.exists())
                    self.assertNotIn('result_path', row)

    def test_legacy_workers_cannot_verify_or_save_nested_only_drafts(self):
        for engine in ('gemini', 'minimax', 'claude', 'codex', 'cursor'):
            folder = self.root / engine
            folder.mkdir()
            (folder / 'PROMPT.txt').write_text(DATA['prompt'])
            state.job_event(engine, 'launched', job_dir=str(folder), repo=str(self.root), worktree=str(folder),
                            branch='draft/test', engine=engine, kind='non-coding')
            with patch.object(legacy, 'CONDUCTOR', self.core), \
                 patch.object(legacy, 'setup_record', return_value=folder), \
                 patch.object(api_worker, 'evidence', return_value=('Captured evidence', {})), \
                 patch.object(legacy, 'ask', return_value=DATA['answer']) as ask, \
                 patch.object(api_worker, 'save_report') as save:
                self.assertEqual(legacy.execute(engine), 1)
            self.assertEqual(ask.call_count, 1)
            save.assert_not_called()
            self.assertFalse(state.jobs()[engine].get('verified'))
            self.assertFalse((folder / 'RESULT.txt').exists())


FILE services/test_services.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test recovery, isolation, spending and process death without live model calls."""
import concurrent.futures
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace as NS
from unittest.mock import patch
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(HERE))
import api,budget,command,jobs,lanes,runtime,state,worker,workspaces


class ServicesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='harden-test-')
        self.root=Path(self.tmp.name);self.core=self.root/'conductor';self.core.mkdir()
        self.patches=[]
        for module in (api,budget,jobs,lanes,runtime,state,worker,workspaces):
            for name,value in [('ROOT',self.root),('CONDUCTOR',self.core)]:
                if hasattr(module,name):self.patches.append(patch.object(module,name,value))
        self.patches += [patch.object(runtime,'HEARTBEATS',self.core/'heartbeats'),patch.object(runtime,'SERVICES',self.core/'services'),patch.dict(os.environ,{'BOB_ROOT':str(self.root)})]
        for p in self.patches:p.start()
    def tearDown(self):
        for p in reversed(self.patches):p.stop()
        self.tmp.cleanup()
    def folder(self):
        p=self.root/'job';p.mkdir();return p
    def recover(self):
        with patch.object(runtime,'screen_sessions',return_value={}):jobs.recover()
    def test_journal_tail_repair_preserves_blocked_job(self):
        state.job_event('a','running',attempts=1)
        with (self.core/'jobs.jsonl').open('ab') as f:f.write(b'{partial')
        state.job_event('a','blocked',outcome='blocked')
        self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['a']['attempts'],1)
        self.assertTrue((self.core/'jobs.jsonl.interrupted-tail').exists())
    def test_middle_corruption_is_visible(self):
        state.job_event('a','running');p=self.core/'jobs.jsonl';p.write_text('bad\n'+p.read_text())
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):state.jobs()
    def test_terminal_results_cannot_reopen_but_legacy_blockers_can(self):
        state.job_event('a','harvested',outcome='result')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):state.job_event('a','running')
        state.job_event('b','harvested',outcome='blocked');jobs.import_legacy()
        self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['b']['state'],'blocked')
    def test_blocker_is_never_harvested_or_closed(self):
        folder=self.folder();(folder/'BLOCKER.txt').write_text('unfinished')
        state.job_event('a','running',job_dir=str(folder));self.recover()
        self.assertIsNone(jobs.closeout(folder));self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['a']['state'],'blocked')
        self.assertGreater(state.jobs()['a']['retry_at'],time.time())
    def test_legacy_orphan_is_retained_without_replay(self):
        state.write_json(self.root/'local-cursor-job-watch-state.json',{'jobs':{'lost':{'running':True}}})
        jobs.import_legacy();self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['lost']['state'],'blocked')
        self.assertTrue((self.root/'lost/ORPHAN_WARNING.txt').exists())
    def test_three_failures_escalate(self):
        state.job_event('a','running',attempts=3);jobs.blocked(state.jobs()['a'],'failure')
        self.assertTrue(state.jobs()['a']['escalated'])
        self.assertIn('job-a',state.read_json(self.core/'alerts.json'))
    def test_verified_result_harvest_is_idempotent(self):
        folder=self.folder();(folder/'RESULT.txt').write_text('evidence')
        state.job_event('a','running',job_dir=str(folder),managed=True,verified=True)
        self.recover();self.recover();self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['a']['state'],'harvested')
        self.assertEqual(len(list((self.root/'next-prompts/_inbox').glob('*'))),1)
    def test_unverified_result_stays_unfinished(self):
        folder=self.folder();(folder/'RESULT.txt').write_text('claim')
        state.job_event('a','running',job_dir=str(folder),managed=True);self.recover()
        self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['a']['state'],'blocked')
    def test_live_writer_is_not_harvested(self):
        folder=self.folder();(folder/'RESULT.txt').write_text('still writing')
        state.job_event('a','running',job_dir=str(folder))
        with patch.object(runtime,'screen_sessions',return_value={'a':'1.a'}):jobs.recover()
        self.assertEqual(state.jobs()['a']['state'],'running')
    def test_alert_only_updates_json(self):
        (self.core/'STATUS.md').write_text('runtime owns this');state.alert('a','failure')
        self.assertEqual((self.core/'STATUS.md').read_text(),'runtime owns this')
    def test_runtime_renders_notes_and_login_state(self):
        (self.core/'status').mkdir();(self.core/'status/notes-cursor.md').write_text('real observation')

    def test_concurrent_budget_reservations_cannot_cross_cap(self):
        def reserve(_):
            try:budget.reserve('minimax','x',1_000_000);return True
            except budget.BudgetExceeded:return False
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=10) as pool:results=list(pool.map(reserve,range(50)))
        self.assertLess(sum(results),50);self.assertLessEqual(budget.totals('minimax')['committed_usd'],48)
    def test_response_usage_settles_unknown_retains_reservation(self):
        row=budget.reserve('minimax','x',1000);budget.settle('minimax',row,{'usage':{'prompt_tokens':100,'completion_tokens':200}})
        self.assertAlmostEqual(budget.totals('minimax')['spent_usd'],.00027)
        row=budget.reserve('minimax','x',1000);budget.settle('minimax',row,{})
        self.assertAlmostEqual(budget.totals('minimax')['reserved_usd'],row['reserved_usd'])
    def test_import_spend_once(self):

        budget.import_ux_ledger();budget.import_ux_ledger();self.assertEqual(budget.totals('minimax')['spent_usd'],2)
    def test_cap_stops_transport(self):
        state._append(budget.path('minimax'),{'call_id':'old','cost_usd':48})
        with patch.object(api,'run') as transport:
            with self.assertRaises(budget.BudgetExceeded):api.http_json('https://example.invalid',lane='minimax',prompt='x',max_tokens=1)
        transport.assert_not_called()
    def test_http_secret_file_and_retry(self):
        calls=[]
        def transport(cmd,**kw):
            config=Path(cmd[cmd.index('--config')+1]);self.assertIn('fixture-secret',config.read_text())
            self.assertEqual(config.stat().st_mode & 0o777,0o600);self.assertNotIn('fixture-secret',' '.join(cmd));self.assertNotIn('input',kw)
            calls.append(cmd);Path(cmd[cmd.index('-o')+1]).write_text('{"ok":true}')
            return NS(returncode=0,stdout='503' if len(calls)==1 else '200')
        with patch.object(api,'run',side_effect=transport),patch.object(api.time,'sleep'):
            self.assertTrue(api.http_json('https://example.invalid',headers={'Authorization':'fixture-secret'})['ok'])
        self.assertEqual(len(calls),2)

    def test_watchdog_caps_restarts(self):
        now=time.time();runtime.HEARTBEATS.mkdir();sessions={'svc-'+n:'1.svc-'+n for n in runtime.INTERVALS}
        for n in runtime.INTERVALS:(runtime.HEARTBEATS/n).touch()
        state.write_json(self.core/'supervisor.json',{n:{'ever_started':True,'started_at':now} for n in runtime.INTERVALS})

    def test_timeout_kills_grandchild(self):
        path=self.root/'child.pid';child="import os,signal,time;signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,signal.SIG_IGN);open(%r,'w').write(str(os.getpid()));time.sleep(60)"%str(path)
        parent="import subprocess,sys,time;subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-c',%r]);time.sleep(60)"%child
        start=time.monotonic()
        with self.assertRaises(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):command.run([sys.executable,'-c',parent],timeout=.5,capture_output=True)
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic()-start,3)
        probe=command.run(['ps','-p',path.read_text(),'-o','stat='],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
        self.assertTrue(not probe.stdout.strip() or probe.stdout.strip().startswith('Z'),probe.stdout)
    def test_devnull_and_core_write_boundary(self):

        self.assertEqual(result.stdout.strip(),"''")
        target=self.core/'PROMPT.md';target.write_text('protected')
        argv=[sys.executable,'-c',"from pathlib import Path;Path(%r).write_text('bad')"%str(target)]
        result=command.run(workspaces.sandbox_command(argv,self.root),capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode,0);self.assertEqual(target.read_text(),'protected')

        workspaces.git(self.core,'add','services/example.py')

        self.assertTrue(list((self.core/'reviews/quarantine').rglob('ROLLBACK.txt')))

    def test_coding_boundary_allows_real_commit_and_normal_push(self):
        repo=self.root/'repo';repo.mkdir();remote=self.root/'remote.git'
        command.run(['git','init','--bare',str(remote)],capture_output=True,timeout=10,check=True)
        workspaces.git(repo,'init','-b','draft/fixture');workspaces.git(repo,'config','user.name','Fixture');workspaces.git(repo,'config','user.email','fixture@localhost')
        workspaces.git(repo,'remote','add','origin',str(remote))
        (repo/'proof.txt').write_text('verified commit/push boundary\n')
        for args in [['add','proof.txt'],['commit','-m','fixture'],['push','origin','HEAD']]:
            command.run(workspaces.sandbox_command(['git','-C',str(repo),*args],repo),capture_output=True,timeout=10,check=True)
        self.assertEqual(workspaces.git(repo,'rev-parse','HEAD'),workspaces.git(remote,'rev-parse','refs/heads/draft/fixture'))

    def test_claude_is_never_selected_for_coding_or_general_jobs(self):
        health={n:{'state':'ready'} for n in lanes.LANES}
        self.assertFalse(jobs.can_assign({'kind':'coding','project':'WebJam'},'claude',[],health))
