    def test_claude_short_prompt_and_durable_rate_limit_backoff(self):
        with patch.object(lanes,'analysis_workspace',return_value=self.root),patch.object(lanes,'cli',return_value='A short review with evidence') as cli:
            lanes.ask('claude','x'*20000,purpose='review',timeout=500)
        self.assertLess(len(cli.call_args.args[1]),7000);self.assertEqual(cli.call_args.args[4],120)
        result=NS(returncode=1,stdout='{"is_error":true,"result":"rate_limit_error"}',stderr='')
        with patch.object(lanes,'run',return_value=result),patch.object(lanes,'sandbox_command',side_effect=lambda cmd,cwd,**kwargs:cmd):
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):lanes.cli('claude','review',self.root,False,120)
        with patch.object(lanes,'cli') as cli:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'cooldown'):lanes.ask('claude','review',purpose='review')
        cli.assert_not_called()

    def test_claude_session_limit_on_stdout_preserves_the_reset_reason(self):
        result=NS(returncode=1,stdout=json.dumps(dict(is_error=True,result="You've hit your session limit · resets 6:20pm (America/Chicago)")),stderr='')
        with patch.object(lanes,'run',return_value=result),patch.object(lanes,'sandbox_command',side_effect=lambda cmd,cwd,**kwargs:cmd):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'session limit.*6:20pm'):
                lanes.cli('claude','review',self.root,False,120)
        self.assertIn('6:20pm', state.read_json(self.core / 'lane-cooldowns.json')['claude']['detail'])

    def test_live_dashboard_snapshot_fields(self):
        import live_dashboard
        jobs_map = {"job-a": {"job_id": "job-a", "state": "running", "launched_at": time.time() - 120,
                               "engine": "cursor", "job_dir": str(self.root / "job-a")}}
        ops = live_dashboard.build_live_ops(sessions={"job-a": "1.job-a"}, jobs_map=jobs_map,
                                            lanes={"cursor": {"state": "busy"}}, spend={"minimax": {"committed_usd": 1.5}},
                                            now=time.time())
        self.assertIn("running_jobs", ops)
        self.assertEqual(ops["minimax_spend"]["cap_usd"], 59.0)
        self.assertTrue(ops["running_jobs"][0]["plain_now"])
        self.assertIn("scores", ops)
        self.assertIn("roles", ops)
        self.assertIn("now_line", ops)
        self.assertIn("webchat_round", ops)
        job = ops["running_jobs"][0]
        self.assertIn("log_tail", job)
        self.assertIn("last_commit", job)
        self.assertIn("stall_state", job)
        self.assertIn("events", job)
        self.assertTrue(any(e.get("kind") == "status" for e in job["events"]))
        self.assertIn("dashboard_freshness_seconds", ops)

    def test_alignment_events_append_and_tail(self):
        ledger_dir = str(HERE.parent / "state")
        if ledger_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, ledger_dir)
        import alignment_events

        seq = alignment_events.append_event(self.root, actor="test", kind="chat-turn", summary="hello alignment")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(seq, 1)
        rows = alignment_events.read_events(self.root, since_seq=seq - 1)
        self.assertTrue(any(r.get("summary") == "hello alignment" for r in rows))
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "conductor/state/events.jsonl").exists())

    def test_state_ledger_catchup_and_now(self):
        ledger_dir = str(HERE.parent / "state")
        if ledger_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, ledger_dir)
        import ledger as state_ledger

        state_ledger.write_now(self.root, "Round 8 alignment pass", sha="deadbeef")
        state_ledger.append_inbox(self.root, "re-read INBOX between steps", source="test")
        state_ledger.write_catchup(self.root)
        catchup = (self.root / "conductor/state/BOB-CATCHUP.md").read_text()
        self.assertIn("INBOX", catchup)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "conductor/state/NOW.md").exists())
        loaded = state_ledger.load_state(self.root)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(int(loaded.get("revision") or 0), 2)

    def test_state_ledger_mutate_under_lock(self):
        ledger_dir = str(HERE.parent / "state")
        if ledger_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, ledger_dir)
        import ledger as state_ledger

        rev0 = int(state_ledger.load_state(self.root).get("revision") or 0)
        state_ledger.record_webchat_turn(self.root, ask="hello", provider="state")
        state_ledger.add_open_ask(self.root, "ship tab polish")
        rev1 = int(state_ledger.load_state(self.root).get("revision") or 0)
        self.assertGreater(rev1, rev0)

    def test_scores_table_uses_highest_round_not_mtime(self):
        import live_dashboard
        with patch.object(live_dashboard, "CONDUCTOR", self.core):
            reviews = self.core / "reviews"
            reviews.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            old = reviews / "WEBCHAT-READY-R5-RESULT.md"
            new = reviews / "WEBCHAT-READY-R11-RESULT.md"
            old.write_text("| Area | R4 | **R5** |\n| Reliability | 9 | **9.5** |\n")
            new.write_text("| Area | R10 | **R11** |\n| Reliability | 9.8 | **9.9** |\n")
            os.utime(old, (time.time() + 60, time.time() + 60))
            rows = live_dashboard._scores_table()
        self.assertTrue(rows)
        self.assertIn("9.9", rows[0]["score"])

    def test_alignment_events_paginated_read(self):
        ledger_dir = str(HERE.parent / "state")
        if ledger_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, ledger_dir)
        import alignment_events

        for index in range(1, 95):
            alignment_events.append_event(self.root, actor="test", kind="tick", summary=f"tick {index}")
        page = alignment_events.read_events_since_paginated(self.root, since_seq=0, page_size=40, max_pages=3)
        self.assertEqual(len(page), 94)

    def test_alignment_collect_events_truncation(self):
        ledger_dir = str(HERE.parent / "state")
        if ledger_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, ledger_dir)
        import alignment_events

        for index in range(1, 250):
            alignment_events.append_event(self.root, actor="test", kind="chat-turn", summary=f"msg {index}")
        batch = alignment_events.collect_events_since(self.root, since_seq=0, page_size=40, max_pages=6)
        self.assertEqual(len(batch["events"]), 240)
        self.assertTrue(batch["truncated"])
        rest = alignment_events.collect_events_since(self.root, since_seq=batch["high_seq"], page_size=40, max_pages=6)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(rest["events"]), 9)
        self.assertFalse(rest["truncated"])

    def test_public_live_ops_allowlists_log_tail(self):
        import live_dashboard
        payload = live_dashboard.build_live_ops(sessions={}, jobs_map={}, lanes={}, spend={}, now=time.time())
        payload["running_jobs"] = [{
            "engine": "cursor", "stall_state": "running", "elapsed_seconds": 10,
            "plain_now": "PASS tests 46", "log_tail": ["[secret removed]", "Round 6 handback drill OK"],
            "last_commit": {"sha": "abc1234", "message": "fix webchat panel"},
            "round": 6, "live_screen": True, "started_at": "2026-09-27T00:00:00+00:00",
        }]
        payload["dashboard_freshness_seconds"] = 42
        payload["roles"] = {"building": "cursor", "reviewing": "gemini"}
        public = live_dashboard.public_live_ops(payload)
        self.assertEqual(public.get("dashboard_freshness_seconds"), 42)
        self.assertEqual(public.get("service_screen_count"), 0)
        self.assertEqual(public.get("roles", {}).get("building"), "cursor")
        tail = public["running_jobs"][0]["log_tail"]
        self.assertEqual(len(tail), 1)
        self.assertIn("handback", tail[0].lower())
        events = public["running_jobs"][0]["events"]
        self.assertTrue(any(e.get("kind") == "log" for e in events))
        self.assertTrue(any(e.get("kind") == "commit" for e in events))


    def test_public_live_ops_screen_count_contract(self):
        import json

        import live_dashboard

        payload = {
            "service_screens": ["svc-a", "svc-b", "[secret removed]"],
            "service_screen_count": "3",
            "running_jobs": [],
        }
        public = live_dashboard.public_live_ops(payload)
        self.assertEqual(public.get("service_screen_count"), 3)
        self.assertNotIn("service_screens", public)
        blob = json.dumps(public)
        self.assertNotIn("ghp_", blob)
        bad = live_dashboard.public_live_ops({"service_screen_count": -5, "service_screens": ["x"]})
        self.assertEqual(bad.get("service_screen_count"), 0)
        typed = live_dashboard.public_live_ops({"service_screen_count": True, "service_screens": ["a", "b"]})
        self.assertEqual(typed.get("service_screen_count"), 2)

    def test_compact_handback_digest(self):
        webchat = HERE.parent / "webchat"
        sys.path.insert(0, str(webchat))
        from handback import append_turn, compact_digest, refresh_summary
        folder = self.core / "handback"
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (folder / "BOB-STATE.md").write_text("# state\nRunning: nightly review\n")
        append_turn(self.root, ask="status?", provider="gemini", jobs=[], files=[], commits=["deadbeef"],
                    confirmations=[], refusals=[], open_items=[])
        refresh_summary(self.root)
        digest = compact_digest(self.root)
        self.assertIn("nightly", digest.lower())
        self.assertIn("status", digest.lower())

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main(verbosity=2)


FILE webchat/test_api_call.py
"""API child process must honor separate deployed code and live data roots."""
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


class APIRootTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_pinned_checkout_imports_own_services_and_loads_live_credentials(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            base = Path(directory)
            code, data = base / "workspaces/tested-code", base / "live-data"
            (code / "webchat").mkdir(parents=True)
            (code / "services").mkdir()
            (data / "conductor").mkdir(parents=True)
            for name in ("api_call.py", "billing.py"):
                shutil.copyfile(Path(__file__).with_name(name), code / "webchat" / name)
            (code / "services/budget.py").write_text("CAP_USD = 59.0\ndef check(lane): pass\n")
            (code / "services/lanes.py").write_text("from contextlib import nullcontext\ndef slot(lane): return nullcontext()\n")
            (code / "services/api.py").write_text(
                "import os\n"
                "def model_call(lane, prompt, purpose):\n"
                "    assert os.environ[lane.upper() + '_API_KEY'] == 'offline-fixture-key'\n"
                "    assert prompt == 'fixture prompt' and purpose == 'webchat'\n"
                "    return '<bob-webchat-reply>Pinned API helper worked.</bob-webchat-reply>'\n")
            (data / "conductor/keys.env").write_text(
                "MINIMAX_API_KEY=offline-fixture-key\nGEMINI_API_KEY=offline-fixture-key\n")
            env = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if not key.endswith(("_API_KEY", "_TOKEN"))}
            env["BOB_ROOT"] = str(data)
            for lane in ("minimax", "gemini"):
                with self.subTest(lane=lane):
                    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(code / "webchat/api_call.py"), lane],
                                            cwd=base, env=env, input="fixture prompt", text=True,
                                            capture_output=True, timeout=10)
                    self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
                    self.assertEqual(result.stdout.strip(), "Pinned API helper worked.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/test_api_workers.py
"""The reported Jeff turn must cause assignments, never a model acknowledgement."""
import json
import uuid
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat
from task_dispatch import task_queue as queue
from test_webbob_replyq_1856 import SessionFixture
from worker_assignment import requested_workers
from reply_authority import enforce, operational_reply
from jobs_view import build_jobs_view

REQUEST = "remember you can use Gemini and MiniMax for the ongoing jobs"


class APIAssignmentTests(SessionFixture):
    def source(self, project="Web Bob"):
        return queue.enqueue(self.root, self.identifier, str(uuid.uuid4()),
                             "Improve " + project + " queue recovery", kind="coding", worker="codex", title=project)

    def test_real_incident_assigns_both_and_restart_does_not_duplicate(self):
        source = self.source()
        request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, request)
        message = value["messages"][-1]
        rows = [queue.tasks(self.root)[key] for key in message["task_ids"]]
        self.assertEqual([r["requested_worker"] for r in rows], ["gemini", "minimax"])
        self.assertEqual([r["kind"] for r in rows], ["audit", "test-writing"])
        for row in rows:
            self.assertEqual(row["source_job_id"], source["id"])
            self.assertIn(row["id"], message["content"])
            self.assertIn(row["title"], message["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("ready", message["content"].lower())
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, request)
        replacement = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        try:
            replacement.reconcile_tasks()
            self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 3)
        finally:
            replacement.close()

    def test_single_worker_names_the_actual_task_in_queued_and_started_receipts(self):
        self.source()
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Use MiniMax for current work", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        row = queue.tasks(self.root)[value["messages"][-1]["task_id"]]
        self.assertIn("test-case drafts", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        claim = queue.claim(self.root, "minimax")
        queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running")
        self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
        reply = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["content"]
        self.assertEqual(reply, value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertIn(row["id"], reply)
        self.assertIn(row["title"], reply)

    def test_assignments_deliver_one_attributed_result_without_reassigning_coder(self):
        source = self.source()
        coder = queue.claim(self.root, "codex")
        queue.transition(self.root, coder["id"], coder["claim_token"], "running")
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        for lane in ("gemini", "minimax"):
            row = queue.claim(self.root, lane)
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], row["claim_token"], "running")
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], row["claim_token"], "succeeded", result=lane + " specific report")
        for _ in range(2):
            self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
        messages = [m for m in self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"] if m.get("task_status")]
        self.assertEqual(len(messages), 1)
        self.assertIn("Worker assignments completed: 2/2", messages[0]["content"])
        self.assertIn("gemini specific report", messages[0]["content"])
        self.assertIn("minimax specific report", messages[0]["content"])
        self.assertEqual(queue.tasks(self.root)[source["id"]]["claim_token"], coder["claim_token"])

    def test_unrelated_hold_does_not_block_an_allowed_source_project(self):
        self.source("WebJam")
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Hold off on Web Bob", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertEqual(len(value["messages"][-1]["task_ids"]), 2)
        self.assertIn("WebJam", value["messages"][-1]["content"])

    def test_honest_no_jobs_hold_maintenance_and_missing_key_outcomes(self):
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertIn("couldn't assign", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.source()
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Hold off on Web Bob", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertIn("current holds", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "The hold is lifted", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        (self.root / "conductor/MAINTENANCE").touch()
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertIn("maintenance", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        (self.root / "conductor/MAINTENANCE").unlink()
        (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps({"minimax": {"state": "needs_key"}}))
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertIn("needs key", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 1)
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_status_and_jobs_show_exact_api_model_and_task(self):
        self.source()
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, REQUEST, str(uuid.uuid4()))
        processes = {}
        for index, (lane, model) in enumerate((("gemini", "gemini-2.5-pro"), ("minimax", "MiniMax-M2.7"))):
            claim = queue.claim(self.root, lane)
            pid = 7200 + index
            queue.transition(self.root, claim["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running", pid=pid)
            queue.transition(self.root, claim["id"], claim["claim_token"], "executing", model=model)
            processes[pid] = dict(pid=pid, ppid=1, command="python3 /code/dispatch_worker.py " + claim["id"],
                                  process_state="S", elapsed_seconds=12)
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
            status = operational_reply(self.root, "server", "status with diagnostics")
            jobs = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        for model in ("gemini-2.5-pro", "MiniMax-M2.7"):
            self.assertIn(model, status)
            self.assertIn(model, json.dumps(jobs["working"]))
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs["working"]), 2)
        self.assertIn("Whole-repo audit", status)
        self.assertIn("test-case drafts", status)

    def test_forbidden_provider_and_acknowledgement_fallback(self):
        self.source()
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Use Gemini and Grok for ongoing jobs", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.assertIn("never Grok", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 1)
        for reply in ("Got it! Gemini and MiniMax are ready", "I've assigned both to the jobs."):
            guarded = enforce(self.root, REQUEST, dict(content=reply, provider="gemini"))
            self.assertIn("haven't assigned", guarded)
        for text in ("Are Gemini and MiniMax useful for ongoing jobs?", "Don't use Gemini for ongoing work",
                     "Remember Gemini pricing", "Use Gemini to research queues"):
            self.assertIsNone(requested_workers(text))
        for text in ("Remember to use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing work", "Remember, we can use Gemini and MiniMax"):
            self.assertEqual(requested_workers(text), ["gemini", "minimax"])

    def test_natural_task_types_queue_on_requested_api_workers(self):
        for text, kind in (("Use Gemini to audit the whole repo", "audit"),
                           ("Use Gemini to plan Web Bob improvements", "planning"),
                           ("Use Gemini to research durable queue designs", "research"),
                           ("Use MiniMax to write test cases for queue recovery", "test-writing"),
                           ("Use MiniMax to grade eval results for Web Bob", "eval-grading")):
            with self.subTest(text=text):
                value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, text, str(uuid.uuid4()))
                row = queue.tasks(self.root)[value["messages"][-1]["task_id"]]
                self.assertEqual(row["kind"], kind)

    def test_multiple_project_api_reports_do_not_get_coding_instructions(self):
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Use Gemini to research Web Bob and WebJam", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        rows = [queue.tasks(self.root)[key] for key in value["messages"][-1]["task_ids"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 2)
        for row in rows:
            self.assertEqual(row["kind"], "research")
            self.assertIn("reviews/", row["prompt"])
            self.assertNotIn("assigned worktree", row["prompt"])


FILE webchat/test_build_info.py
"""Exercise deployed code with a different, deliberately stale data directory."""
import json
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
import uuid
from types import SimpleNamespace
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Server
import build_info
from build_info import BUILD, CODE_ROOT, STATIC, git_checkout_root, load_build, VERSION_QUESTION
from live_redeploy_check import Client
from test_webchat import Fixture
from live_status_snapshot import _screen_sessions


class CheckoutIdentity(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name).resolve()
        self.repo = self.root / "repo"
        module = self.repo / "webchat/build_info.py"
        module.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        module.write_text("# Tracked fixture identity module.\n")
        self.git("init", "-b", "main")
        self.git("add", ".")
        self.git("-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@localhost", "commit", "-m", "Fixture")

    def git(self, *args):
        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(self.repo), *args], text=True,
                                       stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).strip()

    def test_running_identity_module_is_tracked_in_this_checkout(self):
        running = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
        self.assertEqual(Path(build_info.__file__).resolve(), running / "webchat/build_info.py")
        self.assertEqual(CODE_ROOT, running)
        actual = Path(subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(running), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
                                             text=True).strip()).resolve()
        self.assertEqual(git_checkout_root(), actual)
        self.assertEqual(actual, running)
        self.assertEqual(subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(running), "cat-file", "-t",
                                                  "HEAD:webchat/build_info.py"], text=True).strip(), "blob")

    def test_normal_and_linked_worktrees_have_real_git_identity(self):
        self.assertTrue((self.repo / ".git").is_dir())
        self.assertEqual(git_checkout_root(self.repo), self.repo)
        linked = self.root / "linked"
        self.git("worktree", "add", "--detach", str(linked), "HEAD")
        self.assertTrue((linked / ".git").is_file())
        self.assertEqual(git_checkout_root(linked), linked)
        self.assertEqual(load_build(linked)["sha"], self.git("rev-parse", "HEAD"))

    def test_nested_code_must_be_committed_to_use_the_parent_git_identity(self):
        nested = self.repo / "apps/webbob"
        module = nested / "webchat/build_info.py"
        module.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        module.write_text("# Nested identity fixture.\n")
        self.assertIsNone(git_checkout_root(nested))
        self.assertEqual(load_build(nested)["sha"], "unknown")
        self.git("add", ".")
        self.assertIsNone(git_checkout_root(nested))
        self.git("-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@localhost", "commit", "-m", "Nested code")
        self.assertEqual(git_checkout_root(nested), self.repo)
        self.assertEqual(load_build(nested)["sha"], self.git("rev-parse", "HEAD"))

    def test_git_failures_return_no_checkout(self):
        for error in (OSError("git unavailable"), subprocess.TimeoutExpired("git", 5),
                      subprocess.CalledProcessError(128, "git")):
            with self.subTest(error=type(error).__name__), patch("build_info.subprocess.check_output", side_effect=error):
                self.assertIsNone(git_checkout_root(self.repo))

    def test_inherited_git_overrides_do_not_choose_the_repository(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"GIT_DIR": str(self.root / "wrong-git"), "GIT_WORK_TREE": str(self.root)}):
            self.assertEqual(git_checkout_root(self.repo), self.repo)

    def test_deleted_or_linked_identity_module_cannot_supply_context(self):
        module = self.repo / "webchat/build_info.py"
        module.unlink()
        self.assertIsNone(git_checkout_root(self.repo))
        other = self.root / "external.py"
        other.write_text("# Not the running checkout's module.\n")
        module.symlink_to(other)
        self.assertIsNone(git_checkout_root(self.repo))


class ReleaseIdentity(Fixture):
    def test_single_screen_and_temporarily_incomplete_screen_listing(self):
        complete = SimpleNamespace(stdout="123.lane-only (Detached)\n1 Socket in /tmp/screens.\n", stderr="")
        partial = SimpleNamespace(stdout="123.lane-only (Unreachable)\n1 Socket in /tmp/screens.\n", stderr="")
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.subprocess.run", side_effect=[partial, complete]):
            self.assertEqual(_screen_sessions(self.root), {"lane-only": "123.lane-only"})
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.subprocess.run", return_value=partial):
            self.assertIsNone(_screen_sessions(self.root))

    def test_assets_and_header_come_from_code_not_persistent_data(self):
        stale = self.folder / "static"
        stale.mkdir()
        for name in ("app.js", "style.css", "index.html", "login.html"):
            (stale / name).write_text("OLD UI")
        server = Server(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.chat)
        thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        client = Client(f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}", self.folder / ".password")
        try:
            with patch("app.HERE", self.folder):
                self.assertEqual(client.call("/api/version"), BUILD)
                client.login()
                with client.opener.open(client.url + "/") as response:
                    index = response.read().decode()
                    self.assertIn("no-cache", response.headers["Cache-Control"])
                    self.assertIn("no-store", response.headers["Cache-Control"])
                for name in ("app.js", "style.css", "manifest.webmanifest"):
                    url = "/" + name + "?v=" + BUILD["short_sha"]
                    self.assertIn(url, index)
                    with client.opener.open(client.url + url) as response:
                        self.assertEqual(response.read(), (STATIC / name).read_bytes())
                self.assertIn('id="build-info"', index)
                self.assertIn(BUILD["built_at"], index)
                self.assertNotIn("__WEBBOB_", index)
                self.assertNotIn("OLD UI", index)
        finally:
            server.shutdown()
            server.server_close()
            thread.join(3)

    def test_version_reply_is_only_own_sha_despite_other_project_history_and_hold(self):
        (self.root / "BOB-STATE.md").write_text("WebJam build deadbee. Web Bob is old1234.")
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "hold off", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        for prompt in ("what version/build are you", "What build are you running?", "What is your build number?",
                       "What version are you?", "What SHA are you running?"):
            reply = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, prompt, str(uuid.uuid4()))
            self.assertEqual(reply["messages"][-1]["content"], BUILD["short_sha"])
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.assertFalse(VERSION_QUESTION.search("What version is WebJam?"))

    def test_archive_identity_uses_release_manifest_and_never_parent_git(self):
        self.assertEqual(load_build(self.root)["sha"], "unknown")
        manifest = dict(sha="a" * 40, built_at="2026-09-27T22:00:00+00:00")
        (self.root / "webbob-build.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest))
        self.assertEqual(load_build(self.root)["short_sha"], "a" * 7)
        self.assertEqual(load_build(self.root)["built_at"], manifest["built_at"])


FILE webchat/test_chat_intent.py
"""Regressions for Jeff's multi-project push and stale hold incident."""
import json
import uuid
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat
from chat_intent import projects
from providers import Providers
from reply_authority import enforce
from task_dispatch import task_queue as queue
from test_webchat import Fixture

PRIORITY = "Ok prioritize making Web Bob better, then WebJam, then AdoptIQ, then Storyboard."
PUSH = "Now push everything to the max and fix anything you find along the way."
HOLD = ("Hold off on any coding jobs or service restarts for Web Bob. Bob has Codex fixing job pickup right now, "
        "and two fixes at once will clash. You can keep answering questions and reporting status, "
        "but don't queue, start, or restart anything for Web Bob until I say go.")
NAMES = ["Web Bob", "WebJam", "AdoptIQ", "StoryBoard"]


class IntentTests(Fixture):
    def test_literal_project_push_saves_scoped_publication_authority(self):
        receipt = self.submit("push WebJam and AdoptIQ")["messages"][-1]
        rows = [queue.tasks(self.root)[identifier] for identifier in receipt["task_ids"]]
        self.assertEqual({row["publish_project"] for row in rows}, {"WebJam", "AdoptIQ"})
        single = self.submit("push WebJam")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(queue.tasks(self.root)[single["task_id"]]["publish_project"], "WebJam")
        local = self.submit("Improve WebJam")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertIsNone(queue.tasks(self.root)[local["task_id"]]["publish_project"])

    def submit(self, text, request_id=None, **kwargs):
        value = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, text, request_id or str(uuid.uuid4()), **kwargs)
        self.wait()
        return self.chat.history(self.identifier) if self.identifier in self.chat.conversations else value

    def reload(self):
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)

    def ready(self, *lanes):
        (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps({lane: dict(state="ready", active=0) for lane in lanes}))

    def test_exact_live_push_creates_two_jobs_and_releases_only_named_holds(self):
        self.ready("codex", "cursor")
        self.submit("hold")
        request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        receipt = self.submit("push WebJam and AdoptIQ", request)["messages"][-1]
        rows = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        self.assertEqual([row["title"] for row in rows], ["WebJam", "AdoptIQ"])
        self.assertEqual(set(receipt["task_ids"]), {row["id"] for row in rows})
        self.assertEqual({row["kind"] for row in rows}, {"coding"})
        self.assertNotIn("hold", receipt["content"].lower())
        self.reload()
        self.submit("push WebJam and AdoptIQ", request)
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 2)
        self.assertIn("On hold", self.submit("fix Web Bob login")["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_exact_live_going_question_uses_server_status_without_generation(self):
        answer = self.submit("is it going now?")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(answer["provider"], "state")
        self.assertIn("are idle", answer["content"])
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_incident_two_turn_request_persists_one_real_job_per_project(self):
        self.ready("codex", "cursor", "claude", "gemini", "minimax")
        self.submit(PRIORITY)
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.reload()
        request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        value = self.submit(PUSH, request)
        receipt = value["messages"][-1]
        rows = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        self.assertEqual([row["title"] for row in rows], NAMES)
        self.assertEqual([row["requested_worker"] for row in rows], ["codex", "cursor", "codex", "cursor"])
        self.assertEqual({row["kind"] for row in rows}, {"coding"})
        self.assertEqual(set(receipt["task_ids"]), {row["id"] for row in rows})
        self.assertEqual(len(receipt["content"].splitlines()), 4)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "conductor/state/queue.jsonl").is_file())
        for row in rows:
            self.assertEqual(projects(row["prompt"]), [row["title"]])
            self.assertEqual(row["status"], "queued")
            self.assertIn(row["title"], receipt["content"])
            self.assertIn(row["requested_worker"].title(), receipt["content"])
            self.assertIn(row["id"], receipt["content"])
        self.submit(PUSH, request)
        self.reload()
        self.submit(PUSH, request)
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 4)
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_inline_request_deduplicates_aliases_and_go_uses_saved_priorities(self):

    def test_named_resume_only_accepts_a_matching_saved_task(self):

    def test_analysis_spreads_across_free_compatible_workers_and_real_claims_update_receipt(self):
        self.ready("claude", "gemini", "minimax", "codex", "cursor")
        receipt = self.submit("Compare Web Bob, WebJam, AdoptIQ and StoryBoard using this evidence: each has one queued task.")["messages"][-1]
        rows = [queue.tasks(self.root)[identifier] for identifier in receipt["task_ids"]]
        self.assertEqual(len({row["requested_worker"] for row in rows}), 4)
        for row in rows:

            self.assertIsNotNone(claim)
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running")
        self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
        updated = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(updated["content"], receipt["content"])
        self.assertEqual(updated["action_card"]["state"], "running")
        self.assertTrue(all(task["status"] == "running" for task in updated["action_card"]["tasks"]))

    def test_busy_lane_and_existing_claim_do_not_get_free_worker_preference(self):

    def test_project_batch_partial_enqueue_recovers_frozen_plan_once(self):
        original = queue.enqueue
        calls = []
        def fail_second(*args, **kwargs):
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) == 2:
                raise OSError("interrupted append")
            return original(*args, **kwargs)
        with patch.object(queue, "enqueue", side_effect=fail_second):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                self.submit("Push everything to the max: Web Bob, WebJam, AdoptIQ, StoryBoard")
        rows = list(queue.tasks(self.root).values())
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 1)
        self.assertIsNone(queue.claim(self.root, rows[0]["requested_worker"]))
        self.assertNotIn("task_ids", self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["messages"][-1])
        self.ready("cursor")
        self.reload()
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 4)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["task_ids"]), 4)
        self.assertEqual({row["requested_worker"] for row in queue.tasks(self.root).values()}, {"codex", "cursor"})

    def test_project_batch_initial_save_failure_has_no_queue_effect(self):

        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_hold_is_scoped_durable_and_resume_dispatches_waiting_work_once(self):
        self.submit(HOLD)
        self.assertIn("Web Bob", self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.reload()
        blocked = self.submit("Fix Web Bob login")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertIn("On hold", blocked["content"])
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.submit("Fix WebJam login")
        # Work on another project must not erase the waiting Web Bob request.
        self.assertIn("Web Bob", self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.reload()
        request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        receipt = self.submit("Okay, please resume.", request)["messages"][-1]
        self.assertIn("task_id", receipt)
        self.assertFalse(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.submit("Okay, please resume.", request)
        self.reload()
        self.assertFalse(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 2)
        self.assertNotIn("hold", self.submit("Are we still on hold?")["messages"][-1]["content"].lower())

    def test_go_clears_hold_without_replaying_old_jobs_and_rehold_is_explicit(self):

        self.submit("go ahead and fix Web Bob login")

    def test_push_without_targets_preserves_saved_permissions(self):

        self.assertEqual(row["kind"], "coding")
        self.assertEqual(projects(row["prompt"]), ["Web Bob"])

    def test_new_hold_defers_saved_unqueued_intent_until_resume(self):

                self.submit("Fix Web Bob and WebJam")
        self.submit("hold")
        self.reload()
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.submit("resume")
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 2)
        self.reload()
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 2)

    def test_lift_wording_clears_hold_and_failed_save_cannot_grant_permission(self):
        self.submit("hold")
        with patch.object(self.chat, "save", side_effect=OSError("state unavailable")):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                self.submit("go")
        self.reload()
        self.assertIn("all", self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.submit("Hold is lifted.")
        self.assertFalse(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])

    def test_saved_permissions_override_stale_model_hold_in_prompt_and_reply(self):
        self.submit(HOLD)
        self.submit("go")
        value = self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]
        provider = Providers(self.root, self.chat.redact)
        with patch.object(provider, "snapshot", return_value=[]):
            prompt = provider.prompt(value, fast=True)
        facts = json.loads(prompt.split("SERVER SAVED CHAT PERMISSIONS (override older history and snapshots):\n")[1])
        self.assertEqual(facts["holds"], {})
        with patch.object(self.fake, "reply", return_value=dict(provider="gemini", attempts=[], content=
                "Your earlier hold on Web Bob code and service restarts still applies. I need your permission before continuing.")):
            answer = self.submit("Any concerns?")["messages"][-1]["content"]
        self.assertEqual(answer, "Ready when you are.")
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_reply_cites_only_a_matching_active_hold(self):
        self.submit(HOLD)
        value = self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]
        answer = dict(provider="gemini", content="WebJam is on hold until you say go.")
        self.assertNotIn("hold", enforce(self.root, "What about WebJam?", answer, value))
        content = enforce(self.root, "Is Web Bob on hold?", answer, value)
        self.assertIn("On hold: Web Bob", content)
        self.assertNotIn("WebJam", content)

    def test_pause_and_approval_claims_follow_current_permissions(self):
        claims = ("Web Bob is paused until you say resume.",
                  "Web Bob is paused.", "Coding has been paused.",
                  "I am holding Web Bob pending your approval.",
                  "Web Bob is awaiting your go-ahead.")
        self.submit(HOLD)
        for claim in claims:
            with self.subTest(claim=claim, held=True):
                content = enforce(self.root, "What about Web Bob?", dict(provider="gemini", content=claim),
                                  self.chat.conversations[self.identifier])
                self.assertEqual(content, "On hold: Web Bob. Say go or resume when you're ready.")
        self.submit("go")
        self.reload()
        for claim in claims:
            with self.subTest(claim=claim, held=False), patch.object(self.fake, "reply", return_value=
                    dict(provider="gemini", attempts=[], content="The next issue is the login button. " + claim)):
                content = self.submit("Any concerns?")["messages"][-1]["content"]
                self.assertEqual(content, "The next issue is the login button.")
                self.assertFalse(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_maintenance_and_worker_restrictions_still_apply_to_batches(self):
        for text, worker in (("Fix Web Bob and WebJam", "gemini"), ("Fix Web Bob and WebJam", "grok")):
            receipt = self.submit(text, worker=worker)["messages"][-1]
            self.assertEqual(receipt["action_card"]["state"], "refused")
        (self.root / "conductor/MAINTENANCE").touch()
        receipt = self.submit("Fix Web Bob and WebJam")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(receipt["action_card"]["state"], "refused")
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))


FILE webchat/test_deploy_integrate.py
"""Integration gate fails closed and restores the previous release."""
import contextlib
import io
import json
import os
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from deploy_integrate import latest_sessions, main
from integration_rubric import check_reply, style_errors, sessions
from task_dispatch import task_queue


class IntegrationGate(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_current_capture_matches_without_rewriting_sessions_and_rejects_drift(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            saved_sessions = root / "conductor/webchat/sessions"
            saved_sessions.mkdir(parents=True)
            captured = sessions()
            original = {}
            for source in captured:
                path = saved_sessions / (source["source_id"] + ".json")
                messages = []
                for exchange in source["exchanges"]:
                    messages.extend([dict(role="user", content=exchange["message"]),
                                     dict(role="assistant", content=exchange["before"])])
                path.write_text(json.dumps(dict(messages=messages)))
                original[path] = path.read_bytes()
            self.assertEqual(latest_sessions(root), captured)
            self.assertEqual({p: p.read_bytes() for p in original}, original)
            for path, payload in original.items():
                saved = json.loads(payload)
                messages = saved["messages"]
                for change, changed in (
                    ("appended ask", messages + [dict(role="user", content="Any updates?")]),
                    ("removed ask", messages[2:]),
                    ("rewritten ask", [dict(role="user", content="A different ask"), *messages[1:]]),
                    ("reordered asks", messages[2:] + messages[:2]),
                ):
                    if changed == messages:
                        continue  # A single-ask session has no alternate user order.
                    with self.subTest(session=path.stem, change=change):
                        path.write_text(json.dumps(dict(messages=changed)))
                        before = {p: p.read_bytes() for p in original}
                        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "Original Jeff session changed"):
                            latest_sessions(root)
                        self.assertEqual({p: p.read_bytes() for p in original}, before)
                        path.write_bytes(payload)

    def test_rubric_rejects_all_failed_deployment_promises(self):
        for text in ("I'll make sure they're utilized.", "The system is actively adding work for them.",
                     "I'll focus on getting everything running.", "All LLMs are healthy and ready."):
            self.assertTrue(style_errors(text), text)

    def test_rollback_fences_only_replay_claims_without_deleting_receipts(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            replay = task_queue.enqueue(root, "replay", "request", "audit Web Bob", worker="gemini", kind="audit")
            other = task_queue.enqueue(root, "jeff", "other-request", "audit WebJam", worker="gemini", kind="audit")
            waiting = task_queue.enqueue(root, "replay", "waiting", "Improve WebJam", worker="codex", kind="coding")
            claimed = task_queue.claim(root, "gemini", identifier=replay["id"])
            task_queue.transition(root, replay["id"], claimed["claim_token"], "running")
            cancelled = task_queue.cancel_replay(root, {"replay"}, "Release rollback")
            self.assertEqual({row["id"] for row in cancelled}, {replay["id"], waiting["id"]})
            self.assertIsNone(task_queue.tasks(root)[waiting["id"]]["claimed_at"])
            self.assertIsNone(task_queue.tasks(root)[waiting["id"]]["started_at"])
            self.assertFalse(task_queue.transition(root, replay["id"], claimed["claim_token"], "succeeded", result="too late"))
            self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(root)[other["id"]]["status"], "queued")
            self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(root)[replay["id"]]["created_at"], replay["created_at"])

    def test_wrong_live_version_rolls_back_local_and_public_endpoints(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder, patch.dict(os.environ):
            root = Path(folder)
            candidate, old = root / "candidate", root / "old-code"
            for directory in (root / "conductor/state", root / "conductor/reviews", root / "conductor/webchat",
                              root / "rollbacks", candidate / "webchat/evidence", old):
                directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            (root / "conductor/webchat/.password").write_text("private-test-password")
            (root / "conductor/webchat/.public-origin").write_text("https://public.example")
            (old / "webbob-build.json").write_text('{"sha":"dff84e3"}')
            for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
                (root / "conductor/state" / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(old))
            (candidate / "webchat/evidence/integrate-tests.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(
                sha="candidate-sha", suite_exit=0, eval_exit=0, eval=dict(passed=56, total=56))))
            def command(argv):
                if argv[0] == "gh":
                    return json.dumps(dict(isDraft=True, state="OPEN", headRefOid="candidate-sha", baseRefName="draft/webbob-clean-0927"))
                if "rev-parse" in argv: return "candidate-sha"
                if "branch" in argv: return "draft/webbob-integrate-2057"
                return ""
            versions = iter([dict(sha="dff84e3"), dict(sha="wrong-sha"), dict(sha="dff84e3"), dict(sha="dff84e3")])
            client = MagicMock()
            client.url = "http://127.0.0.1:8787"
            client.call.side_effect = lambda route: next(versions) if route == "/api/version" else {}
            with patch("sys.argv", ["deploy_integrate.py", "--root", str(root), "--candidate", str(candidate), "--draft-pr", "https://example.org/pr"]), \
                    patch("deploy_integrate.run", side_effect=command), \
                    patch("deploy_integrate.Client", return_value=client), \
                    patch("deploy_integrate.latest_sessions", return_value=[]), \
                    patch("deploy_integrate.restart", return_value=dict(other_screens_unchanged=True)) as restart, \
                    contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                self.assertEqual(main(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(restart.call_count, 2)
            report = (root / "conductor/reviews/WEBBOB-INTEGRATE-DEPLOYMENT-0927-attempt1.json").read_text()
            result = json.loads(report)
            self.assertTrue(result["rolled_back"])
            self.assertEqual(result["restored_public_version"]["sha"], "dff84e3")
            self.assertNotIn("private-test-password", report)
            self.assertEqual(set(restart.call_args.args[1]), {"webchat", "dispatch"})


FILE webchat/test_deploy_replyq.py
"""A release must reject weak replies, queued-only work, and wrong build identity."""
import contextlib
import io
import json
import os
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from deploy_replyq import main, style_errors, verify_executions
from task_dispatch import task_queue


class ReplyGateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_style_rejects_original_failures_but_allows_fact_links(self):
        for reply in ("Okay, I'll focus on getting everything running.",
                      "Codex: job webchat-1234567890abcdef.\nlogs/review.log; last: raw text.",
                      "One. Two. Three. Four. Five.", ""):
            self.assertTrue(style_errors(reply), reply)
        self.assertFalse(style_errors("Codex pushed WebJam's draft at [abc1234](https://example.com/draft/webchat-1234567890abcdef)."))

    def test_queue_receipts_cannot_pass_as_execution(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            row = task_queue.enqueue(root, "conversation", "request", "Fix WebJam login", worker="codex", kind="coding")
            rows, started = verify_executions(root, [row["id"]])
            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 1)
            self.assertEqual(started, [])
            claim = task_queue.claim(root, "codex")
            task_queue.transition(root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running")
            self.assertEqual(verify_executions(root, [row["id"]])[1], [])
            task_queue.transition(root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "executing")
            self.assertEqual(len(verify_executions(root, [row["id"]])[1]), 1)

    def test_wrong_live_version_restores_snapshot_and_verifies_old_sha(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder, patch.dict(os.environ):
            root = Path(folder)
            candidate, old = root / "candidate", root / "old-code"
            for directory in (root / "conductor/state", root / "conductor/reviews", root / "conductor/webchat",
                              root / "rollbacks", candidate / "webchat/evidence", candidate / "reviews", old):
                directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            (root / "conductor/webchat/.password").write_text("private-test-password")
            (old / "webbob-build.json").write_text('{"sha":"dff84e3"}')
            for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
                (root / "conductor/state" / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(old))
            (candidate / "webchat/evidence/replyq-tests.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(
                sha="candidate-sha", suite_exit=0, eval_exit=0, eval=dict(passed=33, total=33))))
            def command(argv):
                if argv[0] == "gh":
                    return json.dumps(dict(isDraft=True, state="OPEN", headRefOid="candidate-sha"))
                if "rev-parse" in argv:
                    return "candidate-sha"
                if "branch" in argv:
                    return "draft/webbob-replyq-1856"
                return ""
            versions = iter([dict(sha="dff84e3"), dict(sha="wrong-sha"), dict(sha="dff84e3")])
            client = MagicMock()
            client.url = "http://127.0.0.1:8787"
            client.call.side_effect = lambda route: next(versions) if route == "/api/version" else {}
            with patch("sys.argv", ["deploy_replyq.py", "--root", str(root), "--candidate", str(candidate)]), \
                    patch("deploy_replyq.run", side_effect=command), \
                    patch("deploy_replyq.Client", return_value=client), \
                    patch("deploy_replyq.latest_sessions", return_value=[]), \
                    patch("deploy_replyq.restart", return_value=dict(other_screens_unchanged=True)) as restart, \
                    contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                self.assertEqual(main(), 1)
            self.assertEqual(restart.call_count, 2)
            restored = restart.call_args.args[1]
            self.assertEqual(set(restored), {"webchat", "dispatch"})
            self.assertIn("rollbacks/webbob-replyq-", str(restored["webchat"]))
            self.assertEqual((restored["webchat"] / "webbob-build.json").read_text(), '{"sha":"dff84e3"}')
            report = (root / "conductor/reviews/WEBBOB-REPLYQ-DEPLOYMENT-0927.json").read_text()
            self.assertTrue(json.loads(report)["rolled_back"])
            self.assertNotIn("private-test-password", report)
            self.assertTrue((candidate / "reviews/WEBBOB-REPLYQ-DEPLOY-FAIL-0927.md").exists())


FILE webchat/test_failover.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Failure/breaker tests plus real subprocess and durable-queue regressions."""
import json
import fcntl
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat, RequestError
from failover import AllDown, Circuit, ORDER, ProviderError, classify, reset_time
from providers import Cancelled, Providers, clean_env, parse_codex, parse_cursor, run
from actions import queue_once


class Fixture(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.provider = Providers(self.root, lambda s: s)
        self.turn = {"messages": [{"role": "user", "status": "done", "content": "Debug this connection; remember violet kite.", "request_id": str(uuid.uuid4())}]}
        self.cancel = threading.Event()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.provider.close()
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def reply(self):
        return self.provider.reply(self.turn, self.cancel, lambda *_: None)


class FailoverTests(Fixture):
    def test_all_failure_classes_for_each_provider_and_sanitized_audit(self):
        cases = [(1, "not logged in", "not_logged_in"), (0, "Rate limit 429 retry-after: 40", "rate_limit"),
                 (1, "operation timed out", "timeout"), (19, "fatal process exit", "nonzero_exit"),
                 (0, "empty or garbage answer", "invalid_output"), (1, "connection reset by peer", "network")]
        for name in ORDER:
            for code, detail, kind in cases:
                with self.subTest(provider=name, kind=kind):
                    for row in self.provider.circuit.entries.values():
                        row.update(failures=0, retry_at=0)
                    seen = []
                    def call(provider, *_):
                        seen.append(provider)
                        if provider == name:
                            raise ProviderError(detail + " private-output", code=code)
                        if ORDER.index(provider) < ORDER.index(name):
                            raise ProviderError(kind="busy")
                        return "Next provider answered.", None
                    with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=call):
                        if name == ORDER[-1]:
                            with self.assertRaises(AllDown) as error:
                                self.reply()
                            attempts = error.exception.attempts
                        else:
                            answer = self.reply()
                            attempts = answer["attempts"]
                            self.assertEqual(answer["provider"], ORDER[ORDER.index(name) + 1])
                    self.assertEqual(next(a for a in attempts if a["provider"] == name)["kind"], kind)
                    self.assertNotIn("claude", seen)
        data = self.provider.circuit.log_path.read_text()
        self.assertNotIn("private-output", data)
        self.assertNotIn("violet kite", data)
        self.assertTrue(all(isinstance(json.loads(line), dict) for line in data.splitlines()))
        self.assertEqual(self.provider.circuit.log_path.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)

    def test_breaker_backoff_persistence_half_open_exclusion_and_recovery(self):
        now = [1000]
        c = self.provider.circuit
        c.clock = lambda: now[0]
        for delay in (300, 600, 1200, 1800, 1800):
            self.assertEqual(c.failed("codex", ProviderError(kind="timeout")), now[0] + delay)
            self.assertFalse(c.acquire("codex"))
            now[0] += delay
            self.assertEqual(c.snapshot()["codex"]["state"], "half_open")
            self.assertTrue(c.acquire("codex"))
            self.assertFalse(c.acquire("codex"))
            c.release("codex")
        restored = Circuit(c.path, c.log_path, clock=lambda: now[0])
        self.assertEqual(restored.entries["codex"]["failures"], 5)
        restored.succeeded("codex")
        self.assertEqual(restored.snapshot()["codex"]["state"], "ready")
        self.assertEqual(restored.failed("codex", ProviderError(kind="timeout")), now[0] + 300)

    def test_rate_reset_and_probe_does_not_clear_quota(self):
        now = datetime(2026, 9, 27, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp()
        for detail, delay in [("retry-after: 90", 90), ('"retryDelay": "45s"', 45), ("try again in 2 minutes", 120),
                              ("reset_at: " + str(int(now + 4000)), 4000), ("resets 2026-09-27T01:00:00Z", 3600),
                              ("retry-after: Sun, 27 Sep 2026 01:00:00 GMT", 3600)]:
            self.assertEqual(reset_time(detail, now), now + delay, detail)
        self.assertIsNone(reset_time("quota exhausted", now))
        c = self.provider.circuit
        c.clock = lambda: now
        c.failed("codex", ProviderError(kind="rate_limit", retry_at=now + 7200))
        c.probed("codex")
        self.assertEqual(c.entries["codex"]["retry_at"], now + 7200)
        self.assertEqual(c.snapshot()["codex"]["state"], "cooldown")

    def test_background_probes_do_not_generate_or_invoke_claude(self):
        with patch.object(self.provider, "run", return_value=(0, "configured", "")) as runner:
            self.provider.probe_once()
        self.assertEqual(runner.call_count, 4)
        for args in runner.call_args_list:
            self.assertNotIn("prompt", args.kwargs)
            self.assertNotIn("claude", args.args[0])
        self.assertTrue(all(self.provider.circuit.entries[n]["probed_at"] for n in ORDER))

    def test_mid_answer_failure_discards_partial_and_reuses_exact_context(self):
        prompts = []
        def call(name, prompt, *_):
            prompts.append(prompt)
            if name == "codex":
                return parse_codex('\n'.join(map(json.dumps, [
                    {"type": "item.completed", "item": {"type": "agent_message", "text": "Partial must not appear"}},
                    {"type": "turn.failed", "error": {"message": "network disconnected"}}])))
            return "Full recovered answer", None
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=call):
            answer = self.reply()
        self.assertEqual(answer["content"], "Full recovered answer")
        self.assertEqual(prompts[0].split("\nSERVER FACTS")[0], prompts[1].split("\nSERVER FACTS")[0])
        self.assertIn('"answering_provider": "codex"', prompts[0])
        self.assertIn('"answering_provider": "cursor"', prompts[1])
        self.assertIn('"queue_write_confirmed_this_turn": false', prompts[1])
        self.assertEqual(answer["attempts"][0]["kind"], "network")
        self.assertTrue(answer["attempts"][0]["partial_discarded"])
        self.assertNotIn("Partial must not appear", json.dumps(answer))

    def test_structured_errors_with_zero_exit_and_malformed_output(self):
        for parser, value, kind in [(parse_cursor, '{"is_error":true,"result":"Usage limit reached"}', "rate_limit"),
                                    (parse_codex, '{"type":"error","message":"not logged in"}', "not_logged_in"),
                                    (parse_codex, '{"type":"item.completed","item":42}', "invalid_output"),
                                    (parse_cursor, '{"result":"<html>bad gateway</html>"}', "invalid_output")]:
            with self.assertRaises(ProviderError) as error:
                parser(value)
            self.assertEqual(error.exception.kind, kind)

    def test_all_down_queues_no_fake_answer_and_skips_until_cooldown(self):
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=ProviderError(kind="network")) as call:
            with self.assertRaises(AllDown) as error:
                self.reply()
            self.assertEqual(call.call_count, 4)
            self.assertIn("queued", str(error.exception))
            with self.assertRaises(AllDown):
                self.reply()
            self.assertEqual(call.call_count, 4)

    def test_claude_only_when_selected_and_stop_never_falls_through(self):
        self.turn["preferred_provider"] = "claude"
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("A short critique", None)) as call:
            self.assertEqual(self.reply()["provider"], "claude")
            self.assertEqual(call.call_count, 1)
        self.turn["preferred_provider"] = "auto"
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=Cancelled("Reply stopped")) as call:
            with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
                self.reply()
            self.assertEqual(call.call_count, 1)
            self.assertEqual(self.provider.circuit.entries["codex"]["failures"], 0)

    def test_explicit_provider_is_first_then_falls_back_with_visible_attempts(self):
        for selected in (*ORDER, "claude"):
            with self.subTest(provider=selected):
                self.turn["preferred_provider"] = selected
                with patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("Selected provider answered", None)) as call:
                    self.assertEqual(self.reply()["provider"], selected)
                    self.assertEqual([c.args[0] for c in call.call_args_list], [selected])
                def answer(name, *_):
                    if name == selected:
                        raise ProviderError(kind="network")
                    return "Recovered from the selected provider failure", None
                with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=answer):
                    result = self.reply()
                    self.assertEqual(result["provider"], next(name for name in ORDER if name != selected))
                    self.assertEqual(result["attempts"][0]["provider"], selected)
                for row in self.provider.circuit.entries.values():
                    row.update(failures=0, retry_at=0)

    def test_explicit_claude_preference_falls_back_to_order_on_failure(self):
        self.turn["preferred_provider"] = "claude"
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"BOB_WEBCHAT_FORCE_FAIL": "claude"}), patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("Recovered answer", None)):
            result = self.reply()
        self.assertEqual(result["provider"], ORDER[0])
        self.assertEqual(result["attempts"][0]["provider"], "claude")

    def test_minimax_uses_metered_helper_and_per_provider_timeouts(self):
        with patch.object(self.provider, "run", return_value=(0, "<bob-webchat-reply>Fine</bob-webchat-reply>", "")) as call:
            self.provider.call("minimax", "context", None, self.cancel)
        self.assertTrue(call.call_args.args[0][1].endswith("api_call.py"))
        self.assertIn("minimax", call.call_args.args[0])
        self.assertEqual(call.call_args.kwargs["timeout"], 120)
        self.assertEqual(classify(1, "BudgetExceeded: MiniMax $48 cap"), "budget")
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"BOB_WEBCHAT_TIMEOUT_CODEX": "33", "BOB_WEBCHAT_TIMEOUT_CURSOR": "44"}):
            other = Providers(self.root, lambda s: s)
        self.assertEqual(other.timeouts["codex"], 33)
        self.assertEqual(other.timeouts["cursor"], 44)

    def test_prompt_stdin_reaches_eof(self):
        code, out, _ = run([sys.executable, "-c", "import sys; print(repr(sys.stdin.read()))"], cwd=self.root, env=clean_env(), cancel=self.cancel, prompt="private context", timeout=3)
        self.assertEqual((code, out.strip()), (0, "'private context'"))
        code, out, _ = run([sys.executable, "-c", "import sys; print(repr(sys.stdin.read()))"], cwd=self.root, env=clean_env(), cancel=self.cancel, timeout=3)
        self.assertEqual((code, out.strip()), (0, "''"))

    def test_real_timeout_and_stop_kill_children_including_nested_groups(self):
        for mode in ("timeout", "stop"):
            with self.subTest(mode=mode):
                marker = self.root / (mode + ".json")
                cancel = threading.Event()
                if mode == "stop":
                    threading.Timer(.7, cancel.set).start()
                with self.assertRaises(Cancelled if mode == "stop" else ProviderError):
                    run([sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).with_name("drill_child.py")), str(marker)], cwd=self.root,
                        env=dict(clean_env(), BOB_WEBCHAT_DRILLS="1"), cancel=cancel, timeout=.6 if mode == "timeout" else 5)
                pids = json.loads(marker.read_text())
                for pid in [pids["parent"], *pids["children"]]:
                    result = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-p", str(pid), "-o", "stat="], capture_output=True, text=True)
                    self.assertTrue(not result.stdout.strip() or result.stdout.strip().startswith("Z"), (pid, result.stdout))

    def test_drill_file_requires_startup_opt_in(self):
        path = self.root / "conductor/webchat/.drill.json"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text('{"force_fail":{"codex":"network"}}')
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"BOB_WEBCHAT_DRILLS": "0", "BOB_WEBCHAT_FORCE_FAIL": ""}):
            self.assertEqual(self.provider.drill()["force_fail"], {})

    def test_scoped_drill_does_not_change_real_chat_health(self):
        cid = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.turn["id"] = cid
        path = self.root / "conductor/webchat/.drill.json"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"conversation_ids": [cid], "force_fail": {n: "network" for n in ORDER}}))
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"BOB_WEBCHAT_DRILLS": "1"}), patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("Healthy real chat", None)) as call:
            with self.assertRaises(AllDown):
                self.reply()
            call.assert_not_called()
            self.assertTrue(all(row["failures"] == 0 for row in self.provider.circuit.entries.values()))
            self.turn["id"] = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self.assertEqual(self.reply()["content"], "Healthy real chat")

    def test_api_concurrency_and_single_half_open_probe(self):
        c = self.provider.circuit
        for name in ("gemini", "minimax"):
            self.assertTrue(c.acquire(name))
            self.assertTrue(c.acquire(name))
            c.release(name)
            self.assertEqual(c.snapshot()[name]["state"], "busy")
            c.release(name)
            c.failed(name, ProviderError(kind="network"))
            c.entries[name]["retry_at"] = 0
            self.assertTrue(c.acquire(name))
            self.assertFalse(c.acquire(name))
            c.release(name)

    def test_concurrent_turns_do_not_change_each_others_timeouts(self):
        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
        observed = []
        def call(*_):
            barrier.wait(timeout=3)
            observed.append(self.provider.current_call.timeout)
            return "Answer", None
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=call):
            threads = [threading.Thread(target=self.provider._call_timed, args=("gemini", "prompt", None, self.cancel, duration)) for duration in (7, 29)]
            for thread in threads:
                thread.start()
            for thread in threads:
                thread.join(4)
        self.assertEqual(sorted(observed), [7, 29])
        self.assertEqual(self.provider.timeouts["gemini"], 120)


class QueueTests(Fixture):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.chat = Chat(self.root / "conductor/webchat", self.root, self.provider)
        self.identifier = next(iter(self.chat.conversations))

    def tearDown(self):
        self.chat.close()
        super().tearDown()

    def wait(self):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while self.chat.worker_info(self.identifier) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(.01)
        self.assertFalse(self.chat.worker_info(self.identifier))

    def submit(self, text="What is a safe memory question?", request_id=None):
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, text, request_id or str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.wait()

    def test_queue_persists_and_retries_original_turn_once(self):
        request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=ProviderError(kind="network")):
            self.submit(request_id=request_id)
        value = self.chat.history(self.identifier)
        self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-1]["status"], "waiting")
        self.assertNotIn("retry_prompt", value)
        frozen = self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["retry_prompt"]
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.chat.folder, self.root, self.provider)
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["status"], "waiting")
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "What is a safe memory question?", request_id)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"]), 2)
        with self.assertRaises(RequestError):
            self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "Different turn", str(uuid.uuid4()))
        self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["messages"][-1]["retry_at"] = 0
        for row in self.provider.circuit.entries.values():
            row["retry_at"] = 0
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("Recovered", None)) as call:
            self.chat.retry_queued()
            self.wait()
        self.assertEqual(call.call_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(call.call_args.args[1].split("\nSERVER FACTS")[0], frozen)
        result = self.chat.history(self.identifier)
        self.assertEqual(len(result["messages"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(result["messages"][-1]["content"], "Recovered")
        self.assertEqual(result["messages"][-1]["status"], "done")

    def test_cancel_waiting_request_prevents_replay(self):
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call", side_effect=ProviderError(kind="network")):
            self.submit()
        self.chat.stop(self.identifier)
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call") as call:
            self.chat.retry_queued()
        call.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["status"], "cancelled")

    def test_prohibited_action_is_refused_and_duplicate_card_is_once_only(self):
        request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        with patch.object(self.provider, "call") as call:
            self.submit("merge #158", request_id)
            self.submit("merge #158", request_id)
        call.assert_not_called()
        result = self.chat.history(self.identifier)
        self.assertEqual(len(result["messages"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(result["messages"][-1]["action_card"]["state"], "refused")
        self.assertFalse(result["messages"][-1]["action_card"]["executable"])

    def test_confirmation_receipt_survives_failed_conversation_save(self):
        # A persisted proposal from an earlier server version remains valid.
        self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["pending_job"] = "review PR #161"
        self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["pending_job_id"] = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.chat.save(self.chat.conversations[self.identifier])
        request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        from task_dispatch import task_queue
        original_save = self.chat.save
        calls = []
        def interrupted_save(value):
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) == 2:
                raise OSError("disk full after enqueue")
            original_save(value)
        with patch.object(self.chat, "save", side_effect=interrupted_save):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "yes, start job", request_id)
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, "yes, start job", request_id)
        self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
        self.assertEqual(len(task_queue.tasks(self.root)), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["action_card"]["state"], "queued")

    def test_ambiguous_action_and_crash_after_effect_never_execute_twice(self):
        action_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        with patch("actions.queue_draft", side_effect=OSError("failed after external effect")) as queue:
            self.assertFalse(queue_once(self.root, action_id, "review draft")["ok"])
            self.assertFalse(queue_once(self.root, action_id, "review draft")["ok"])
            self.assertEqual(queue.call_count, 1)
        with patch("actions.queue_draft") as queue:
            self.assertFalse(queue_once(self.root, action_id, "changed task")["ok"])
            queue.assert_not_called()

    def test_handback_failure_preserves_completed_answer(self):
        with patch("app.append_turn", side_effect=OSError("unavailable")), patch.object(self.provider, "call", return_value=("Completed answer", None)):
            self.submit()
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["status"], "done")
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["content"], "Completed answer")

    def test_action_queue_uses_scheduler_lock_before_writing(self):
        from job_queue import queue_draft
        folder = self.root / "conductor/locks"
        folder.mkdir(parents=True)
        with (folder / "queue.lock").open("a") as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            result = queue_draft(self.root, "review a draft")
            self.assertFalse(result["ok"])
            self.assertFalse((self.root / "next-prompts/INDEX.md").exists())
        result = queue_draft(self.root, "review a draft")
        self.assertTrue(result["ok"])
        self.assertIn(result["file"], (self.root / "next-prompts/INDEX.md").read_text())


class BillingTests(Fixture):
    def test_chat_cap_blocks_shared_transport_after_background_cap_increase(self):
        ledger = self.root / "conductor/ledger"
        ledger.mkdir(parents=True)
        (ledger / "minimax.jsonl").write_text(json.dumps({"call_id": "existing", "cost_usd": 59}) + "\n")
        services = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "services"
        script = """
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1])
sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[2])
import api, budget
from billing import enforce_chat_cap
budget.CAP_USD = 96
enforce_chat_cap(budget)
assert budget.CAP_USD == 59
def no_http(*a, **kw):
    raise AssertionError('transport must never run')
api.run = no_http
try:
    api.model_call('minimax', 'test', max_tokens=1)
except budget.BudgetExceeded:
    print('PASS: reservation rejected before transport')
else:
    raise AssertionError('cap bypassed')
"""
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", script, str(services), str(Path(__file__).parent)],
            env=dict(os.environ, BOB_ROOT=str(self.root), MINIMAX_API_KEY="fixture-only"), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
        self.assertIn("reservation rejected", result.stdout)
        self.assertEqual(len((ledger / "minimax.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()), 1)

FILE webchat/test_integrate_2057.py
