class PinnedDeploymentAssignments(SessionFixture):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        from build_info import CODE_ROOT
        self.code_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
        self.assertEqual(CODE_ROOT, self.code_root)
        self.assertEqual(Path(subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "-C", str(self.code_root), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], text=True).strip()).resolve(),
            self.code_root)
        self.pins = self.root / "conductor/state"
        self.pins.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
            (self.pins / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(self.code_root) + "\n")
        # Attempt 0204 had this screen only: no identifiable project lane.
        sampler = patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={
            "lane-integrate-deploy-0204": "53678.lane-integrate-deploy-0204"})
        sampler.start()
        self.addCleanup(sampler.stop)

    def test_attempt2_turn_one_dispatches_from_the_matching_release_pins(self):
        from integration_rubric import check_reply, sessions
        from live_status_snapshot import build_live_status_snapshot
        exchange = sessions()[0]["exchanges"][0]
        before = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(before["queue_counts"]["queued"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(before["queue_counts"]["claimed"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(before["work_counts"]["running"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(before["work"]), 1)
        lane = before["work"][0]
        self.assertEqual(lane["screen"], "lane-integrate-deploy-0204")
        self.assertEqual(lane["state"], "unverified")
        self.assertEqual(lane["source"], "screen")
        for field in ("project", "repo", "task", "job_id", "worker"):
            self.assertIsNone(lane.get(field), field)
        started = time.time()
        answer = self.send(exchange["message"])
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        after = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(check_reply(answer, exchange, self.identifier, started, before, after, records), [])
        self.assertEqual(len(records), 2)
        self.assertEqual(after["work_counts"]["running"], 0)
        for row in records.values():
            self.assertEqual(row["status"], "queued")
            self.assertIsNone(row["source_job_id"])
            self.assertIn("Project: Web Bob", row["prompt"])
            self.assertIn("matching deployment pins", row["prompt"])
            self.assertIn("no current task or worker execution is confirmed", row["prompt"])
            self.assertNotIn("Ongoing task:", row["prompt"])
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        replay = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, exchange["message"], answer["request_id"])["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(replay["task_ids"], answer["task_ids"])
        self.assertEqual(replay["content"], answer["content"])
        self.assertEqual(set(queue.tasks(self.root)), set(records))

    def test_pins_are_context_without_an_open_screen_or_a_claim_of_running_work(self):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}):
            answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
            self.assertEqual(len(answer.get("task_ids", [])), 2)

        self.assertIn("waiting to run", status["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("running for", status["content"])

    def test_missing_empty_relative_mismatched_and_unreadable_pins_fail_closed(self):
        for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
            pin = self.pins / (name + "-code-root")
            for value in (None, "", ".", str(self.root), str(self.root / "missing")):
                with self.subTest(pin=name, value=value):
                    pin.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    if value is not None:
                        pin.write_text(value)
                    answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
                    self.assertFalse(answer.get("task_ids"))
                    self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
                    pin.write_text(str(self.code_root))
            pin.unlink()
            pin.mkdir()
            self.assertIn("couldn't assign", self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])["content"])
            pin.rmdir()
            pin.write_text(str(self.code_root))

    def test_pinned_context_respects_project_global_and_partial_release_holds(self):
        for hold, release, permitted in (
            ("Hold off on Web Bob", None, False),
            ("Hold off", None, False),
            ("Hold off", "Resume WebJam", False),
            ("Hold off", "Resume Web Bob", True),
            ("Hold off on WebJam", None, True),
        ):
            with self.subTest(hold=hold, release=release):
                self.identifier = self.chat.new_conversation()["id"]
                self.send(hold)
                if release:
                    self.send(release)
                before = set(queue.tasks(self.root))
                answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
                added = set(queue.tasks(self.root)) - before
                self.assertEqual(len(added), 2 if permitted else 0)
                if permitted:
                    for key in added:
                        self.assertTrue(queue.tasks(self.root)[key]["prompt"].startswith("Project: Web Bob\n"))
                else:
                    self.assertIn("current holds", answer["content"])

    def test_pins_do_not_authorize_other_projects_or_tool_workers(self):

    def test_parked_project_holds_survive_the_permitted_webbob_dispatch(self):

            self.assertIsNone(row["publish_project"])

    def test_rollback_copy_without_git_evidence_cannot_supply_report_context(self):

        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_git_marker_without_a_repository_cannot_authorize_jobs(self):
        for marker in ("file", "directory"):
            with self.subTest(marker=marker):
                code = self.root / marker
                code.mkdir()
                (code / "webchat").mkdir()
                (code / "webchat/build_info.py").write_text("# Untracked identity fixture.\n")
                if marker == "file":
                    (code / ".git").write_text("not a git checkout\n")
                else:
                    (code / ".git").mkdir()
                for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
                    (self.pins / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(code) + "\n")
                with patch("worker_assignment.CODE_ROOT", code):
                    answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
                self.assertFalse(answer.get("task_ids"))
                self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_nested_tracked_code_uses_its_git_root_with_pins_to_the_running_code(self):
        repo = self.root / "monorepo"
        code = repo / "apps/webbob"
        module = code / "webchat/build_info.py"
        module.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        module.write_text("# Tracked identity module for the nested fixture.\n")
        def git(*args):
            return subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(repo), *args], stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        git("init", "-b", "main")
        git("add", ".")
        git("-c", "user.name=Test", "-c", "user.email=test@localhost", "commit", "-m", "Nested fixture")
        for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
            (self.pins / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(code) + "\n")
        with patch("worker_assignment.CODE_ROOT", code):
            answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        self.assertEqual([records[key]["requested_worker"] for key in answer.get("task_ids", [])],
                         ["gemini", "minimax"])
        self.assertTrue(all("matching deployment pins" in row["prompt"] for row in records.values()))

    def test_absolute_symlink_pins_resolve_to_the_running_checkout(self):
        alias = self.root / "release-link"
        alias.symlink_to(self.code_root, target_is_directory=True)
        for name in ("webchat", "dispatch"):
            (self.pins / (name + "-code-root")).write_text(str(alias) + "\n")

    def test_pinned_context_does_not_displace_a_permitted_ongoing_job(self):
        source = queue.enqueue(self.root, "external", "source", "Improve WebJam recording", kind="coding")
        answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
        rows = queue.tasks(self.root)
        self.assertEqual([rows[key]["source_job_id"] for key in answer["task_ids"]], [source["id"], source["id"]])

    def test_pinned_context_still_respects_maintenance_and_provider_unavailability(self):
        maintenance = self.root / "conductor/MAINTENANCE"
        maintenance.touch()
        self.assertIn("maintenance", self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])["content"])
        maintenance.unlink()
        for state in ("needs_key", "cooldown", "budget_exhausted"):
            with self.subTest(state=state):
                (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps({"minimax": {"state": state}}))
                self.assertIn(state.replace("_", " "), self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])["content"])
                self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))


class IntegratedSessions(SessionFixture):
    def seed(self):
        return queue.enqueue(self.root, "external-project-work", str(uuid.uuid4()),
                             "Improve WebJam recording reliability", kind="coding", worker="codex", title="WebJam")

    def test_all_19_captured_messages_in_conversation_order(self):
        self.seed()
        checked = 0
        for source in SESSIONS:
            self.identifier = self.chat.new_conversation()["id"]
            for index, exchange in enumerate(source["exchanges"]):
                with self.subTest(session=source["source_id"], turn=index + 1, message=exchange["message"]):
                    before = set(queue.tasks(self.root))
                    started = time.time()
                    answer = self.send(exchange["message"])
                    records = queue.tasks(self.root)
                    identifiers = answer.get("task_ids") or ([answer["task_id"]] if answer.get("task_id") else [])
                    self.assertEqual(set(identifiers), set(records) - before)
                    self.assertEqual(receipt_errors(answer, records, self.identifier, answer["request_id"], started), [])
                    self.assertNotRegex(answer["content"], r"(?i)I'll make sure|actively adding work|ready|unknown task")
                    if source["source_id"].startswith("b33fac"):
                        if index in (0, 1, 5):
                            self.assertEqual([records[i]["requested_worker"] for i in identifiers], ["gemini", "minimax"])
                            self.assertEqual([records[i]["kind"] for i in identifiers], ["audit", "test-writing"])
                            for identifier in identifiers:
                                self.assertIn(records[identifier]["title"], answer["content"])
                        elif index == 6:
                            self.assertEqual(len(identifiers), 1)
                            self.assertEqual(records[identifiers[0]]["kind"], "coding")
                        elif index == 7:
                            self.assertEqual(answer["content"], "Hi Jeff!")
                            self.assertFalse(identifiers)
                        else:
                            self.assertEqual(answer["provider"], "state")
                            self.assertFalse(identifiers)
                            for name in ("Codex", "Claude", "Cursor", "Gemini", "MiniMax"):
                                self.assertIn(name, answer["content"])
                            self.assertIn("waiting to run", answer["content"])
                    elif source["source_id"].startswith("8126301c"):
                        if index == 0:
                            self.assertEqual(answer["content"], "Which projects should I work on?")
                        elif index == 5:
                            self.assertEqual([records[i]["title"] for i in identifiers], ["WebJam", "AdoptIQ", "StoryBoard", "Fantasy Football"])
                        elif index == 6:
                            self.assertEqual(answer["content"], "Hi Jeff!")
                            self.assertFalse(identifiers)
                        else:
                            self.assertEqual(answer["provider"], "state")
                    else:
                        self.assertEqual([records[i]["title"] for i in identifiers], ["WebJam", "AdoptIQ"])
                    # An HTTP retry and restart must preserve exact receipt IDs.
                    self.chat.submit(self.identifier, exchange["message"], answer["request_id"])
                    self.assertEqual(set(queue.tasks(self.root)), set(records))
                    checked += 1
        replacement = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        try:
            self.assertEqual(set(queue.tasks(self.root)), set(records))
            self.assertEqual(replacement.get(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["content"], answer["content"])
        finally:
            replacement.close()
        self.assertEqual(checked, 19)

    def test_unsupported_promises_cannot_escape_the_model_boundary(self):
        claims = [
            "I'll make sure they're utilized for ongoing jobs.",
            "Yes, the system is actively adding work for them.",
            "I'll make sure to maximize the use of all available resources.",
            "I assigned a Gemini audit and MiniMax tests.",
            "The workers have started those jobs.",
            "Your tasks were queued and delegated to Gemini.",
            "I have put Gemini and MiniMax to work.",
            "Gemini is now working on it.",
            "I queued job `webchat-" + "a" * 32 + "`.",
        ]
        for claim in claims:
            with self.subTest(claim=claim):
                self.fake.reply_text = claim
                answer = self.send("Thanks Bob")
                self.assertEqual(answer["content"], NO_DISPATCH)
                self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_fake_stale_foreign_and_missing_receipts_fail(self):
        self.seed()
        started = time.time()
        answer = self.send("Use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing jobs")
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        valid = (self.identifier, answer["request_id"], started)
        self.assertFalse(receipt_errors(answer, records, *valid))
        for mutation in ("absent", "old", "foreign", "uncited", "omitted", "invented"):
            rows, reply = copy.deepcopy(records), copy.deepcopy(answer)
            identifier = reply["task_ids"][0]
            if mutation == "absent": rows.pop(identifier)
            if mutation == "old": rows[identifier]["created_at"] = started - 30
            if mutation == "foreign": rows[identifier]["request_id"] = "another-turn:0"
            if mutation == "uncited": reply["content"] = reply["content"].replace(identifier, "")
            if mutation == "omitted": reply["task_ids"].pop()
            if mutation == "invented": reply["content"] += " webchat-" + "0" * 32
            self.assertTrue(receipt_errors(reply, rows, *valid), mutation)

    def test_context_survives_restart_and_a_hold_still_blocks_assignment(self):
        self.seed()
        self.send("Use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing jobs")
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        answer = self.send("Are you going to add work for them to do?")
        self.assertEqual(len(answer["task_ids"]), 2)
        self.send("Hold off")
        before = set(queue.tasks(self.root))
        answer = self.send("We want them workout too take everything to the max.")
        self.assertIn("couldn't assign", answer["content"])
        self.assertEqual(set(queue.tasks(self.root)), before)

    def test_empty_status_says_idle_and_queued_assignments_are_not_idle(self):
        answer = self.send("Is work being performed on all of them now?")
        self.assertIn("are idle", answer["content"])
        self.seed()
        self.send("Use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing jobs")
        answer = self.send("What about Gemini and MiniMax?")
        self.assertIn("Gemini", answer["content"])
        self.assertIn("MiniMax", answer["content"])
        self.assertIn("waiting to run", answer["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("ready", answer["content"])

    def test_all_llms_directive_makes_a_real_job_per_named_worker(self):
        self.seed()
        answer = self.send("Use all LLMs for ongoing work")
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        self.assertEqual([records[i]["requested_worker"] for i in answer["task_ids"]], list(queue.MODEL_WORKERS))
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_observed_active_project_lane_can_supply_missing_prompt_context(self):
        observed = dict(records={}, work=[dict(state="running", worker="codex", project="Web Bob",
                                               title="Web Bob integration", task=None)])
        with patch("worker_assignment.observe_work", return_value=observed):
            answer = self.send("Use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing jobs")
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(len(answer["task_ids"]), 2)
        for identifier in answer["task_ids"]:
            self.assertIn("original task prompt was not recorded", records[identifier]["prompt"])
            self.assertIn("Project: Web Bob", records[identifier]["prompt"])
            self.assertIn(identifier, answer["content"])

    def test_frozen_turn_one_with_only_an_unverified_project_lane(self):
        from integration_rubric import check_reply, sessions
        from live_status_snapshot import build_live_status_snapshot
        # Failed attempt 2205: no queued/claimed jobs, no confirmed worker,
        # and these two open screens. Only the review lane identifies a project.
        screens = {"lane-integrate-deploy-2205": "68531.lane-integrate-deploy-2205",
                   "lane-webbob-llm-reviews": "29817.lane-webbob-llm-reviews"}
        exchange = sessions()[0]["exchanges"][0]
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=screens):
            before = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
            self.assertEqual(before["queue_counts"]["queued"], 0)
            self.assertEqual(before["queue_counts"]["claimed"], 0)
            self.assertEqual(before["work_counts"]["running"], 0)
            self.assertEqual(before["work_counts"]["unverified"], 2)
            started = time.time()
            answer = self.send(exchange["message"])
            records = queue.tasks(self.root)
            after = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
            self.assertEqual(check_reply(answer, exchange, self.identifier, started, before, after, records), [])
            self.assertEqual(len(records), 2)
            self.assertEqual(after["work_counts"]["running"], 0)
            for row in records.values():
                self.assertIsNone(row["source_job_id"])
                self.assertEqual(row["status"], "queued")
                self.assertIn("Project: Web Bob", row["prompt"])
                self.assertIn("worker activity is unverified", row["prompt"])
                self.assertNotIn("Ongoing task:", row["prompt"])
            self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        replay = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, exchange["message"], answer["request_id"])["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(replay["task_ids"], answer["task_ids"])
        self.assertEqual(replay["content"], answer["content"])
        self.assertEqual(set(queue.tasks(self.root)), set(records))

    def test_unverified_project_context_respects_scope_and_api_only_work(self):
        screens = {"lane-webbob-llm-reviews": "29817.lane-webbob-llm-reviews"}
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=screens):
            for text in ("Use Gemini and MiniMax for ongoing WebJam jobs",
                         "Use Codex for ongoing jobs", "Use all LLMs for ongoing jobs"):
                with self.subTest(text=text):
                    self.assertIn("couldn't assign", self.send(text)["content"])
                    self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
            self.send("Hold off on Web Bob")
            self.assertIn("current holds", self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])["content"])
            self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_unverified_lane_fallback_does_not_displace_an_ongoing_job(self):
        source = self.seed()
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"lane-webbob-llm-reviews": "29817.lane-webbob-llm-reviews"}):
            answer = self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])
        records = queue.tasks(self.root)
        self.assertEqual([records[key]["source_job_id"] for key in answer["task_ids"]], [source["id"], source["id"]])

    def test_unidentified_screens_and_unassigned_processes_do_not_supply_projects(self):
        from worker_activity import parse_processes
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"lane-integrate-deploy-2205": "68531.lane-integrate-deploy-2205"}), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes("40 1 01:00 S codex exec -\n")):
            self.assertIn("couldn't assign", self.send(SESSIONS[0]["exchanges"][0]["message"])["content"])
            self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_health_probes_and_unattributed_processes_are_not_task_execution(self):
        from worker_activity import collect_work, parse_processes
        table = parse_processes("10 1 01:00 S cursor-agent status\n"
                                "20 1 01:00 S codex exec resume --help\n"
                                "30 1 01:00 S claude --version\n"
                                "40 1 01:00 S codex exec -\n")
        rows = collect_work({}, [], table, time.time(), root=self.root)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 1)
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]["worker"], "codex")
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]["state"], "unverified")
        self.assertFalse(rows[0]["assignment_recorded"])
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=table):
            reply = self.send("What is working on what?")
        self.assertIn("Codex has activity I can't confirm yet", reply["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("running for", reply["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("Worker process", reply["content"])

    def test_verbose_completion_is_concise_with_full_report_available(self):
        from integration_rubric import style_errors
        from jobs_view import result_text
        self.seed()
        answer = self.send("Fix Web Bob queue recovery")
        identifier = answer["task_id"]
        row = queue.claim(self.root, "codex", identifier=identifier)
        queue.transition(self.root, identifier, row["claim_token"], "running")
        report = ("Fixed a verified quiet-hours bypass.\n\nReproduced with a failing test. Tests pass.\n\n"
                  "Changed paths: app.js. test_quiet_hours_js.mjs.\n\nWorktree is clean. No push or deployment.\n" * 10)
        queue.transition(self.root, identifier, row["claim_token"], "succeeded", result=report)
        self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
        completion = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]
        self.assertFalse(style_errors(completion["content"]))
        self.assertIn("Fixed a verified quiet-hours bypass", completion["content"])
        self.assertIn("/api/jobs/result?id=" + identifier, completion["content"])
        self.assertEqual(result_text(self.root, identifier)["text"], report)
        original = next(m for m in self.chat.get(self.identifier)["messages"] if m["id"] == answer["id"])
        self.assertEqual(original["content"], answer["content"])


FILE webchat/test_job_results_js.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Exercise the actual renderer and result dialog without a live dispatch.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import fs from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";

const source = fs.readFileSync(new URL("static/app.js", import.meta.url), "utf8");
const slice = (start, end) => source.slice(source.indexOf(start), source.indexOf(end, source.indexOf(start)));
const elements = Object.fromEntries(["report-title", "report-date", "report-body", "report"].map(id => [id, { textContent: "" }]));
let opened = 0, fetched = "", unavailable = false;
elements.report.showModal = () => { opened += 1; };
const context = vm.createContext({
  $: id => elements[id],
  api: async url => {
    fetched = url;
    if (unavailable) throw new Error("offline");
    return { project: "Web Bob", task: "Audit replies", text: "Full report: <img src=x onerror=alert(1)>" };
  },
});
vm.runInContext(slice("  function escapeHtml(", "  function isQuietHoursCt(") +
  slice("  function renderMarkdown(", "  const stored =") +
  slice("  async function showJobResult(", "  function composerState("), context);
const id = "webchat-" + "a".repeat(32), url = "/api/jobs/result?id=" + id;
assert.match(context.renderMarkdown(`[full result](${url})`), new RegExp(`data-job-id="${id}"`));
assert.match(context.renderMarkdown(`[<img src=x>](${url})`), /&lt;img src=x&gt;/);
for (const unsafe of ["javascript:alert(1)", "//evil.example", "/api/jobs/result?id=../../secret", url + "&extra=1"]) {
  assert(!context.renderMarkdown(`[result](${unsafe})`).includes("data-job-id"), unsafe);
}
assert.match(context.renderMarkdown("[PR](https://example.org/pr)"), /<a href="https:\/\/example.org\/pr"/);
await context.showJobResult(url);
assert.equal(opened, 1);
assert.equal(fetched, url);
assert.equal(elements["report-title"].textContent, "Web Bob");
assert.equal(elements["report-body"].textContent, "Full report: <img src=x onerror=alert(1)>");
assert.equal(elements["report-body"].innerHTML, undefined);
unavailable = true;
await context.showJobResult(url);
assert.match(elements["report-body"].textContent, /could not be loaded/);
console.log("job_results_js: OK");


FILE webchat/test_jobs_view.py
"""Jobs rendering contract: real journals and deterministic host observations."""
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from jobs_view import build_jobs_view, labels, result_text
from test_worker_activity import PS, SCREENS
from worker_activity import parse_processes


class JobsViewTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        for module, value in (("_screen_sessions", SCREENS), ("process_table", parse_processes(PS))):
            mock = patch("live_status_snapshot." + module, return_value=value)
            mock.start()
            self.addCleanup(mock.stop)

    def write(self, name, value):
        path = self.root / name
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(value)
        return path

    def queue(self, *rows, journal="conductor/state/queue.jsonl"):
        return self.write(journal, "".join(json.dumps(row) + "\n" for row in rows))

    def test_live_lanes_empty_queue_ignore_poisoned_dashboard_and_handback(self):
        poison = "Handback drill r10-2: running screen drill-r10-2 · abc1234 · Web Bob R38"
        self.write("conductor/dashboard.json", json.dumps({"live_ops": {"now_line": poison, "running_jobs": [{"plain_now": poison}]}}))
        self.write("conductor/state/NOW.md", poison)
        self.write("conductor/handback/HANDBACK-SUMMARY.md", poison)
        before = {p.relative_to(self.root): p.read_bytes() for p in self.root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
        view = build_jobs_view(self.root, now=10000)
        self.assertEqual(len(view["working"]), 4)
        clean = next(row for row in view["working"] if row["key"] == "lane-webbob-clean")
        self.assertEqual(clean["project"], "Web Bob")
        self.assertEqual(clean["elapsed_seconds"], 1080)
        self.assertEqual(clean["models"], ["Codex · gpt-fixture"])
        self.assertEqual(view["recently_finished"], [])
        for value in ("Handback", "abc1234", "R38", "command", "Private", "sha"):
            self.assertNotIn(value, json.dumps(view))
        self.assertEqual(before, {p.relative_to(self.root): p.read_bytes() for p in self.root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()})

    def test_queue_claim_and_screen_are_one_card_with_queue_description(self):
        self.queue(dict(id="job", status="running", title="Fix the Web Bob login button", worker="codex", pid=201, started_at=9000))
        view = build_jobs_view(self.root, now=10000)
        self.assertEqual(len(view["working"]), 4)
        row = next(row for row in view["working"] if row["key"] == "lane-webbob-clean")
        self.assertEqual(row["task"], "Fix the Web Bob login button")
        self.assertEqual(row["project"], "Web Bob")

    def test_multiple_workers_in_a_lane_are_one_card(self):
        processes = parse_processes(PS + "\n204 201 05:00 S claude --model opus -p Task\n")
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(len(view["working"]), 4)
        row = next(row for row in view["working"] if row["key"] == "lane-webbob-clean")
        self.assertEqual(row["models"], ["Codex · gpt-fixture", "Claude · opus"])

    def test_standalone_task_header_supplies_project_and_task(self):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}), patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(
                "10 1 04:00 S codex exec Follow-up 4 on draft/webbob-clean-0927: redesign Web Bob's Jobs tab. Private details\n")):
            row = build_jobs_view(self.root)["working"][0]
        self.assertEqual(row["project"], "Web Bob")
        self.assertEqual(row["task"], "Redesign Web Bob's Jobs tab")
        self.assertNotIn("Private", json.dumps(row))

    def test_service_name_is_not_a_task_description(self):
        unknown = labels({}, dict(screen="svc-job-runner", title="Task details unavailable"))
        self.assertEqual(unknown["task"], "Task details unavailable")
        known = labels({}, dict(screen="svc-job-runner", title="Review Web Bob login"))
        self.assertEqual(known["task"], "Review Web Bob login")
        self.assertEqual(known["project"], "Web Bob")

    def test_finished_last_five_use_terminal_time_not_delivery_time(self):
        rows = [dict(id=f"job-{i}", status="succeeded", title=f"WebJam fix {i}", finished_at=100 + i, result=f"Actual result {i}") for i in range(8)]
        self.queue(*rows, dict(rows[0], delivered_at=1000, updated_at=1000),
                   dict(id="drill-r10-2", status="succeeded", finished_at=2000, result="abc1234"),
                   dict(id="waiting", status="queued", updated_at=3000))
        view = build_jobs_view(self.root, now=4000)
        self.assertEqual([row["key"] for row in view["recently_finished"]], [f"job-{i}" for i in (7, 6, 5, 4, 3)])
        self.assertEqual(view["details"]["queued"], 1)
        self.assertTrue(all(row["result_url"] for row in view["recently_finished"]))
        self.assertEqual(result_text(self.root, "job-7")["text"], "Actual result 7")
        self.assertIsNone(result_text(self.root, "drill-r10-2"))

    def test_result_links_are_backed_by_files_or_saved_text_and_safe_urls(self):
        path = self.write("job/RESULT.md", "Saved report")
        self.queue(dict(job_id="webjam-fix", state="harvested", result_path=str(path), pr_url="https://github.com/example/repo/pull/12"),
                   dict(job_id="missing", state="harvested", result_path="gone.md", recovery="deliverables persisted", pr_url="javascript:alert(1)"),
                   dict(job_id="outside", state="harvested", result_path="../outside.md", result_url="https://user:password@example.com/private"),
                   journal="conductor/jobs.jsonl")
        rows = {row["key"]: row for row in build_jobs_view(self.root)["recently_finished"]}
        self.assertEqual(result_text(self.root, "webjam-fix")["text"], "Saved report")
        self.assertTrue(rows["webjam-fix"]["pr_url"])
        self.assertIsNone(rows["missing"]["result_url"])
        self.assertIsNone(rows["missing"]["pr_url"])
        self.assertIsNone(rows["outside"]["result_url"])
        self.assertIsNone(rows["outside"]["external_result_url"])
        self.assertIsNone(result_text(self.root, "missing"))

    def test_symlink_result_outside_root_is_not_read(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as outside:
            secret = Path(outside) / "private.md"
            secret.write_text("private")
            link = self.root / "result.md"
            link.symlink_to(secret)
            self.queue(dict(id="bad", status="succeeded", result_path=str(link)))
            self.assertIsNone(result_text(self.root, "bad"))

    def test_stale_claims_do_not_turn_into_live_cards_and_missing_checks_are_honest(self):
        self.queue(dict(id="old", status="running", pid=999, worker="codex", claimed_at=1))
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}), patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value={}):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(view["working"], [])
        self.assertEqual(view["details"]["unconfirmed"], 1)
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=None):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertFalse(view["inspection"]["processes_available"])
        self.assertEqual(len(view["working"]), 4)
        self.assertTrue(all(row["state"] == "unverified" for row in view["working"]))
        self.assertTrue(all(row["elapsed_seconds"] is None for row in view["working"]))

    def test_paused_and_failed_states_are_not_success(self):
        self.queue(dict(id="failed", status="failed", error="Check failed", finished_at=10))
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(PS.replace("24:00 S+", "24:00 T+"))):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(next(row for row in view["working"] if row["key"] == "lane-projects-push")["state"], "paused")
        self.assertEqual(view["recently_finished"][0]["state"], "failed")
        self.assertEqual(result_text(self.root, "failed")["text"], "Check failed")

    def test_health_and_cost_use_persisted_evidence(self):
        self.write("conductor/webchat/sessions/provider-health.json", json.dumps(dict(codex=dict(last_success=9500, checked_at=9500), minimax=dict(reason="rate limit", retry_at=11000, checked_at=9900))))
        self.queue(dict(call_id="1", cost_usd=None, reserved_usd=2), dict(call_id="1", cost_usd=1), dict(call_id="2", cost_usd=None, reserved_usd=.5), journal="conductor/ledger/minimax.jsonl")
        view = build_jobs_view(self.root, now=10000)
        health = {row["provider"]: row for row in view["details"]["health"]}
        self.assertEqual(health["Codex"]["state"], "ready")
        self.assertEqual(health["MiniMax"]["state"], "cooldown")
        self.assertEqual(health["Codex"]["age_seconds"], 500)
        self.assertEqual(view["details"]["costs"], [dict(provider="MiniMax", spent_usd=1, reserved_usd=.5)])

    def test_torn_queue_is_not_idle_and_read_does_not_repair_or_lock_it(self):
        path = self.queue(dict(id="waiting", status="queued", title="Fix chat"))
        path.write_text(path.read_text() + '{"id":')
        before = path.read_bytes()
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}), patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value={}):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(view["working"], [])
        self.assertEqual(view["details"]["queued"], 1)
        self.assertFalse(view["inspection"]["queue_available"])
        self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), before)
        self.assertFalse((path.parent / ".queue.lock").exists())

    def test_unreadable_queue_is_not_an_empty_queue(self):
        queue = self.root / "conductor/state/queue.jsonl"
        queue.mkdir(parents=True)
        view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertFalse(view["inspection"]["queue_available"])

    def test_missing_queue_is_valid_empty_state(self):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}), patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value={}):
            view = build_jobs_view(self.root)
        self.assertTrue(view["inspection"]["queue_available"])
        self.assertEqual(view["working"], [])

    def test_service_reviewer_task_uses_plain_words(self):
        from jobs_view import labels

        row = labels({}, {"screen": "svc-webbob-reviewers", "title": "Task details unavailable"})
        self.assertEqual(row, dict(project="Web Bob", task="Reviewing changes"))

    def test_empty_corrupt_and_unknown_cost_ledgers_are_not_zero_dollars(self):
        for value in ("", "not JSON", json.dumps(dict(call_id="a", cost_usd=None)),
                      json.dumps(dict(call_id="a", cost_usd=-5)), json.dumps(dict(call_id="a", cost_usd="bad"))):
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                self.write("conductor/ledger/minimax.jsonl", value)
                row = build_jobs_view(self.root)["details"]["costs"][0]
                self.assertIsNone(row["spent_usd"])
                self.assertIsNone(row["reserved_usd"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/test_lane_collector.py
"""Lane collector fixtures: screen -ls and ps output only."""
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from lane_collector import collect_bob_lanes, prompt_slug
from test_worker_activity import PS, SCREENS
from worker_activity import parse_processes


class LaneCollectorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        (self.root / "logs").mkdir()
        (self.root / "reviews").mkdir()
        (self.root / "conductor/state").mkdir(parents=True)

    def collect(self, *, now=100_000):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=SCREENS), \
                patch("worker_activity.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(PS)):
            return collect_bob_lanes(self.root, now=now, sessions=SCREENS, processes=parse_processes(PS))

    def test_live_lanes_map_llm_project_task_and_elapsed(self):
        (self.root / "logs/webbob-clean-prompt.txt").write_text(
            "Project: Web Bob\nTask: Fix chat replies for overnight lanes.\nWrite reviews/WEBBOB-LANES-READY-0927.md\n")
        (self.root / "logs/webbob-clean.log").write_text("working\n")
        (self.root / "reviews/WEBBOB-LANES-READY-0927.md").write_text("# ready\n")
        bob = self.collect()
        live = {row["screen"]: row for row in bob["live"]}
        self.assertEqual(len(live), 4)
        clean = live["lane-webbob-clean"]
        self.assertEqual(clean["llm"], "codex")
        self.assertEqual(clean["model"], "gpt-fixture")
        self.assertEqual(clean["project"], "Web Bob")
        self.assertIn("Fix chat replies", clean["task"])
        self.assertEqual(clean["elapsed_seconds"], 18 * 60)
        self.assertEqual(clean["result_path"], "reviews/WEBBOB-LANES-READY-0927.md")

    def test_finished_lane_within_twelve_hours(self):
        prompt = self.root / "logs/webbob-old-prompt.txt"
        prompt.write_text("Project: WebJam\nTask: Ship the login fix.\nWrite reviews/WEBJAM-DONE.md\n")
        review = self.root / "reviews/WEBJAM-DONE.md"
        review.write_text("done\n")
        now = 200_000.0
        for path in (prompt, review):
            path.touch()
        import os

        os.utime(prompt, (now - 3600, now - 3600))
        os.utime(review, (now - 1800, now - 1800))
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}), \
                patch("worker_activity.process_table", return_value={}):
            bob = collect_bob_lanes(self.root, now=now, sessions={}, processes={})
        self.assertEqual(len(bob["live"]), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(bob["finished_recent"]), 1)
        finished = bob["finished_recent"][0]
        self.assertEqual(finished["screen"], "lane-webbob-old")
        self.assertEqual(finished["project"], "WebJam")
        self.assertEqual(finished["result_path"], "reviews/WEBJAM-DONE.md")

    def test_prompt_slug_strips_dates(self):
        self.assertEqual(prompt_slug("proj-adoptiq-2004-prompt"), "lane-proj-adoptiq")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/test_live_integration_proof.py
"""The live proof must tolerate completion between its queue and chat reads."""
import hashlib
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

from live_integration_proof import Proof


class ProofTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_resume_refreshes_queue_after_terminal_chat_without_resubmitting(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            proof = Proof.__new__(Proof)
            proof.root = Path(directory)
            proof.save = Mock()
            proof.call = Mock(side_effect=AssertionError("Must not submit a duplicate task"))
            entry = dict(provider="codex", conversation_id="conversation", task=dict(task_id="task-1"))
            proof.data = {"providers": [entry]}
            running = dict(id="task-1", status="running", attempts=1)
            finished = dict(running, status="succeeded", worker="codex", created_at=1,
                            claimed_at=2, started_at=3, finished_at=4, result="Real answer",
                            result_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"Real answer").hexdigest())
            proof.history = Mock(return_value={"messages": [dict(
                id="answer-1", task_id="task-1", task_status="succeeded", provider="codex",
                created_at=5, content="codex finished:\n\nReal answer")]})
            journal = proof.root / "conductor/state/queue.jsonl"
            journal.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
            journal.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(dict(id="task-1", event=event,
                status=event, updated_at=i)) for i, event in enumerate(("claimed", "running", "succeeded"), 2)))
            with patch("live_integration_proof.task_queue.tasks", side_effect=[{"task-1": running}, {"task-1": finished}]):
                proof.resume("codex")
            proof.call.assert_not_called()
            self.assertEqual(entry["task"]["status"], "succeeded")
            self.assertEqual(entry["task"]["result_message_id"], "answer-1")
            self.assertIn("completed_at", entry)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/test_live_maxmode_proof.py
"""Proof verification must select preserved receipts and reject absent overlap."""
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import live_maxmode_proof as proof


class ProofTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def verify(self, overlap=True, duplicate=False):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            receipt = root / "preserved-proof.json"
            rows = [dict(id=lane, group_id="group", worker=lane, requested_worker=lane,
                         status="succeeded", attempts=1, execution_started_at=1,
                         finished_at=10, delivered_at=11) for lane in (*sorted(proof.MODELS), "mini")]
            data = dict(conversation_id="conversation", group_id="group", snapshots=[])
            if overlap:
                data["all_five_running"] = {"at": "previous observation"}
            receipt.write_text(json.dumps(data))
            result = dict(id="answer", group_id="group", task_status="succeeded", content="One combined answer")
            def call(path):
                if path == "/api/jobs":
                    return dict(dispatch=rows, read_at=12)
                return dict(messages=[result] * (2 if duplicate else 1))
            with patch.object(proof, "Client") as client, patch.object(sys, "argv", ["proof", "verify", "--root", str(root), "--receipt", str(receipt)]), patch("builtins.print"):
                client.return_value.call.side_effect = call
                proof.main()
            return json.loads(receipt.read_text())

    def test_verify_preserved_receipt_without_resubmitting_request(self):
        data = self.verify()
        self.assertEqual(data["overlap"]["seconds"], 9)
        self.assertEqual(data["answer"]["id"], "answer")

    def test_rejects_missing_live_simultaneous_execution_observation(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(AssertionError, "No proven overlap"):
            self.verify(overlap=False)

    def test_rejects_duplicate_group_answers(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(AssertionError, "exactly one"):
            self.verify(duplicate=True)


FILE webchat/test_live_redeploy_check.py
"""Release gating must fail closed on authentication and individual HTTP failures."""
import contextlib
import io
import json
import threading
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Server
from live_redeploy_check import CHECKS, CheckFailure, Client, verify_four, follow_through
from build_info import BUILD
from task_dispatch import task_queue
from test_webchat import Fixture


class ReleaseChecks(Fixture):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.server = Server(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.chat)
        threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        self.url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}"
        self.client = Client(self.url, self.folder / ".password")
        self.output = self.root / "result.json"

    def tearDown(self):
        self.server.shutdown()
        self.server.server_close()
        super().tearDown()

    def verify(self):
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
            return verify_four(self.client, self.root, self.output)

    def test_login_failure_marks_all_four_failed_and_writes_no_jobs(self):
        password = self.folder / "wrong-password"
        password.write_text("deliberately-invalid")
        self.client.password_file = password
        result = self.verify()
        self.assertFalse(result["all_pass"])
        self.assertEqual(set(result["checks"]), set(CHECKS))
        for check in result["checks"].values():
            self.assertFalse(check["pass_check"])
            self.assertIn("HTTP 401", check["error"])
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "conductor/state/queue.jsonl").exists())
        self.assertNotIn("deliberately-invalid", self.output.read_text())

    def test_authenticated_four_checks_use_real_http_and_durable_jobs(self):
        result = self.verify()
        self.assertTrue(result["all_pass"], json.dumps(result))
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.assertNotIn(self.chat.password, self.output.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(len(result["checks"]["push2"]["jobs"]), 2)

    def test_go_http_error_cannot_be_mistaken_for_cleared_hold(self):
        original = self.client.call
        def expired_session(route, data=None):
            if data and data.get("message") == "go":
                raise CheckFailure("HTTP 401 at /api/messages")
            return original(route, data)
        with patch.object(self.client, "call", side_effect=expired_session):
            result = self.verify()
        self.assertFalse(result["all_pass"])
        self.assertFalse(result["checks"]["go"]["pass_check"])
        self.assertEqual(result["checks"]["go"]["error"], "HTTP 401 at /api/messages")
        self.assertTrue(all(result["checks"][name]["pass_check"] for name in CHECKS if name != "go"))

    def test_async_failed_review_cannot_pass_on_its_queue_receipt(self):
        result = self.verify()
        job_id = result["checks"]["claude161"]["jobs"][0]["id"]
        claim = task_queue.claim(self.root, "claude")
        self.assertEqual(claim["id"], job_id)
        task_queue.transition(self.root, job_id, claim["claim_token"], "rejected", error="session limit; no review")
        result = follow_through(self.client, self.root, self.output, result, BUILD["sha"], timeout=0)
        self.assertFalse(result["all_pass"])
        self.assertIn("claude161:", result["failure"])
        self.assertIn("session limit", result["failure"])


FILE webchat/test_live_replies_check.py
"""The new live gate must reject queue receipts without real execution."""
import contextlib
import io
import threading

from app import Server
from live_redeploy_check import Client
from live_replies_check import verify_replies
from test_webchat import Fixture


class RepliesGate(Fixture):
    def test_authenticated_exact_replay_cannot_pass_on_four_queued_jobs(self):
        server = Server(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.chat)
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        try:
            client = Client(f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}", self.folder / ".password")
            output = self.root / "proof.json"
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                proof = verify_replies(client, self.root, output, timeout=0)
            self.assertFalse(proof["all_pass"])
            self.assertEqual(len(proof["task_ids"]), 4)
            self.assertEqual(len(proof["exchanges"]), 6)
            self.assertIn("four actual executions", proof["failure"])
            self.assertFalse(proof["started_jobs"])
            self.assertNotIn(self.chat.password, output.read_text())
            self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        finally:
            server.shutdown()
            server.server_close()


FILE webchat/test_live_status_snapshot.py
"""Integration regressions for the dispatch journal in provider context."""
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from live_status_snapshot import build_live_status_snapshot, encode_snapshot, provider_health
from providers import Providers
from test_worker_activity import PS, SCREENS
from worker_activity import parse_processes


class SnapshotTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        (self.root / "conductor/state").mkdir(parents=True)
        self.queue = self.root / "conductor/state/queue.jsonl"
        sampler = patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value={})
        sampler.start()
        self.addCleanup(sampler.stop)

    def snapshot(self):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"svc-job-runner": "123.svc-job-runner"}), \
                patch("ops_panel.load_live_ops", return_value={"live_ops": {"now_line": "Handback drill r10: running screen drill-r10"}}):
            return build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)

    def test_latest_dispatch_states_workers_and_torn_tail_are_read_only(self):
        rows = [dict(id="task-a", status="queued", requested_worker="cursor", updated_at=1),
                dict(id="task-a", status="running", worker="cursor", updated_at=3),
                dict(id="task-b", status="succeeded", worker="gemini", updated_at=2),
                dict(id="task-c", status="failed", worker="minimax", updated_at=4),
                dict(id="task-d", status="queued", requested_worker="claude", updated_at=5)]
        original = "\n".join(json.dumps(row) for row in rows) + '\n{"id":"torn'
        self.queue.write_text(original)
        snap = self.snapshot()
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_counts"], dict(queued=1, claimed=1, done=1, failed=1))
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_jobs"]["claimed"][0]["job_id"], "task-a")
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_jobs"]["queued"][0]["worker"], "claude")
        self.assertEqual(snap["lane_locks"]["cursor"]["active_jobs"], ["task-a"])
        self.assertEqual(self.queue.read_text(), original)
        self.assertIsNone(snap["now_line"])

    def test_recovered_generation_overrides_old_failure_and_ignores_drills(self):
        path = self.root / "conductor/webchat/sessions/provider-health.json"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"gemini": {"reason": "network", "retry_at": 500, "checked_at": 100},
                                   "minimax": {"reason": "network", "retry_at": 500, "checked_at": 100, "probe": "ok"}}))
        log = self.root / "logs/webchat-failover.log"
        log.parent.mkdir()
        log.write_text('\n'.join(json.dumps(r) for r in [
            dict(provider="gemini", event="success", timestamp=200, context="production"),
            dict(provider="gemini", event="failure", timestamp=300, context="live-drill", reason="network"),
            dict(provider="minimax", event="probe", result="ok", timestamp=400, context="production")]))
        health = provider_health(self.root, now=450)
        self.assertEqual(health["gemini"]["state"], "ready")
        self.assertIsNone(health["gemini"]["reason"])
        self.assertEqual(health["minimax"]["state"], "cooldown")
        self.assertEqual(provider_health(self.root, now=600)["minimax"]["state"], "recovery_due")
        self.assertEqual(health["cursor"]["state"], "unchecked")

    def test_successful_harvest_recovery_is_done_and_counts_include_omitted_rows(self):
        rows = [dict(job_id=f"job-{i}", state="harvested", outcome="result",
                     recovery="harvest_complete", timestamp=i) for i in range(30)]
        (self.root / "conductor/jobs.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(row) for row in rows))
        snap = self.snapshot()
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_counts"]["done"], 30)
        self.assertEqual(len(snap["queue_jobs"]["done"]), 24)
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_jobs"]["done"][0]["job_id"], "job-29")
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_jobs"]["failed"], [])

    def test_live_lane_work_overrides_cached_ready_and_survives_prompt_budget(self):
        (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps({name: dict(state="ready") for name in ("claude", "codex")}))
        self.queue.write_text(json.dumps(dict(id="old-private-id", status="succeeded", updated_at=1)))
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=SCREENS), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(PS)):
            snap = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_counts"]["claimed"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(snap["work_counts"], dict(running=4, paused=0, claimed=0, unverified=0))
        for name in ("claude", "codex"):
            self.assertEqual(snap["lane_locks"][name]["state"], "busy")
            self.assertEqual(snap["lane_locks"][name]["cached_state"], "ready")
        self.assertIn("4 live worker(s) running", snap["plain_summary"])
        self.assertEqual(sum(row["role"] == "worker" for row in snap["screen_lanes"]), 4)
        snap["queue_jobs"]["done"] *= 100
        payload, clipped = encode_snapshot(snap, 8000)
        self.assertTrue(clipped)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(payload)["work"], snap["work"])

    def test_failed_inspection_is_not_reported_as_an_empty_successful_check(self):
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=None), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=None):
            snap = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(snap["inspection"], dict(screens_available=False, processes_available=False, queue_available=True))

    def test_work_and_lane_details_share_one_sample_including_inspection_failure(self):
        from live_status_snapshot import read_job_journals
        for sample in (parse_processes(PS), None):
            with self.subTest(failed=sample is None), \
                    patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=SCREENS), \
                    patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=sample) as processes, \
                    patch("live_status_snapshot.read_job_journals", wraps=read_job_journals) as journals, \
                    patch("worker_activity.process_table", side_effect=AssertionError("Unexpected second sample")):
                snap = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
            self.assertEqual(processes.call_count, 1)
            self.assertEqual(journals.call_count, 1)
            self.assertEqual(snap["bob_lanes"]["inspection"]["processes_available"], sample is not None)
            for lane in snap["bob_lanes"]["live"]:
                worker = next(row for row in snap["work"] if row.get("screen") == lane["screen"])
                self.assertEqual(lane["state"], worker["state"])
                self.assertEqual(lane["model"], worker["model"])

    def test_active_assignments_are_not_truncated_in_saved_observations(self):
        self.queue.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(dict(id=f"job-{i}", status="queued", requested_worker="gemini"))
                                         for i in range(30)))
        snap = self.snapshot()
        self.assertEqual(snap["queue_counts"]["queued"], 30)
        self.assertEqual(len(snap["queue_jobs"]["queued"]), 30)

    def test_status_does_not_refresh_or_write_live_ledger_when_dashboard_is_stale(self):
        dashboard = self.root / "conductor/dashboard.json"
        dashboard.write_text(json.dumps(dict(live_ops=dict(generated_at="2020-01-01T00:00:00+00:00", running_jobs=[]))))
        before = {str(path): path.read_bytes() for path in self.root.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
        with patch("ops_panel.inline_live_ops") as refresh, \
                patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}):
            snap = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        refresh.assert_not_called()
        self.assertTrue(snap["live_ops_stale"])
        self.assertEqual(before, {str(path): path.read_bytes() for path in self.root.rglob("*") if path.is_file()})

    def test_prompt_budget_keeps_valid_json_current_jobs_health_and_disk(self):
        self.queue.write_text(json.dumps(dict(id="current-task", status="running", worker="codex")) + "\n")
        snap = self.snapshot()
        snap["queue_jobs"]["done"] = [dict(job_id="old-job-" + str(i), recovery="x" * 160) for i in range(100)]
        snap["bob_lanes"] = dict(live=[dict(task="active duplicate " + "x" * 16000)],
                                 finished_recent=[dict(task="finished " + "x" * 16000)])
        payload, clipped = encode_snapshot(snap, 8000)
        parsed = json.loads(payload)
        self.assertTrue(clipped)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(payload), 8000)
        self.assertEqual(parsed["service_health"], snap["service_health"])
        self.assertEqual(parsed["disk"], snap["disk"])
        self.assertEqual(parsed["queue_jobs"]["claimed"][0]["job_id"], "current-task")
        provider = Providers(self.root, lambda text: text)
        self.addCleanup(provider.close)
        for lite in (True, False):
            with patch("live_status_snapshot.build_live_status_snapshot", return_value=snap):
                parts = provider.snapshot(lite=lite)
            part = next(p for p in parts if p["path"].startswith("live_status_snapshot"))
            self.assertIn("disk", json.loads(part["text"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE webchat/test_quiet_hours_js.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Boundary tests for quiet-hours hour logic (matches app.js + quiet_hours.py). */
function inQuietHoursCentralHour(hour) {
  return hour >= 23 || hour < 7;
}

const cases = [
  [22, false],
  [23, true],
  [0, true],
  [6, true],
  [7, false],
  [12, false],
];
let failed = 0;
for (const [hour, expect] of cases) {
  const got = inQuietHoursCentralHour(hour);
  if (got !== expect) {
    console.error(`hour ${hour}: expected ${expect}, got ${got}`);
    failed += 1;
  }
}
if (failed) process.exit(1);
console.log("quiet_hours_js: OK");


FILE webchat/test_replies_session.py
"""Exact user turns from session 8126301c; real journals, no model calls."""
import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat
from chat_intent import projects
from task_dispatch import parse_work, task_queue as queue
from test_webchat import Fixture
from worker_activity import parse_processes

EXCHANGES = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("fixtures").joinpath("replies_session_0927.json").read_text())["exchanges"]
ASKS = [row["message"] for row in EXCHANGES]
NAMES = ["WebJam", "AdoptIQ", "StoryBoard", "Fantasy Football"]


class SessionReplies(Fixture):
    def submit(self, text, request=None, **kwargs):
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, text, request or str(uuid.uuid4()), **kwargs)
        self.wait()
        return self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]

    def evidence(self):
        screens, ps = {}, []
        for pid, screen, command, project, task in (
            (100, "lane-webjam-fix", "codex exec --model gpt-test task", "WebJam", "Fix login validation"),
            (200, "lane-adoptiq-test", "cursor-agent -p task", "AdoptIQ", "Test intake validation"),
            (300, "svc-storyboard-review", "python3 /repo/compat.py gemini -f prompt", "StoryBoard", "Review scene editing"),
            (400, "lane-fantasy-audit", "python3 /repo/compat.py minimax -f prompt", "Fantasy Football", "Audit lineup scoring"),
            (500, "lane-webjam-tests", "codex exec task", "WebJam", "Test login retries"),
        ):
            screens[screen] = f"{pid}.{screen}"
            ps.extend([f"{pid} 1 12:00 S SCREEN -dmS {screen} bash", f"{pid+1} {pid} 11:00 S {command}"])
            stem = screen.split("-", 1)[1]
            (self.root / "logs" / (stem + "-prompt-1720.txt")).write_text(f"Project: {project}\nTask: {task}. Run relevant tests.\n")
            (self.root / "logs" / (stem + "-1720.log")).write_text("Validated one case\nStill checking boundaries\n")
        board = {name: dict(state="ready") for name in ("codex", "cursor", "gemini", "minimax")}
        board["claude"] = dict(state="cooldown", detail="claude: You've hit your session limit · resets 6:20pm (America/Chicago)")
        (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps(board))
        return screens, parse_processes("\n".join(ps))

    def test_exact_worker_questions_criticism_and_tone_list_every_worker(self):
        (self.root / "logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        screens, processes = self.evidence()
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=screens), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
            for text in ASKS[1:5] + ["What's working on what?", "what's going on each LLM"]:
                with self.subTest(text=text):
                    reply = self.submit(text)
                    answer = reply["content"]
                    self.assertEqual(reply["provider"], "state")
                    self.assertNotIn("running for", answer)
                    for task in NAMES:
                        self.assertIn(task, answer)
                    for name in NAMES:
                        self.assertIn(name, answer)
                    self.assertNotIn("running for", answer)
                    self.assertIn("Claude is cooling down until 6:20 PM CT", answer)
                    self.assertNotIn("cooling down", answer.split("Claude")[0])
                    self.assertLessEqual(len(answer.split(". ")), 4)
                    for unwanted in ("unknown task", "details unavailable", "I'll focus", "Chat health", "Recent logs", "Disk space"):
                        self.assertNotIn(unwanted.lower(), answer.lower())
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))

    def test_missing_assignment_uses_readable_lane_without_log_dump(self):
        (self.root / "logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (self.root / "logs/mystery-1720.log").write_text("phase one\nwaiting for response\n")
        with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"lane-mystery": "100.lane-mystery"}), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(
                    "100 1 08:00 S SCREEN -dmS lane-mystery bash\n101 100 07:00 S codex exec private task")):
            answer = self.submit(ASKS[1])["content"]
        for expected in ("Codex is working on Mystery",):
            self.assertIn(expected, answer)
        for hidden in ("lane-mystery", "logs/", "waiting for response", "model not reported", "7 min"):
            self.assertNotIn(hidden, answer)
        self.assertNotIn("unknown task", answer.lower())

    def test_exact_stress_request_saves_four_scoped_draft_jobs_and_replays_once(self):
        (self.root / "conductor/lanes.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "codex": dict(state="ready"), "cursor": dict(state="ready"), "claude": dict(state="cooldown"),
            "gemini": dict(state="ready"), "minimax": dict(state="ready")}))
        request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        receipt = self.submit(ASKS[5], request)
        self.assertEqual(receipt["provider"], "dispatcher")
        rows = [queue.tasks(self.root)[job] for job in receipt["task_ids"]]
        self.assertEqual([row["title"] for row in rows], NAMES)
        self.assertEqual({row["requested_worker"] for row in rows}, {"codex", "cursor"})
        self.assertEqual(len(receipt["content"].splitlines()), 4)
        self.assertNotIn("Started", receipt["content"])
        for row in rows:
            self.assertEqual(projects(row["prompt"]), [row["title"]])
            self.assertEqual(row["publish_project"], row["title"])
            self.assertTrue(row["draft_pr"])
            self.assertEqual(row["kind"], "coding")
            self.assertIn("worktree", row["prompt"])
            self.assertIn("draft branch", row["prompt"])
            self.assertIn(row["id"], receipt["content"])
            claim = queue.claim(self.root, row["requested_worker"], identifier=row["id"])
            self.assertIsNotNone(claim)
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "running")
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "executing")
            self.chat.reconcile_tasks()
            updated = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][1]
            self.assertEqual(updated["content"], receipt["content"])
            self.assertEqual(updated["action_card"]["state"], "running")
            queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claim["claim_token"], "succeeded", result="Verified fixture result")
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        self.submit(ASKS[5], request)
        self.assertEqual(len(queue.tasks(self.root)), 4)
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_exact_initial_request_uses_saved_projects_or_asks_for_targets(self):
        answer = self.submit(ASKS[0])
        self.assertEqual(answer["content"], "Which projects should I work on?")
        self.assertFalse(queue.tasks(self.root))
        self.submit("Prioritize WebJam, AdoptIQ, StoryBoard and Fantasy Football")
        answer = self.submit(ASKS[0])
        self.assertEqual([queue.tasks(self.root)[job]["title"] for job in answer["task_ids"]], NAMES)
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)

    def test_action_does_not_turn_into_status_and_questions_or_negation_do_not_dispatch(self):
        for text in ("Should we start WebJam and AdoptIQ?", "Don't start WebJam and AdoptIQ", "What would happen if we start all projects?"):
            self.assertEqual(parse_work(text), (None, "auto"))
        self.assertTrue(parse_work(ASKS[5])[0])
        self.assertTrue(parse_work("push WebJam and Stkryboard")[0])
        self.assertEqual(projects("WebJam, AdoptiQ, Stkryboard, Storyboard, Fantasy Football"), NAMES)

    def test_status_stays_authoritative_for_every_explicit_chat_provider(self):
        for provider in ("codex", "claude", "cursor", "gemini", "minimax"):
            reply = self.submit(ASKS[4], provider=provider)
            self.assertEqual(reply["provider"], "state")
            self.assertIn("Codex", reply["content"])
            self.assertIn("are idle", reply["content"])
        self.assertFalse(self.fake.calls)


FILE webchat/test_reply_authority.py
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from reply_authority import enforce, operational_reply
from test_worker_activity import PS, SCREENS
from worker_activity import parse_processes


class AuthorityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_services_and_fresh_log_observations_are_available_on_request(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            (root / "logs").mkdir()
            (root / "logs/job-runner.log").write_text("Claimed review job\n")
            with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"svc-job-runner": "12.svc-job-runner"}):
                answer = operational_reply(root, "server", "is it going now?")
            self.assertIn("are idle", answer)
            self.assertNotIn("job runner", answer)
            self.assertNotIn("Recent logs:", answer)
            self.assertNotIn("I don't see active work", answer)
            with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={"svc-job-runner": "12.svc-job-runner"}):
                answer = operational_reply(root, "server", "status with diagnostics")
            self.assertIn("logs/job-runner.log: Claimed review job", answer)

    def setUp(self):
        for name in ("_screen_sessions", "process_table"):
            sampler = patch("live_status_snapshot." + name, return_value={})
            sampler.start()
            self.addCleanup(sampler.stop)

    def test_model_cannot_confirm_a_queue_write(self):
        for text in ("I've added this job to the builder inbox.", "The job I queued is still running.",
                     "I queued a council pass.", "Started on Claude — job abc123.",
                     "I've queued it.", "Claude is working on it.", "I started the review.",
                     "Done — job abc123 is running.", "Job abc123 has been created.",
                     "You're all set — Claude has your task.", "Claude’s on it."):
            with self.subTest(text=text):
                answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Ok going now?", dict(content=text, provider="gemini"))
                self.assertEqual(answer, "I did not start, assign, or queue any work in this turn.")

    def test_plain_answers_and_job_explanations_are_unchanged(self):
        for text in ("Got it — I’ll keep it short.", "Hi Jeff!", "A lease expires; a lock needs to be released.",
                     "Jobs wait in the queue until a worker is free.", "I can review PR #161.",
                     "A job is queued when every worker is busy.",
                     "The video is paused. Press play to continue.",
                     "Jobs can be paused before they start.",
                     "Here are the details:\n\n- First fact.\n- Second fact."):
            with self.subTest(text=text):
                self.assertEqual(enforce(Path('/unused'), "Tell me more", dict(content=text, provider="gemini")), text)

    def test_guard_keeps_the_answer_and_removes_only_the_false_claim(self):
        for claim in ("I've queued a task.", "Started on Claude — job abc123.",
                      "No server-confirmed job was created by this reply. Send `start a job: <task>` to create a tracked job."):
            with self.subTest(claim=claim):
                answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Please be brief", dict(content="Got it — I’ll keep it short.\n\n" + claim, provider="gemini"))
                self.assertEqual(answer, "Got it — I’ll keep it short." if claim.startswith("No server") else
                                 "I did not start, assign, or queue any work in this turn.")

    def test_style_preference_never_gets_an_unsolicited_status_report(self):
        answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Keep replies short and friendly.",
                         dict(content="Hey! All quiet — no jobs running, ledger's synced. Nothing to report right now.", provider="claude"))
        self.assertEqual(answer, "Got it.")

    def test_no_work_notices_do_not_leak_into_casual_replies(self):
        for notice in ("No jobs running.", "No task has been queued.", "Nothing was started."):
            answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Hi Bob", dict(content="Hi Jeff! " + notice, provider="claude"))
            self.assertEqual(answer, "Hi Jeff!")

    def test_identity_and_health_are_derived_from_server_not_model(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            with patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value={}):
                answer = enforce(root, "How everything going. We using all resources now?",
                                 dict(provider="gemini", content="Cursor is handling our chat; Gemini and MiniMax are still failing."))
        self.assertIn("Gemini", answer)
        self.assertIn("are idle", answer)
        self.assertNotIn("Answering provider:", answer)
        self.assertNotIn("still failing", answer)
        self.assertNotIn("Cursor is handling", answer)

    def test_exact_progress_followup_uses_workers_instead_of_provider_empty_queue_claim(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder, \
                patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=SCREENS), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(PS)):
            root = Path(folder)
            (root / "conductor").mkdir()
            (root / "conductor/jobs.jsonl").write_text('{"job_id":"shelf-load-1659","state":"harvested"}\n')
            for text in ("is it going now?", "Ok great is it moving along now?", "Is anything running?", "Are things making progress?", "What's running?",
                         "Status without internal IDs or raw diagnostics, please"):
                with self.subTest(text=text):
                    answer = enforce(root, text, dict(provider="gemini", content=
                        "Nothing is running. All lanes are ready. Use `start a job: task`."))
                    self.assertIn("Claude is working on", answer)
                    self.assertIn("Codex is working on Web Bob", answer)
                    self.assertIn("Gemini", answer)
                    self.assertIn("MiniMax", answer)
                    self.assertNotIn("running for", answer)
                    self.assertNotIn(";", answer)
                    self.assertLessEqual(len(answer.split(". ")), 4)
                    for hidden in ("None", "shelf-load-1659", "History:", "provider:", "start a job:", "All lanes", "logs/", "lane-", "last:"):
                        self.assertNotIn(hidden, answer)

    def test_internal_details_and_answering_model_are_available_when_requested(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder, \
                patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=SCREENS), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=parse_processes(PS)):
            answer = operational_reply(Path(folder), "gemini", "Status with screen names and PIDs; who is answering?")
        self.assertIn("lane-projects-push, 103", answer)
        self.assertIn("Gemini answered this message.", answer)
        self.assertNotIn("None", answer)

    def test_unverified_screens_and_unavailable_checks_never_claim_idle(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            for screens, processes in ((SCREENS, {}), ({}, None), (None, {})):
                with self.subTest(screens=screens, processes=processes), \
                        patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=screens), \
                        patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
                    answer = operational_reply(Path(folder), "server")
                    self.assertNotIn("I don't see active work", answer)
                    self.assertNotIn("Yes", answer)
                    self.assertNotIn("None", answer)
                    self.assertIn("confirm", answer)

    def test_special_job_syntax_is_removed_regardless_of_how_model_phrases_it(self):
        for notice in ("Type `start a job: fix login`.", "Please submit using the start a job: prefix.",
                       "To get things moving, please use `start a job: <task description>`."):
            answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Hi Bob", dict(content="Hi Jeff! " + notice, provider="gemini"))
            self.assertEqual(answer, "Hi Jeff!")

    def test_removing_a_footer_preserves_answer_formatting(self):
        useful = "A lease expires. A lock needs to be released.\n\n- First fact.\n- Second fact."
        answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Tell me more", dict(provider="gemini", content=useful +
                         "\n\nNo server-confirmed job was created by this reply. Send `start a job: <task>` to create a tracked job."))
        self.assertEqual(answer, useful)

    def test_casual_chat_does_not_expand_a_model_claim_into_a_status_report(self):
        answer = enforce(Path('/unused'), "Hi Bob", dict(provider="gemini", content=
                         "Hi Jeff! Cursor is handling our chat; Gemini and MiniMax are still failing."))
        self.assertEqual(answer, "Hi Jeff!")


FILE webchat/test_status_observation.py
"""Durable render-time evidence through startup races, retries and restarts."""
import copy
import json
import stat
import time
import uuid
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat
from integration_rubric import check_reply
from live_status_snapshot import build_live_status_snapshot
from reply_authority import format_operational_reply
from status_observation import observation_errors, snapshot_digest
from task_dispatch import task_queue as queue
from test_webbob_replyq_1856 import SessionFixture
from worker_activity import parse_processes


QUESTION = "Ok great works on all LLMs now?"


class StatusObservationTests(SessionFixture):
    def status(self, question=QUESTION):
        before = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        started = time.time()
        answer = self.send(question)
        after = build_live_status_snapshot(self.root)
        return answer, started, before, after

    def errors(self, answer, started, before, after, question=QUESTION):
        return check_reply(answer, dict(message=question, expected="status"), self.identifier,
                           started, before, after, queue.tasks(self.root))

    def job(self, index=0, worker="gemini"):
        return queue.enqueue(self.root, "external", str(uuid.uuid4()), "Review Web Bob task " + str(index),
                             worker=worker, kind="audit" if worker in ("gemini", "minimax") else "coding",
                             title="Web Bob task " + str(index))

    def claim(self, row, pid):
        claimed = queue.claim(self.root, row["requested_worker"], identifier=row["id"])
