        return queue.transition(self.root, row["id"], claimed["claim_token"], "running", pid=pid)

    def processes(self, rows):
        return parse_processes("\n".join(
            f"{row['pid']} 1 00:01 S python3 /fixture/services/dispatch_worker.py {row['id']} private-claim-token"
            for row in rows))

    def test_startup_1_to_3_to_5_uses_exact_saved_sample_not_endpoints(self):
        rows = [self.claim(self.job(i, worker), 100 + i)
                for i, worker in enumerate(("codex", "gemini", "minimax", "gemini", "minimax"))]
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", side_effect=[self.processes(rows[:1]),
                self.processes(rows[:3]), self.processes(rows)]) as sampler:
            answer, started, before, after = self.status()
        observed = answer["status_observation"]["snapshot"]
        self.assertEqual([snap["work_counts"]["running"] for snap in (before, observed, after)], [1, 3, 5])
        self.assertEqual(observed["work_counts"]["claimed"], 2)
        self.assertIn("Codex", answer["content"])
        self.assertIn("Gemini", answer["content"])
        self.assertIn("MiniMax", answer["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("running for", answer["content"])
        self.assertEqual(sampler.call_count, 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.errors(answer, started, before, after), [])
        self.assertNotIn("private-claim-token", json.dumps(answer))
        # Matching a later count is still dishonest about this reply's sample.
        forged = copy.deepcopy(answer)
        forged["content"] = format_operational_reply(after)
        forged["status_observation"]["snapshot"] = after
        self.assertTrue(self.errors(forged, started, before, after))

    def test_queued_claimed_reused_pid_and_running_observations_remain_distinct(self):
        row = self.job()
        waiting, started, before, after = self.status()
        self.assertIn("waiting to run", waiting["content"])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors(waiting, started, before, after))
        row = self.claim(row, 101)
        wrong_process = parse_processes("101 1 00:01 S python3 dispatch_worker.py another-job token")
        for processes, state in (({}, "claimed"), (wrong_process, "claimed"), (self.processes([row]), "running")):
            with self.subTest(state=state, processes=processes), patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
                answer, started, before, after = self.status()
            observed = answer["status_observation"]["snapshot"]
            self.assertEqual(observed["work_counts"][state], 1)
            self.assertFalse(self.errors(answer, started, before, after))
        self.assertIn("waiting to run", self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][1]["content"])

    def test_snapshot_is_atomic_private_and_immutable_across_retry_and_restart(self):
        answer, started, before, after = self.status()
        original = copy.deepcopy(answer)
        path = self.chat.state / (self.identifier + ".json")
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(path.read_text())["messages"][-1], original)
        self.assertEqual(stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode), 0o600)
        public = self.chat.history(self.identifier)
        public["messages"][-1]["status_observation"]["snapshot"]["work"].append({"fake": True})
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        with patch("reply_authority.build_live_status_snapshot", side_effect=AssertionError("Must not recapture")):
            retried = self.chat.submit(self.identifier, QUESTION, original["request_id"])["messages"][-1]
            self.assertEqual(retried, original)
            self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1], original)
        self.assertFalse(self.errors(retried, started, before, after))

    def test_write_failure_cannot_expose_a_successful_unpersisted_status(self):
        from app import private_write
        def fail_completed(path, value):
            if '"status_observation"' in value:
                raise OSError("fixture disk failure")
            private_write(path, value)
        with patch("app.private_write", side_effect=fail_completed), self.assertRaises(OSError):
            self.send(QUESTION)
        saved = json.loads((self.chat.state / (self.identifier + ".json")).read_text())["messages"][-1]
        self.assertNotEqual(saved["status"], "done")
        self.assertNotEqual(self.chat.get(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["status"], "done")

    def test_correction_has_its_own_bound_observation(self):
        first = self.send(QUESTION)
        answer, started, before, after = self.status("This needs to be way better")
        self.assertNotEqual(first["status_observation"]["message_id"], answer["status_observation"]["message_id"])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors(answer, started, before, after, "This needs to be way better"))

    def test_provider_fallback_status_is_observed_and_bound_to_the_overlay(self):
        overlay = self.root / "overlay"
        overlay.mkdir()
        self.chat.overlay_roots[self.identifier] = overlay
        queue.enqueue(overlay, "fixture", "request", "Review overlay task", worker="gemini", kind="audit", title="Overlay task")
        started = time.time()
        self.chat.submit(self.identifier, QUESTION, str(uuid.uuid4()), provider="gemini")
        info = self.chat.workers.get(self.identifier)
        if info:
            info["thread"].join(3)
        answer = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(answer["status"], "done")
        self.assertTrue(self.fake.calls)
        self.assertIn("Overlay task", json.dumps(answer["status_observation"]["snapshot"]))
        self.assertIn("Gemini has work waiting to run", answer["content"])
        self.assertFalse(observation_errors(answer, QUESTION, self.identifier, started, time.time()))

    def test_provider_metadata_cannot_forge_an_observation(self):
        forged = dict(content="Hi Jeff!", provider="gemini", attempts=[], status_observation={"fake": True})
        with patch.object(self.fake, "reply", return_value=forged):
            answer = self.send("Hi")
        self.assertNotIn("status_observation", answer)

    def test_snapshot_and_reply_are_redacted_before_hashing_and_persistence(self):
        marker = self.chat.password  # Synthetic credential from the isolated Chat fixture.
        queue.enqueue(self.root, "external", "fixture", "Review " + marker, worker="gemini", kind="audit", title="Review " + marker)
        answer, started, before, after = self.status()
        self.assertNotIn(marker, json.dumps(answer))
        self.assertIn("[secret removed]", json.dumps(answer["status_observation"]["snapshot"]))
        self.assertNotIn(marker, (self.chat.state / (self.identifier + ".json")).read_text())
        self.assertFalse(self.errors(answer, started, before, after))

    def test_missing_stale_foreign_malformed_and_modified_evidence_fail_closed(self):
        answer, started, before, after = self.status()
        mutations = {
            "missing": lambda a, o: a.pop("status_observation"),
            "version": lambda a, o: o.update(schema_version=2),
            "foreign conversation": lambda a, o: o.update(conversation_id="other"),
            "foreign request": lambda a, o: o.update(request_id="other"),
            "foreign message": lambda a, o: o.update(message_id="other"),
            "question": lambda a, o: o.update(question="another question"),
            "stale": lambda a, o: o.update(observed_from=started - 30),
            "future": lambda a, o: o.update(observed_until=time.time() + 30),
            "not finite": lambda a, o: o.update(observed_from=float("nan")),
            "naive timestamp": lambda a, o: o["snapshot"].update(captured_at="2026-09-27T00:00:00"),
            "digest": lambda a, o: o.update(snapshot_sha256="0" * 64),
            "altered snapshot": lambda a, o: o["snapshot"]["work_counts"].update(running=10),
            "altered reply": lambda a, o: a.update(content=a["content"].replace("idle", "running")),
            "malformed": lambda a, o: o.update(snapshot=[]),
        }
        for name, mutate in mutations.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                modified = copy.deepcopy(answer)
                mutate(modified, modified["status_observation"])
                self.assertTrue(self.errors(modified, started, before, after))

    def test_digest_and_matching_prose_cannot_replace_observed_worker_evidence(self):
        row = self.claim(self.job(), 101)
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=self.processes([row])):
            answer, started, before, after = self.status()
        mutations = {
            "missing pid": lambda s: s["work"][0].update(pid=None),
            "queue source": lambda s: s["work"][0].update(source="queue"),
            "no assignment": lambda s: s["work"][0].update(assignment_recorded=False),
            "failed inspection": lambda s: s["inspection"].update(processes_available=False),
            "worker count": lambda s: s["work_counts"].update(running=2),
            "queue count": lambda s: s["queue_counts"].update(claimed=2),
        }
        for name, mutate in mutations.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                modified = copy.deepcopy(answer)
                snap = modified["status_observation"]["snapshot"]
                mutate(snap)
                modified["status_observation"]["snapshot_sha256"] = snapshot_digest(snap)
                modified["content"] = format_operational_reply(snap)
                self.assertTrue(self.errors(modified, started, before, after))

    def test_legacy_history_loads_without_inventing_a_snapshot(self):
        answer = self.send(QUESTION)
        self.chat.get(self.identifier)["messages"][-1].pop("status_observation")
        self.chat.save(self.chat.get(self.identifier))
        self.chat.close()
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.fake)
        restored = self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(restored["content"], answer["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("status_observation", restored)
        self.assertTrue(observation_errors(restored, QUESTION, self.identifier, 0, time.time()))


FILE webchat/test_tab_status.py
"""Current tab data must not borrow values from stale dashboards or lane caches."""
import http.client
import json
import os
import threading
import time
from unittest.mock import patch

import test_webchat
from app import Server
from tab_status import overview, recorded_usage
from worker_activity import parse_processes


class TabStatusTests(test_webchat.Fixture):
    def write(self, relative, value):
        path = self.root / relative
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(value)
        return path

    def test_overview_is_read_only_and_excludes_old_dashboard_rows(self):
        self.write("conductor/dashboard.json", json.dumps(dict(live_ops=dict(
            generated_at="2020-01-01T00:00:00+00:00", now_line="old drill",
            scores=[dict(area="UX", score="10/10")], running_jobs=[dict(job_id="fake")]))))
        self.write("conductor/lanes.json", json.dumps(dict(minimax=dict(budget=dict(committed_usd=99)))))
        self.write("conductor/state/queue.jsonl", "\n".join(json.dumps(dict(
            id=f"job-{i}", status="queued", title=f"Task {i}", updated_at=i)) for i in range(28)))
        before = {str(path): path.read_bytes() for path in self.root.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
        with patch("ops_panel.inline_live_ops") as refresh:
            data = overview(self.chat)
        refresh.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(len(data["queued"]), 28)
        self.assertEqual(data["work"], [])
        self.assertNotIn("scores", data)
        self.assertNotIn("fake", json.dumps(data))
        self.assertEqual(before, {str(path): path.read_bytes() for path in self.root.rglob("*") if path.is_file()})

    def test_live_worker_and_queue_claim_are_one_job(self):
        self.write("conductor/state/queue.jsonl", json.dumps(dict(
            id="job-one", status="running", pid=100, worker="codex", title="Review login")))
        processes = parse_processes("100 1 01:00 S codex exec --model test-model task\n101 100 00:59 S codex exec task")
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
            data = overview(self.chat)
        self.assertEqual(len(data["work"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(data["work"][0]["title"], "Review login")
        self.assertEqual(data["work"][0]["elapsed_seconds"], 60)
        self.assertEqual(data["work"][0]["state"], "running")
        self.assertEqual(data["queued"], [])

    def test_unavailable_inspection_and_damaged_queue_are_not_empty_success(self):
        self.write("conductor/state/queue.jsonl", '{"id":')
        with patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=None), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot._screen_sessions", return_value=None):
            data = overview(self.chat)
        self.assertEqual(data["inspection"], dict(processes_available=False, screens_available=False, queue_available=False))

    def test_usage_reads_current_ledger_and_does_not_invent_zero_or_cap(self):
        self.write("conductor/lanes.json", json.dumps(dict(minimax=dict(budget=dict(committed_usd=99, cap_usd=59)))))
        self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "minimax", now=time.time()), {})
        path = self.write("conductor/ledger/minimax.jsonl", "\n".join(json.dumps(row) for row in [
            dict(call_id="one", reserved_usd=4), dict(call_id="one", cost_usd=1.25),
            dict(call_id="two", reserved_usd=.5)]))
        result = recorded_usage(self.root, "minimax", now=time.time())
        self.assertEqual(result["spent_usd"], 1.25)
        self.assertEqual(result["reserved_usd"], .5)
        self.assertNotIn("cap_usd", result)
        path.write_text(path.read_text() + '\n{"call_id":')
        self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "minimax", now=time.time()), {})
        for value in (None, False, -1, float("nan")):
            path.write_text(json.dumps(dict(call_id="unknown", cost_usd=value)))
            self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "minimax", now=time.time()), {})

    def test_expired_stale_or_invalid_plan_measurements_are_hidden(self):
        now = time.time()
        path = self.write("conductor/plan-usage.json", json.dumps(dict(codex=dict(used_percent=0, reset_at=now + 3600))))
        os.utime(path, (now, now))
        self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "codex", now=now)["used_percent"], 0)
        os.utime(path, (now - 86401, now - 86401))
        self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "codex", now=now), {})
        for percent, reset in [(50, now - 1), (float("nan"), now + 100), (101, now + 100)]:
            path.write_text(json.dumps(dict(codex=dict(used_percent=percent, reset_at=reset))))
            self.assertEqual(recorded_usage(self.root, "codex", now=now), {})

    def test_model_failure_without_work_has_no_invented_model_rows(self):
        with patch.object(self.fake, "health", side_effect=OSError("unavailable")):
            data = overview(self.chat)
        self.assertEqual(data["models"], {})
        self.assertTrue(data["inspection"]["processes_available"])

    def test_model_failure_keeps_observed_activity_and_plain_task_labels(self):
        processes = parse_processes("100 1 01:00 S codex exec task")
        self.write("conductor/state/queue.jsonl", json.dumps(dict(
            id="job-one", status="running", pid=100, worker="codex", title="Review login · abc1234")))
        with patch.object(self.fake, "health", side_effect=OSError("unavailable")), \
                patch("live_status_snapshot.process_table", return_value=processes):
            data = overview(self.chat)
        self.assertEqual(list(data["models"]), ["codex"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["models"]["codex"]["state"])
        self.assertEqual(data["work"][0]["title"], "Review login")
        self.assertEqual(data["work"][0]["state"], "running")

    def test_overview_requires_login_and_redacts_private_values(self):
        self.write("conductor/state/queue.jsonl", json.dumps(dict(
            id="private-task", status="queued", title=self.chat.password)))
        server = Server(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.chat)
        thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        connection = http.client.HTTPConnection(*server.server_address, timeout=10)
        try:
            connection.request("GET", "/api/overview")
            response = connection.getresponse()
            self.assertEqual(response.status, 401)
            response.read()
            connection.request("POST", "/login", "password=" + self.chat.password,
                               {"Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"})
            response = connection.getresponse()
            cookie = response.getheader("Set-Cookie").split(";", 1)[0]
            response.read()
            connection.request("GET", "/api/overview", headers={"Cookie": cookie})
            response = connection.getresponse()
            self.assertEqual(response.status, 200)
            body = response.read().decode()
            self.assertNotIn(self.chat.password, body)
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(body)["queued"][0]["job_id"], "private-task")
            self.assertEqual(response.getheader("Cache-Control"), "no-store")
        finally:
            connection.close()
            server.shutdown()
            server.server_close()
            thread.join(2)


FILE webchat/test_texting.py
"""Texting consent and HTTP regressions. All messaging uses a mock loopback server."""
import concurrent.futures
import copy
import hmac
import http.client
import json
import os
import stat
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from app import Chat, RequestError, Server
from task_dispatch import task_queue
from texting import Andrea, BlueBubbles, Texting, TextingError, DELIVERY_UNCONFIRMED, SENT_VIA_ANDREA, local_url
from texting_fixture import MockMessaging, OfflineProviders


class TextingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.mock = MockMessaging(self.root)
        self.now = [1790535600.0]
        self.providers = OfflineProviders()
        self.texting = Texting(BlueBubbles(self.mock.config_db),
                              Andrea(self.mock.base, self.mock.token), clock=lambda: self.now[0])
        self.folder = self.root / "conductor/webchat"
        self.chat = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.providers, texting=self.texting)
        self.identifier = self.chat.new_conversation()["id"]
        token = self.chat.login(self.chat.password)
        self.session = self.chat.authenticate("bob_session=" + token)
        self.actor = self.session["digest"]

    def tearDown(self):
        self.chat.close()
        self.mock.close()
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.unexpected_writes, [])
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def say(self, text, identifier=None, actor=None, request_id=None, **kwargs):
        return self.chat.submit(identifier or self.identifier, text, request_id or str(uuid.uuid4()),
                                actor=self.actor if actor is None else actor, **kwargs)

    def draft(self, text="draft reply to Alex Morgan: I’ll bring the charts at 6."):
        value = self.say(text)
        return value["messages"][-1]["text_draft"]

    def private(self):
        return self.chat.conversations[self.identifier].get("_texting", {})

    def test_reads_summaries_and_no_jobs_or_model_calls(self):
        self.assertIn("Alex Morgan", self.say("texts")["messages"][-1]["content"])
        read = self.say("read texts from Alex Morgan")["messages"][-1]["content"]
        self.assertLess(read.index("Rehearsal is Sunday"), read.index("Can you bring"))
        summary = self.say("summarize texts from Alex Morgan")["messages"][-1]["content"]
        self.assertIn("3 messages, 2 incoming and 1 from you", summary)
        self.assertIn("Can you bring the charts at 6?", summary)
        self.assertEqual(self.providers.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(self.root), {})

    def test_ambiguous_names_and_duplicate_transports_require_exact_target(self):
        result = self.say("draft reply to Alex: Hello")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertIn("Several threads match", result["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("text_draft", result)
        duplicate = dict(self.mock.chats[0], guid="SMS;-;+15550101010")
        self.mock.chats.append(duplicate)
        self.assertIn("Several threads", self.say("read texts from Alex Morgan")["messages"][-1]["content"])
        draft = self.draft("draft reply to iMessage;-;+15550101010: Hello")
        self.assertEqual(draft["recipient"]["guid"], "iMessage;-;+15550101010")
        self.assertEqual(draft["recipient"]["addresses"], ["+15550101010"])

    def test_empty_missing_and_unverifiable_thread(self):
        self.mock.messages = []
        self.assertIn("No recent messages", self.say("read texts from Alex Morgan")["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertIn("No matching thread", self.say("read texts from Nobody")["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.mock.chats[0]["participants"] = []
        self.assertIn("could not verify", self.say("draft reply to Alex Morgan: Hi")["messages"][-1]["content"])

    def test_colloquial_text_phrasing_is_not_texting_help(self):
        def conversation():
            return {"id": str(uuid.uuid4()), "messages": [{"role": "user"}, {"role": "assistant", "content": ""}]}

        for phrase in (
            "text me about the bridge mix",
            "text Bob when you're free",
            "text the band tonight",
        ):
            conv = conversation()
            handled = self.texting.handle(
                conv, phrase, self.actor, str(uuid.uuid4()), lambda _value: None, lambda value: value
            )
            self.assertFalse(handled)
        incomplete = self.say("send a text to")["messages"][-1]
        self.assertTrue(incomplete.get("texting"))
        self.assertIn("I can read recent texts", incomplete["content"])

    def test_send_request_is_only_a_draft_and_separate_yes_is_required(self):
        self.mock.enable_web_bob_send = True
        draft = self.draft("send a text to Alex Morgan: yes, send it now")
        self.assertEqual(draft["body"], "yes, send it now")
        self.assertEqual(draft["state"], "pending")
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
        for phrase in ("yes", "ok", "send it", "send it now", "send now", "go", "do it"):
            with self.subTest(phrase=phrase):
                self.assertIn("Nothing sent", self.say(phrase)["messages"][-1]["content"])
                self.assertEqual(self.private()["drafts"][draft["id"]]["state"], "pending")
                self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
                self.assertEqual(self.mock.sends, [])
                self.assertNotIn(("POST", "/web-bob/send"), self.mock.calls)
        self.assertEqual(self.providers.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(self.root), {})
        self.assertEqual(self.say(draft["confirm_message"])["messages"][-1]["content"], SENT_VIA_ANDREA)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.mock.sends), 1)

    def test_same_request_cannot_confirm_draft(self):
        self.mock.enable_web_bob_send = True
        draft = self.draft()
        conversation = copy.deepcopy(self.chat.conversations[self.identifier])
        pending = conversation["_texting"]["drafts"][draft["id"]]
        draft_request = pending["request_id"]
        with patch.object(self.chat, "save", wraps=self.chat.save) as saved:
            with self.assertRaises(RequestError) as error:
                self.say(draft["confirm_message"], request_id=draft_request)
            self.assertEqual(error.exception.status, 409)
            # Exercise the consent guard itself, beyond Chat.submit's replay check.
            content = self.texting.confirm(conversation, draft["confirm_message"],
                                          self.actor, draft_request, self.chat.save)
            self.assertIn("does not match", content)
            saved.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(pending["state"], "pending")
        self.assertEqual("yes " + pending["token"], draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(conversation["_texting"]["audit"], [])
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.sends, [])
        self.assertNotIn(("POST", "/web-bob/send"), self.mock.calls)

        later_request = str(uuid.uuid4())
        value = self.say(draft["confirm_message"], request_id=later_request)
        self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-1]["content"], SENT_VIA_ANDREA)
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["drafts"][draft["id"]]["state"], "sent")
        self.assertEqual(len(self.private()["audit"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"][0]["request_id"], later_request)
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.sends, [{"chatGuid": draft["recipient"]["guid"], "text": draft["body"]}])
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.calls.count(("POST", "/web-bob/send")), 1)

    def test_worker_routing_cannot_bypass_text_draft_gate(self):
        for request in ("ask Codex to send a text to Alex Morgan: Hello",
                        "start a job: send a text to Alex Morgan: Hello",
                        "Claude: draft reply to Alex Morgan: Hello"):
            value = self.say(request, worker="codex")
            self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-1]["text_draft"]["state"], "pending")
        self.assertEqual(self.providers.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(self.root), {})

    def test_embedded_approval_is_not_a_separate_yes(self):
        draft = self.draft()
        for prefix in ("start a job: ", "ask Codex to ", "can you "):
            self.say(prefix + draft["confirm_message"])
            self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])

    def test_texting_does_not_clear_holds_or_capture_later_resume(self):
        self.say("hold all work")
        draft = self.draft()
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertTrue(self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.say("hold all work")
        self.say("go")
        self.assertFalse(self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.private()["audit"]), 1)

    def test_worker_prefixed_texting_still_respects_holds(self):
        self.say("hold all work")
        value = self.say("Claude: draft reply to Alex Morgan: Hello", worker="codex")
        self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-1]["text_draft"]["state"], "pending")
        self.assertTrue(self.chat.conversations[self.identifier]["intent_state"]["holds"])
        self.assertEqual(self.providers.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(task_queue.tasks(self.root), {})

    def test_separate_yes_sends_via_mock_andrea_bluebubbles_path(self):
        self.mock.enable_web_bob_send = True
        draft = self.draft()
        before_sends = len(self.mock.sends)
        value = self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertTrue(value["messages"][-1]["content"].startswith("Sent via Andrea"))
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["drafts"][draft["id"]]["state"], "sent")
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"][0]["event"], "owner_sent_via_andrea")
        self.assertEqual(len(self.mock.sends), before_sends + 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.sends[-1], {"chatGuid": draft["recipient"]["guid"], "text": draft["body"]})
        self.assertIn(("POST", "/web-bob/send"), self.mock.calls)

    def test_separate_yes_success_message_content(self):
        self.mock.enable_web_bob_send = True
        draft = self.draft()
        value = self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-1]["content"], SENT_VIA_ANDREA)
        self.assertTrue(value["messages"][-1]["content"].startswith("Sent via Andrea"))
        history = self.chat.history(self.identifier)
        self.assertEqual(history["messages"][-1]["content"], SENT_VIA_ANDREA)
        self.assertEqual(history["messages"][-3]["text_draft"]["state"], "sent")
        self.assertIsNone(history["messages"][-3]["text_draft"]["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.calls.count(("POST", "/web-bob/send")), 1)

    def test_token_comparison_timing_safe(self):
        # Verify the constant-time primitive, not noisy HTTP wall-clock timings.
        draft = self.draft()
        token = draft["confirm_message"].split()[1]
        wrong = token[:-1] + ("0" if token[-1] != "0" else "1")
        compare = hmac.compare_digest
        with patch("texting.hmac.compare_digest", wraps=compare) as compared:
            value = self.say("yes " + wrong)
            compared.assert_any_call(wrong, token)
        self.assertIn("does not match", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
        with patch("texting.hmac.compare_digest", wraps=compare) as compared:
            self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
            compared.assert_any_call(token, token)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.private()["audit"]), 1)

    def test_exact_confirmation_consumed_once_and_truthfully_blocked(self):
        draft = self.draft()
        request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        value = self.say(draft["confirm_message"], request_id=request_id)
        self.assertIn("Not sent.", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
        audit = self.private()["audit"]
        self.assertEqual(len(audit), 1)
        self.assertEqual((audit[0]["body"], audit[0]["recipient"], audit[0]["actor"]),
                         (draft["body"], draft["recipient"], self.actor))
        self.assertEqual(value["messages"][-3]["text_draft"]["state"], "confirmed_not_sent")
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"], request_id=request_id)
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.private()["audit"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.providers.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.mock.calls), 2)  # recipient lookup plus Andrea send probe
        self.assertEqual(self.mock.calls[-1], ("POST", "/web-bob/send"))

    def test_wrong_conversation_login_or_token_cannot_confirm(self):
        draft = self.draft()
        other = self.chat.new_conversation()["id"]
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"], identifier=other)

        self.say("yes wbtxt-" + "0" * 32)
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.private()["audit"]), 1)

    def test_expiry_restart_and_replay_never_authorize(self):
        draft = self.draft()
        self.now[0] += 300
        self.assertEqual(self.chat.history(self.identifier)["messages"][-1]["text_draft"]["state"], "expired")
        self.assertIn("expired", self.say(draft["confirm_message"])["messages"][-1]["content"])
        draft = self.draft()
        restored = Chat(self.folder, self.root, self.providers,
            texting=Texting(BlueBubbles(self.mock.config_db), Andrea(self.mock.base, self.mock.token), clock=lambda: self.now[0]))
        try:
            value = restored.submit(self.identifier, draft["confirm_message"], str(uuid.uuid4()), actor=self.actor)
            self.assertIn("expired", value["messages"][-1]["content"])
            self.assertEqual(restored.conversations[self.identifier]["_texting"]["audit"], [])
        finally:
            restored.close()

    def test_edit_start_and_recipient_or_body_edit_reset_confirmation(self):
        draft = self.draft()
        self.say("edit text draft " + draft["id"])
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
        value = self.say(f"replace text draft {draft['id']} to Alex Taylor: Changed body")
        revised = value["messages"][-1]["text_draft"]
        self.assertNotEqual(draft["confirm_message"], revised["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(revised["recipient"]["addresses"], ["+15550101011"])
        self.say(draft["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"], [])
        self.say(revised["confirm_message"])
        self.assertEqual(self.private()["audit"][0]["body"], "Changed body")

    def test_failed_ambiguous_edit_also_withdraws_consent(self):
